BonAppé Find — delivered source

Updated email codes, account permissions and restaurant suggestions. Source files in this ZIP are authoritative.

client/src/App.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,onMounted,onUnmounted,watch} from 'vue';
import {useRoute} from 'vue-router';
import {useAuth,useSaved} from './stores/index.js';
import {get} from './services/api.js';
import AppHeader from './components/layout/AppHeader.vue';
import AppFooter from './components/layout/AppFooter.vue';
import ToastHost from './components/layout/ToastHost.vue';
import Icon from './components/common/Icon.vue';
const route=useRoute(),auth=useAuth(),saved=useSaved();
const scrolled=ref(false),progress=ref(0),offline=ref(!navigator.onLine),consent=ref(!localStorage.getItem('bonappefind_consent')),settings=ref({});
try{const prior=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('bonappefind_consent'));if(prior?.date&&Date.now()-prior.date>365*86400000)consent.value=true;}catch{}
function top(){window.scrollTo({top:0,behavior:'smooth'});}
function onScroll(){scrolled.value=window.scrollY>600;const h=document.documentElement.scrollHeight-innerHeight;progress.value=h>0?Math.min(100,window.scrollY/h*100):0;}
function network(){offline.value=!navigator.onLine;}
function accept(){localStorage.setItem('bonappefind_consent',JSON.stringify({date:Date.now()}));consent.value=false;}
onMounted(async()=>{
  window.addEventListener('scroll',onScroll,{passive:true});window.addEventListener('online',network);window.addEventListener('offline',network);
  await auth.load();await saved.sync();
  try{const results=await Promise.all([get('/settings'),get('/health')]);settings.value=results[0].data;runtime.value=results[1].data;}catch{}
});
onUnmounted(()=>{window.removeEventListener('scroll',onScroll);window.removeEventListener('online',network);window.removeEventListener('offline',network);});
watch(()=>route.fullPath,()=>{document.title=(route.meta.title||'Discover')+' | BonAppé Find';});
</script>
<template>
  <a class="skip" href="#main">Skip to main content</a>
  <div class="progress" :style="{width:progress+'%'}" aria-hidden="true"></div>
  <AppHeader/>
  <div v-if="auth.user&&!auth.user.emailVerified" class="notice" role="status"><RouterLink to="/verify-email">Verify your email. Tap here to verify your email address.</RouterLink></div>
  <div v-if="offline" class="notice" role="status">You are offline. Check your connection and try again.</div>
  <div v-if="settings.banner&&(!settings.bannerExpiresAt||new Date(settings.bannerExpiresAt)>new Date())" class="notice">{{settings.banner}}</div>
  <main id="main" tabindex="-1">
    <div v-if="settings.maintenance&&!auth.staff" class="container section"><h1>We are cooking up some improvements.</h1><p>Please check back soon.</p></div>
    <RouterView v-else v-slot="{Component,route:r}"><Transition name="page" mode="out-in"><component :is="Component" :key="r.path" class="route-page"/></Transition></RouterView>
  </main>
  <AppFooter/>
  <ToastHost/>
  <Transition name="slide-up"><div v-if="consent" class="consent card pad row"><Icon name="info" :size="20"/><p class="small">BonAppé Find keeps your saved restaurants in your browser. We do not use advertising cookies.</p><RouterLink to="/cookies" class="small">Cookie Policy</RouterLink><button @click="accept" class="small">Got it</button></div></Transition>
  <Transition name="fade"><button v-if="scrolled" class="top" @click="top" aria-label="Back to top"><Icon name="arrow-up" :size="20"/></button></Transition>
</template>
<style scoped>
.progress{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;height:3px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--moss-gardens),var(--bow));z-index:60;transition:width .1s linear}
.consent{position:fixed;left:20px;bottom:20px;max-width:560px;z-index:50;gap:12px}.consent p{margin:0;flex:1;min-width:180px}
.top{position:fixed;right:20px;bottom:90px;z-index:40;width:48px;height:48px;padding:0;border-radius:50%;box-shadow:var(--shadow-lg);animation:float 3s ease-in-out infinite}
.slide-up-enter-active,.slide-up-leave-active,.fade-enter-active,.fade-leave-active{transition:all .4s var(--ease)}
.slide-up-enter-from,.slide-up-leave-to{opacity:0;transform:translateY(30px)}.fade-enter-from,.fade-leave-to{opacity:0;transform:scale(.7)}
@media(max-width:600px){.consent{left:12px;right:12px;bottom:12px}.top{bottom:20px}}
</style>

client/src/components/ConfirmDialog.vue

<script setup>
// Confirmation dialog built on native <dialog>: Escape, close button and backdrop click all cancel;
// keyboard focus is contained by the browser, starts on the safe button, and returns to the opener.
import {ref,onMounted,onUnmounted,getCurrentInstance} from 'vue';
const uid=getCurrentInstance().uid;
const props=defineProps({title:String,text:String,confirmLabel:{type:String,default:'Confirm'},cancelLabel:{type:String,default:'Cancel'},danger:{type:Boolean,default:true},busy:Boolean});
const emit=defineEmits(['confirm','cancel']);
const dialog=ref(null),closing=ref(false);let previous;
function cancel(){if(closing.value||props.busy)return;closing.value=true;const done=()=>emit('cancel');if(matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches)done();else setTimeout(done,160);}
function backdrop(e){if(e.target!==dialog.value)return;const r=dialog.value.getBoundingClientRect();if(e.clientX<r.left||e.clientX>r.right||e.clientY<r.top||e.clientY>r.bottom)cancel();}
onMounted(()=>{previous=document.activeElement;dialog.value.showModal();document.documentElement.classList.add('modal-open');dialog.value.querySelector('[data-first]')?.focus();});
onUnmounted(()=>{document.documentElement.classList.remove('modal-open');previous?.focus?.();});
</script>
<template>
  <dialog ref="dialog" class="confirm" :class="{closing}" :aria-labelledby="'confirm-title-'+uid" :aria-describedby="'confirm-text-'+uid" @click="backdrop" @cancel.prevent="cancel">
    <button type="button" class="dialog-x ghost" aria-label="Close dialog" @click="cancel"><svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg></button>
    <h2 :id="'confirm-title-'+uid">{{title}}</h2>
    <p :id="'confirm-text-'+uid">{{text}}</p>
    <slot/>
    <div class="row actions">
      <button type="button" data-first class="secondary" :disabled="busy" @click="cancel">{{cancelLabel}}</button>
      <button type="button" :class="[danger?'cta':'',{'is-loading':busy}]" :disabled="busy" @click="emit('confirm')"><span>{{confirmLabel}}</span></button>
    </div>
  </dialog>
</template>
<style scoped>
.confirm{position:fixed;inset:0;margin:auto;max-width:500px;width:calc(100% - 32px);border:1px solid var(--color-border);border-radius:var(--radius-lg);padding:32px 32px 28px;background:var(--color-surface);color:var(--color-text);box-shadow:var(--shadow-lg);animation:dlg-in .26s var(--ease)}
.confirm::backdrop{background:rgba(20,19,10,.62);backdrop-filter:blur(3px);animation:dlg-fade .25s var(--ease)}
.confirm.closing{animation:dlg-out .16s var(--ease) forwards}.confirm.closing::backdrop{animation:dlg-fade .16s reverse forwards}
.confirm h2{font-size:28px;margin:0 40px 12px 0}.confirm p{margin-bottom:24px}
.actions{justify-content:flex-end}
.dialog-x{position:absolute;right:14px;top:14px;width:44px;height:44px;padding:0;border-radius:50%}
@keyframes dlg-in{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(14px) scale(.97)}}@keyframes dlg-out{to{opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px) scale(.98)}}@keyframes dlg-fade{from{opacity:0}}
@media(max-width:600px){.confirm{padding:26px 22px 22px}.actions>*{flex:1}}
</style>

client/src/components/NewsletterBox.vue

<script setup>
import {ref} from 'vue';import {post,message} from '../services/api.js';import {useUi} from '../stores/index.js';
const email=ref(''),busy=ref(false),status=ref(''),ok=ref(false),err=ref('');
function check(){const v=email.value.trim();err.value=!v?'Enter your email address to subscribe.':/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(v)?'':'Enter an email address like name@example.com.';return !err.value;}
async function submit(){status.value='';ok.value=false;if(!check())return;busy.value=true;try{const r=await post('/newsletter',{email:email.value.trim()});status.value=r.data.message;ok.value=true;useUi().toast(status.value);email.value='';err.value='';}catch(e){err.value=message(e);}finally{busy.value=false;}}
</script>
<template><form novalidate @submit.prevent="submit"><label for="newsletter-email">Get one tasty story a month</label><div class="row nl-row"><input id="newsletter-email" v-model="email" type="email" autocomplete="email" placeholder="Your email address" :aria-invalid="!!err" aria-describedby="newsletter-msg" @blur="email&&check()" @input="err&&check()"><button class="cta" :class="{'is-loading':busy,'is-success':ok}" :disabled="busy"><span>{{ok?'Subscribed':'Subscribe'}}</span></button></div><p id="newsletter-msg" class="small nl-msg" :class="{err:!!err,ok}" role="status" aria-live="polite">{{err||status}}</p></form></template>
<style scoped>.nl-row{flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:stretch}.nl-row input{flex:1;min-width:0}.nl-row button{flex:none}.nl-msg{min-height:22px;margin:8px 0 0}.nl-msg.err{color:#f0b3b0;font-weight:700}.nl-msg.ok{color:#c7cb9f;font-weight:700}@media(max-width:420px){.nl-row{flex-wrap:wrap}.nl-row button{width:100%}}</style>

<template><section class="card pad"><h2>{{title}}</h2><div role="img" :aria-label="'Bar chart: '+title+'. A data table follows.'"><div v-for="r in rows.slice(0,10)" :key="r._id" style="margin:14px 0"><div class="row between small"><span>{{r._id}}</span><b>{{r.count}}</b></div><div class="bar" :style="{width:(r.count/Math.max(1,...rows.map(x=>x.count))*100)+'%'}"></div></div></div><details><summary>View data table</summary><table><thead><tr><th>Group</th><th>Restaurants</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr v-for="r in rows" :key="r._id"><td>{{r._id}}</td><td>{{r.count}}</td></tr></tbody></table></details></section></template>

client/src/components/VerifyCode.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onUnmounted} from 'vue';
import {post,message} from '../services/api.js';
import {useAuth,useUi} from '../stores/index.js';
const auth=useAuth(),code=ref(''),email=ref(auth.user?.email||''),password=ref(''),correcting=ref(false),busy=ref(false),error=ref(''),status=ref(''),now=ref(Date.now());
const timer=setInterval(()=>now.value=Date.now(),1000);onUnmounted(()=>clearInterval(timer));
const cooldown=computed(()=>Math.max(0,Math.ceil((new Date(auth.user?.verifySentAt||0).getTime()+60000-now.value)/1000)));
async function run(path,data){busy.value=true;error.value='';status.value=path==='/auth/verify-email'?'Checking your verification code…':'';try{const r=(await post(path,data)).data;if(r.user)auth.user=r.user;else await auth.load();useUi().showVerification(r,auth.user?.email);status.value=r.message;code.value='';password.value='';correcting.value=false;}catch(e){error.value=message(e);status.value='';}finally{busy.value=false;}}
</script>
<template>
<div v-if="!auth.user" class="notice"><RouterLink to="/login">Log in</RouterLink> or <RouterLink to="/register">create an account</RouterLink> to verify your email.</div>
<div v-else class="stack">
<p v-if="error" role="alert" class="error-box">{{error}}</p><p v-if="status" role="status" class="notice">{{status}}</p>
<p v-if="auth.user.emailVerified" class="notice">Your email is verified.</p>
<template v-else>
<p>Enter the verification code sent to your email.</p><p class="small">Registered email: {{auth.user.email}}</p>
<form class="stack" @submit.prevent="run('/auth/verify-email',{code})"><label for="verification-code">Verification code</label><input id="verification-code" v-model="code" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="one-time-code" pattern="[0-9]{6}" minlength="6" maxlength="6" required placeholder="Six-digit code"><button :disabled="busy">{{busy?'Checking your verification code…':'Verify email'}}</button></form>
<button type="button" class="secondary" :disabled="busy||cooldown>0" @click="run('/auth/resend-verification',{})">{{cooldown?'Resend code in '+cooldown+'s':'Resend code'}}</button>
<button type="button" class="secondary" :disabled="busy" @click="correcting=!correcting">Correct email address</button>
<form v-if="correcting" class="stack" @submit.prevent="run('/auth/correct-email',{email,password})"><label for="correct-email">Correct email address</label><input id="correct-email" v-model="email" type="email" required><label for="correct-password">Current password</label><input id="correct-password" v-model="password" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" required><button :disabled="busy||cooldown>0">Save email and request code</button></form>
<p class="small muted">Codes expire in 10 minutes and work once. After five incorrect attempts, request a new code. If email delivery is not configured in development, no email is sent; a simulated code appears in a popup and in the server terminal.</p>
</template></div>
</template>

client/src/components/common/BaseModal.vue

<script setup>
// Accessible modal: native <dialog> (focus is trapped and Escape is handled by the browser),
// visible close button, backdrop click, focus restored to the opener, and an optional
// "discard changes?" step when `dirty` is true.
import {ref,onMounted,onUnmounted,nextTick,getCurrentInstance} from 'vue';
const uid=getCurrentInstance().uid;
const props=defineProps({title:{type:String,required:true},dirty:Boolean,size:{type:String,default:'md'}});
const emit=defineEmits(['close']);
const dialog=ref(null),closing=ref(false),warn=ref(false);let opener;
function reallyClose(){if(closing.value)return;closing.value=true;const done=()=>emit('close');if(matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches)done();else setTimeout(done,180);}
function requestClose(){if(props.dirty&&!warn.value){warn.value=true;nextTick(()=>dialog.value?.querySelector('[data-keep]')?.focus());return;}reallyClose();}
function onBackdrop(e){if(e.target===dialog.value){const r=dialog.value.getBoundingClientRect();if(e.clientX<r.left||e.clientX>r.right||e.clientY<r.top||e.clientY>r.bottom)requestClose();}}
onMounted(()=>{opener=document.activeElement;dialog.value.showModal();document.documentElement.classList.add('modal-open');});
onUnmounted(()=>{document.documentElement.classList.remove('modal-open');opener?.focus?.();});
</script>
<template>
  <dialog ref="dialog" class="modal" :class="['size-'+size,{closing}]" :aria-labelledby="'modal-title-'+uid" @cancel.prevent="requestClose" @click="onBackdrop">
    <header class="modal-head"><h2 :id="'modal-title-'+uid">{{title}}</h2><button type="button" class="modal-close ghost" aria-label="Close dialog" @click="requestClose"><svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M6 6l12 12M18 6L6 18"/></svg></button></header>
    <div class="modal-body"><slot/></div>
    <footer v-if="$slots.footer" class="modal-foot"><slot name="footer"/></footer>
    <Transition name="expand-fade"><div v-if="warn" class="discard" role="alertdialog" aria-labelledby="discard-t"><p id="discard-t"><b>Discard your changes?</b> What you typed here has not been saved.</p><div class="row"><button type="button" data-keep class="secondary small" @click="warn=false">Keep editing</button><button type="button" class="cta small" @click="reallyClose">Discard</button></div></div></Transition>
  </dialog>
</template>
<style scoped>
.modal{width:calc(100% - 32px);max-width:520px;border:1px solid var(--color-border);border-radius:var(--radius-lg);padding:0;background:var(--color-surface);color:var(--color-text);box-shadow:var(--shadow-lg);animation:modal-in .28s var(--ease)}
.modal.size-lg{max-width:760px}
.modal::backdrop{background:rgba(20,19,10,.62);backdrop-filter:blur(3px);animation:fade-in .25s var(--ease)}
.modal.closing{animation:modal-out .18s var(--ease) forwards}.modal.closing::backdrop{animation:fade-out .18s forwards}
.modal-head{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:16px;padding:22px 24px 8px}
.modal-head h2{margin:0;font-size:28px}
.modal-close{width:44px;height:44px;padding:0;flex:none;border-radius:50%}
.modal-body{padding:8px 24px 24px}.modal-foot{display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:flex-end;padding:0 24px 24px}
.discard{position:sticky;bottom:0;margin:0;padding:16px 24px;background:var(--bow-soft);border-top:1.5px solid var(--bow)}.discard p{margin:0 0 12px}
@keyframes modal-in{from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(14px) scale(.97)}}@keyframes modal-out{to{opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px) scale(.98)}}
@keyframes fade-in{from{opacity:0}}@keyframes fade-out{to{opacity:0}}
@media(max-width:600px){.modal-head h2{font-size:24px}.modal-body,.modal-foot,.discard,.modal-head{padding-inline:18px}}
</style>

  dollar:'<path d="M12 3v18M16.5 7.5C15.5 6.5 14 6 12 6c-2.5 0-4 1.2-4 3s1.5 2.6 4 3 4 1.2 4 3-1.5 3-4 3c-2 0-3.6-.6-4.6-1.8"/>',
  logout:'<path d="M9 4H5v16h4M16 8l4 4-4 4M20 12H9"/>',
  copy:'<rect x="8" y="8" width="12" height="12" rx="2"/><path d="M4 16V6a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h10"/>',
  print:'<path d="M7 9V3h10v6M7 17H4v-7h16v7h-3M7 14h10v7H7z"/>',

  trash:'<path d="M4 7h16M10 11v6M14 11v6M6 7l1 13h10l1-13M9 7V4h6v3"/>',
};
const body=computed(()=>paths[props.name]||paths.info);
</script>

client/src/components/common/Pagination.vue

<script setup>
import {toRef} from 'vue';
import Icon from './Icon.vue';
import {usePagination} from '../../composables/usePagination.js';
const props=defineProps({meta:{type:Object,default:()=>({})},busy:Boolean});
const emit=defineEmits(['page']);
const {pages}=usePagination(toRef(props,'meta'));
</script>
<template>
  <nav class="pager" aria-label="Pagination">
    <button class="ghost" :disabled="(meta.page||1)<=1||busy" @click="emit('page',meta.page-1)"><Icon name="chevron-left" :size="16"/> Previous page</button>
    <template v-for="(n,i) in pages" :key="i">
      <span v-if="n==='…'" class="gap" aria-hidden="true">…</span>
      <button v-else class="ghost num" :class="{active:n===meta.page}" :aria-label="'Go to page '+n" :aria-current="n===meta.page?'page':undefined" @click="emit('page',n)">{{n}}</button>
    </template>
    <button class="ghost" :disabled="(meta.page||1)>=(meta.pages||1)||busy" @click="emit('page',meta.page+1)">Next page <Icon name="chevron-right" :size="16"/></button>
  </nav>
</template>
<style scoped>
.pager{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:center;justify-content:center;margin-top:32px}.num{min-width:44px;padding:8px}.gap{padding:0 4px;color:var(--color-muted)}
</style>

client/src/components/layout/AppHeader.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onMounted,onUnmounted,watch} from 'vue';
import {useRouter,useRoute} from 'vue-router';
import {useAuth,useSaved} from '../../stores/index.js';
import Icon from '../common/Icon.vue';
const router=useRouter(),route=useRoute(),auth=useAuth(),saved=useSaved();
const menu=ref(false),scrolled=ref(false),quick=ref(''),dark=ref(document.documentElement.dataset.theme==='dark'),moreOpen=ref(false),userMenu=ref(false),headerEl=ref(null);
const nav=[['Home','/'],['About','/about'],['Features','/features'],['Restaurants','/restaurants'],['Countries','/countries'],['Best Quality','/best-quality'],['Guides','/guides'],['FAQs','/faqs'],['Contact','/contact']];
const more=[['Insights','/insights','trending'],['Cuisines','/cuisines','utensils'],['Surprise Me','/surprise','shuffle'],['Compare','/compare','compare'],['Saved','/saved','heart'],['Team','/team','user'],['How It Works','/how-it-works','info'],['Trending','/trending','flame'],['Newly Added','/new','sparkles'],['Top Rated','/top-rated','star'],['Dishes','/dishes','bowl'],['Festivals','/festivals','calendar']];
const accountLink=computed(()=>auth.staff?'/admin':auth.user?.role==='owner'?'/owner':'/account');
const dashLabel=computed(()=>auth.staff?'Admin dashboard':auth.user?.role==='owner'?'Owner dashboard':'My dashboard');
const initials=computed(()=>(auth.user?.name||'?').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).slice(0,2).map(w=>w[0]).join('').toUpperCase());
const firstName=computed(()=>(auth.user?.name||'').split(/\s+/)[0]);
const moreActive=computed(()=>more.some(m=>isActive(m[1])));
function isActive(url){return url==='/'?route.path==='/':route.path===url||route.path.startsWith(url+'/');}
function onScroll(){scrolled.value=window.scrollY>12;}
function closeAll(){menu.value=false;moreOpen.value=false;userMenu.value=false;}
function shortcut(e){if(e.key==='/'&&!['INPUT','TEXTAREA','SELECT'].includes(document.activeElement.tagName)){e.preventDefault();setTimeout(()=>document.querySelector('#quick-search')?.focus(),0);}if(e.key==='Escape')closeAll();}
function outside(e){if(headerEl.value&&!headerEl.value.contains(e.target))closeAll();else if(!e.target.closest?.('.user-menu'))userMenu.value=false;}
function theme(){const v=document.documentElement.dataset.theme==='dark'?'light':'dark';document.documentElement.dataset.theme=v;dark.value=v==='dark';try{localStorage.setItem('bonappefind_theme',v);}catch{}}
async function logout(){closeAll();await auth.logout();router.push('/logged-out');}
function submit(){const q=quick.value.trim();router.push({path:'/restaurants',query:q?{search:q}:{}});menu.value=false;}
onMounted(()=>{window.addEventListener('scroll',onScroll,{passive:true});window.addEventListener('keydown',shortcut);document.addEventListener('click',outside);onScroll();});
onUnmounted(()=>{window.removeEventListener('scroll',onScroll);window.removeEventListener('keydown',shortcut);document.removeEventListener('click',outside);});
watch(()=>route.fullPath,closeAll);
</script>
<template>
  <header ref="headerEl" :class="{scrolled}">
    <div class="container header-top">
      <RouterLink to="/" class="wordmark" aria-label="BonAppé Find, go to the home page"><span>BonAppé Find</span></RouterLink>
      <form class="search-pill" role="search" @submit.prevent="submit">
        <label class="sr-only" for="quick-search">Search a restaurant or cuisine</label>
        <Icon name="search" :size="18"/>
        <input id="quick-search" v-model="quick" type="search" placeholder="Search restaurants or cuisines" autocomplete="off">
        <kbd class="hint" aria-hidden="true">/</kbd>
        <button aria-label="Search restaurants" class="go">Search</button>
      </form>
      <div class="header-actions">
        <RouterLink to="/saved" class="icon-link saved-link" :aria-label="'Saved restaurants, '+saved.saved.length+' saved'"><Icon name="heart" :size="20" :fill="saved.saved.length>0"/><span v-if="saved.saved.length" class="count" :key="saved.saved.length">{{saved.saved.length}}</span></RouterLink>
        <button class="icon-link theme" @click="theme" :aria-label="dark?'Switch to light mode':'Switch to dark mode'" :aria-pressed="dark"><Icon :name="dark?'sun':'moon'" :size="20"/></button>
        <div v-if="auth.user" class="user-menu">
          <button class="avatar-btn" type="button" :aria-expanded="userMenu" aria-haspopup="menu" aria-controls="user-panel" :aria-label="'Account menu for '+auth.user.name" @click="userMenu=!userMenu;moreOpen=false"><span class="avatar" aria-hidden="true">{{initials}}</span><span class="who">{{firstName}}</span><Icon name="chevron-down" :size="14" class="chev" :class="{open:userMenu}"/></button>
          <Transition name="drop"><div v-show="userMenu" id="user-panel" class="user-panel" role="menu" aria-label="Account menu">
            <div class="up-head"><span class="avatar big" aria-hidden="true">{{initials}}</span><div><b>{{auth.user.name}}</b><span>{{auth.user.role}}</span></div></div>
            <RouterLink :to="accountLink" role="menuitem"><Icon name="grid" :size="16"/>{{dashLabel}}</RouterLink>
            <RouterLink to="/saved" role="menuitem"><Icon name="heart" :size="16"/>Saved restaurants</RouterLink>
            <RouterLink to="/account/settings" role="menuitem"><Icon name="sliders" :size="16"/>Account settings</RouterLink>
            <button type="button" class="up-logout" role="menuitem" @click="logout"><Icon name="logout" :size="16"/>Log out</button>
          </div></Transition>
        </div>
        <RouterLink v-else to="/login" class="login-btn">Log in</RouterLink>
        <RouterLink v-if="!auth.user||auth.user.role==='customer'" to="/suggest-restaurant" class="btn secondary small add-link">Suggest a Restaurant</RouterLink><RouterLink v-if="auth.canManage" to="/restaurants/new" class="btn cta small add-link"><Icon name="plus" :size="16"/>Add a Restaurant</RouterLink>
        <button class="icon-link menu-toggle" @click="menu=!menu" :aria-expanded="menu" aria-controls="navigation" :aria-label="menu?'Close menu':'Open menu'"><Icon :name="menu?'x':'menu'" :size="24"/></button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <nav @focusout="e=>{if(!e.currentTarget.contains(e.relatedTarget))moreOpen=false}" id="navigation" class="container navigation" :class="{expanded:menu}" aria-label="Main navigation">
      <form class="mobile-search" role="search" @submit.prevent="submit"><Icon name="search" :size="18"/><input v-model="quick" type="search" placeholder="Search restaurants or cuisines" aria-label="Search restaurants or cuisines" autocomplete="off"><button class="go">Search</button></form>
      <div class="nav-links">
        <RouterLink v-for="[name,url] in nav" :key="url" :to="url" :class="{active:isActive(url)}">{{name}}</RouterLink>
        <div class="more" @mouseleave="moreOpen=false">
          <button class="more-btn" :class="{active:moreActive}" :aria-expanded="moreOpen" aria-haspopup="true" aria-controls="more-panel" @click="moreOpen=!moreOpen;userMenu=false" @mouseenter="moreOpen=true">More <Icon name="chevron-down" :size="14" class="chev" :class="{open:moreOpen}"/></button>
          <Transition name="drop"><div v-show="moreOpen||menu" id="more-panel" class="more-menu"><RouterLink v-for="[name,url,icon] in more" :key="url" :to="url"><Icon :name="icon" :size="16"/>{{name}}</RouterLink><RouterLink to="/terms"><Icon name="book" :size="16"/>Terms of Use</RouterLink><RouterLink to="/privacy"><Icon name="shield" :size="16"/>Privacy Policy</RouterLink></div></Transition>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="mobile-actions"><RouterLink v-if="!auth.user||auth.user.role==='customer'" to="/suggest-restaurant" class="btn secondary small">Suggest a Restaurant</RouterLink>
        <RouterLink :to="auth.user?accountLink:'/login'" class="btn secondary small">{{auth.user?dashLabel:'Log in'}}</RouterLink><button v-if="auth.user" class="btn secondary small" type="button" @click="logout">Log out</button><RouterLink v-if="auth.canManage" to="/restaurants/new" class="btn cta small"><Icon name="plus" :size="16"/>Add a Restaurant</RouterLink>
      </div>
    </nav>
  </header>
</template>
<style scoped>
header{background:linear-gradient(180deg,#34451f,var(--forestry));color:var(--candle);position:sticky;top:0;z-index:30;transition:box-shadow .3s,background .3s}
header.scrolled{box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(20,19,10,.35);background:rgba(47,61,32,.96);backdrop-filter:blur(10px)}
.header-top{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:18px 0 14px;gap:28px;transition:padding .3s}
header.scrolled .header-top{padding:8px 0 6px}
.wordmark{font-family:var(--font-brand);font-weight:400;font-size:38px;line-height:1;text-decoration:none;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;white-space:nowrap;color:var(--candle)}
.search-pill{flex:1;max-width:480px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;background:rgba(241,236,219,.12);border:1.5px solid rgba(241,236,219,.22);border-radius:var(--radius-pill);padding:0 6px 0 16px;transition:background .25s,border-color .25s,box-shadow .25s}
.search-pill:hover{background:rgba(241,236,219,.17)}
.search-pill:focus-within{background:var(--candle);color:var(--black-raspberry);border-color:var(--moss-gardens);box-shadow:0 0 0 4px rgba(117,137,86,.35)}
.search-pill input{border:0;background:transparent;color:inherit;min-height:42px;padding:6px 4px;box-shadow:none!important}
.search-pill input::placeholder{color:rgba(241,236,219,.7)}.search-pill:focus-within input::placeholder{color:var(--baby-bear)}

<script setup>
defineProps({items:{type:Array,default:()=>[]}}); // [{label, to}] last item has no link
</script>
<template><nav class="bread" aria-label="Breadcrumb"><template v-for="(i,n) in items" :key="n"><RouterLink v-if="i.to" :to="i.to">{{i.label}}</RouterLink><span v-else aria-current="page">{{i.label}}</span><span v-if="n<items.length-1"> / </span></template></nav></template>

client/src/components/layout/DashShell.vue

<script setup>
import {computed,ref,watch,nextTick,onMounted,onUnmounted} from 'vue';
import {useRouter} from 'vue-router';
import Icon from '../common/Icon.vue';
import {useAuth} from '../../stores/index.js';
const props=defineProps({
  title:String,subtitle:String,
  nav:{type:Array,default:()=>[]},      // [{label,icon,badge}]
  active:String,
  stats:{type:Array,default:()=>[]},    // [{label,value}]
  profileStats:{type:Array,default:()=>[]}, // [{label,value}]
  roleLabel:String,
  addLink:Boolean,
  breakdown:{type:Array,default:()=>[]},  // [{label,value}] for donut
  breakdownTitle:{type:String,default:''}
});
const emit=defineEmits(['select','logout']);
const auth=useAuth(),router=useRouter();
const initials=computed(()=>(auth.user?.name||'?').split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).slice(0,2).map(w=>w[0]).join('').toUpperCase());
const total=computed(()=>props.breakdown.reduce((s,b)=>s+(Number(b.value)||0),0));
const R=44,C=2*Math.PI*R;
const arcs=computed(()=>{let off=0;const cols=['#8bc34a','#2f3d20','#f0b429','#7e2625'];return props.breakdown.map((b,i)=>{const frac=total.value?(Number(b.value)||0)/total.value:0;const a={...b,color:cols[i%cols.length],dash:`${Math.max(0,frac*C-3)} ${C}`,offset:-off*C};off+=frac;return a;});});
function logout(){emit('logout');}
const navEl=ref(null);
function reveal(){nextTick(()=>{const n=navEl.value,b=n?.querySelector('.on');if(n&&b&&n.scrollWidth>n.clientWidth+4)n.scrollTo({left:b.offsetLeft-(n.clientWidth-b.clientWidth)/2,behavior:'smooth'});});}
const canLeft=ref(false),canRight=ref(false);
function measure(){const n=navEl.value;if(!n)return;const max=n.scrollWidth-n.clientWidth;canLeft.value=max>4&&n.scrollLeft>4;canRight.value=max>4&&n.scrollLeft<max-4;}
function nudge(dir){navEl.value?.scrollBy({left:dir*Math.max(160,navEl.value.clientWidth*.7),behavior:'smooth'});}
let drag=null;
function dragStart(e){if(e.pointerType!=='mouse'||e.button!==0)return;const n=navEl.value;if(!n||n.scrollWidth<=n.clientWidth+4)return;drag={x:e.clientX,left:n.scrollLeft,moved:false};}
function dragMove(e){if(!drag)return;const dx=e.clientX-drag.x;if(!drag.moved&&Math.abs(dx)>5){drag.moved=true;navEl.value.setPointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);navEl.value.classList.add('dragging');}if(drag.moved)navEl.value.scrollLeft=drag.left-dx;}
function dragEnd(e){if(!drag)return;if(drag.moved){navEl.value.releasePointerCapture?.(e.pointerId);navEl.value.classList.remove('dragging');}drag=null;}
watch(()=>props.nav,()=>nextTick(measure),{deep:true});
watch(()=>props.active,()=>{reveal();setTimeout(measure,350);});
onMounted(()=>{reveal();measure();window.addEventListener('resize',measure);});
onUnmounted(()=>window.removeEventListener('resize',measure));
</script>
<template>
<div class="dash-wrap"><div class="dash-shell">
  <aside class="dash-side" aria-label="Dashboard navigation">
    <RouterLink to="/" class="dash-brand" aria-label="BonAppé Find home"><span class="brand-box" aria-hidden="true"></span><span>BonAppé Find</span></RouterLink>
    <p class="side-label">{{roleLabel}}</p>
    <div class="nav-scroller" :class="{'fade-l':canLeft,'fade-r':canRight}">
      <button type="button" class="nav-arrow left" v-show="canLeft" tabindex="-1" aria-label="Scroll sections left" @click="nudge(-1)"><Icon name="chevron-left" :size="18"/></button>
      <nav ref="navEl" class="side-nav" role="tablist" aria-label="Dashboard sections" @scroll.passive="measure" @pointerdown="dragStart" @pointermove="dragMove" @pointerup="dragEnd" @pointercancel="dragEnd"><button v-for="n in nav" :key="n.label" role="tab" type="button" :class="{on:active===n.label}" :aria-selected="active===n.label" :tabindex="active===n.label?0:-1" @click="emit('select',n.label)"><Icon :name="n.icon||'grid'" :size="18"/><span>{{n.label}}</span><i v-if="n.badge" class="side-badge">{{n.badge}}</i></button></nav>
      <button type="button" class="nav-arrow right" v-show="canRight" tabindex="-1" aria-label="Scroll sections right" @click="nudge(1)"><Icon name="chevron-right" :size="18"/></button>
    </div>
    <button type="button" class="side-logout" @click="logout"><Icon name="logout" :size="18"/><span>Log out</span></button>
  </aside>

  <main class="dash-main">
    <header class="dash-head"><div><h1>{{title}}</h1><p>{{subtitle}}</p></div><div class="dash-actions"><RouterLink v-if="addLink&&auth.canManage" to="/restaurants/new" class="btn d-primary"><Icon name="plus" :size="16"/>Add a Restaurant</RouterLink><slot name="actions"/></div></header>
    <section v-if="stats.length" class="stat-strip" aria-label="Summary"><article v-for="s in stats" :key="s.label" class="strip-tile"><p>{{s.label}}</p><b>{{s.value}}</b></article></section>
    <div class="dash-body"><slot/></div>
  </main>

  <aside class="dash-profile" aria-label="Your profile">
    <div class="pf-top"><div class="pf-cover" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    <div class="pf-avatar" aria-hidden="true">{{initials}}</div>
    <h2>{{auth.user?.name}}</h2>
    <p class="pf-role">{{auth.user?.role}}<template v-if="auth.user?.account_id"> · {{auth.user.account_id}}</template></p>
    <p class="pf-mail">{{auth.user?.email}}</p>
    <dl v-if="profileStats.length" class="pf-stats"><div v-for="p in profileStats" :key="p.label"><dd>{{p.value}}</dd><dt>{{p.label}}</dt></div></dl></div>
    <div class="pf-bottom">
    <div v-if="breakdown.length&&total" class="pf-donut"><h3>{{breakdownTitle}}</h3>
      <div class="donut" role="img" :aria-label="breakdownTitle+': '+breakdown.map(b=>b.label+' '+b.value).join(', ')"><svg viewBox="0 0 120 120" width="132" height="132"><circle cx="60" cy="60" :r="R" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.08)" stroke-width="14"/><circle v-for="a in arcs" :key="a.label" cx="60" cy="60" :r="R" fill="none" :stroke="a.color" stroke-width="14" stroke-linecap="round" :stroke-dasharray="a.dash" :stroke-dashoffset="a.offset" transform="rotate(-90 60 60)"/></svg><div><b>{{total}}</b><span>Total</span></div></div>
      <ul><li v-for="a in arcs" :key="a.label"><i :style="{background:a.color}"></i>{{a.label}} <b>{{a.value}}</b></li></ul></div>
    <div class="pf-links"><slot name="profile-links"><RouterLink to="/account" class="btn d-primary">Your account</RouterLink></slot></div></div>
  </aside>
</div></div>
</template>

client/src/composables/useCountUp.js

import {ref,watch,onUnmounted} from 'vue';
// Animates a number from 0 to the target when it changes (respects reduced motion).
export function useCountUp(source,duration=1100){
  const shown=ref(0);let frame;
  const reduce=typeof matchMedia!=='undefined'&&matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
  function run(to){
    cancelAnimationFrame(frame);
    if(reduce||!to){shown.value=to||0;return;}
    const start=performance.now();
    const tick=now=>{const t=Math.min(1,(now-start)/duration);shown.value=Math.round(to*(1-Math.pow(1-t,3)));if(t<1)frame=requestAnimationFrame(tick);};
    frame=requestAnimationFrame(tick);
  }
  watch(source,v=>run(Number(v)||0),{immediate:true});
  onUnmounted(()=>cancelAnimationFrame(frame));
  return shown;
}

client/src/composables/useDebounce.js

import {ref,watch} from 'vue';
export function useDebounce(source,delay=250){
  const debounced=ref(source.value);let timer;
  watch(source,v=>{clearTimeout(timer);timer=setTimeout(()=>{debounced.value=v;},delay);});
  return debounced;
}

client/src/composables/useFormValidation.js

// Small form-validation helper: per-field rules, validate on blur, live re-validation after first touch,
// focus the first invalid field on submit, and a `shake` flag the template can use for feedback.
import {ref,reactive,nextTick} from 'vue';
export const required=(msg='This field is required.')=>v=>(v===undefined||v===null||String(v).trim()==='')?msg:'';
export const minLength=(n,msg)=>v=>v&&String(v).trim().length<n?(msg||`Please use at least ${n} characters.`):'';
export const maxLength=(n,msg)=>v=>v&&String(v).length>n?(msg||`Please use no more than ${n} characters.`):'';
export const email=(msg='Enter an email address like name@example.com.')=>v=>v&&!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(String(v).trim())?msg:'';
export const url=(msg='Enter a full link starting with https://')=>v=>{if(!v)return '';try{const u=new URL(v);return ['http:','https:'].includes(u.protocol)?'':msg;}catch{return msg;}};
export const matches=(getOther,msg='These values do not match.')=>v=>v!==getOther()?msg:'';
export function useFormValidation(values,schema,{idPrefix=''}={}){
  const errors=reactive({}),touched=reactive({}),shake=ref(false);
  const check=key=>{for(const rule of schema[key]||[]){const m=rule(values.value?values.value[key]:values[key]);if(m)return m;}return '';};
  function validateField(key){touched[key]=true;errors[key]=check(key);return !errors[key];}
  function onInput(key){if(touched[key])errors[key]=check(key);}
  function reset(){for(const k of Object.keys(schema)){errors[k]='';touched[k]=false;}}
  function setServerErrors(list=[]){for(const x of list)if(x.field)errors[x.field]=x.message;}
  async function validateAll(){let first='';for(const key of Object.keys(schema)){touched[key]=true;errors[key]=check(key);if(errors[key]&&!first)first=key;}
    if(first){shake.value=false;await nextTick();shake.value=true;setTimeout(()=>shake.value=false,500);document.getElementById(idPrefix+first)?.focus();return false;}return true;}
  return{errors,touched,shake,validateField,onInput,validateAll,reset,setServerErrors};
}

client/src/composables/usePagination.js

import {computed} from 'vue';
// Builds a compact page list such as [1,'…',4,5,6,'…',20].
export function usePagination(meta){
  const pages=computed(()=>{
    const total=meta.value.pages||0,page=meta.value.page||1;
    if(total<=7)return Array.from({length:total},(_,i)=>i+1);
    const set=new Set([1,total,page-1,page,page+1]);
    const list=[...set].filter(n=>n>=1&&n<=total).sort((a,b)=>a-b);
    const out=[];list.forEach((n,i)=>{if(i&&n-list[i-1]>1)out.push('…');out.push(n);});
    return out;
  });
  return{pages};
}

client/src/composables/useUnsavedGuard.js

// Warns before leaving when there are unsaved changes: in-app route changes get a dialog,
// closing/reloading the tab gets the browser's own prompt.
import {ref,onMounted,onUnmounted} from 'vue';
import {onBeforeRouteLeave} from 'vue-router';
export function useUnsavedGuard(isDirty){
  const pending=ref(null);
  const beforeUnload=e=>{if(isDirty()){e.preventDefault();e.returnValue='';}};
  onMounted(()=>window.addEventListener('beforeunload',beforeUnload));
  onUnmounted(()=>window.removeEventListener('beforeunload',beforeUnload));
  onBeforeRouteLeave(()=>{if(!isDirty())return true;return new Promise(resolve=>{pending.value=resolve;});});
  return{pending,stay(){pending.value?.(false);pending.value=null;},leave(){pending.value?.(true);pending.value=null;}};
}

client/src/directives/reveal.js

// v-reveal: fades and lifts an element into view once, with an optional stagger index.
// Usage: <div v-reveal> or <div v-reveal="2"> (index multiplies a 70ms delay) or v-reveal.left
let observer;
function getObserver(){
  if(observer||typeof IntersectionObserver==='undefined')return observer;
  observer=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
    for(const e of entries){
      if(e.isIntersecting){e.target.classList.remove('pending');e.target.classList.add('in');observer.unobserve(e.target);}
    }
  },{threshold:.12,rootMargin:'0px 0px -6% 0px'});
  return observer;
}
export default{
  mounted(el,binding){
    el.classList.add('reveal');
    if(binding.modifiers.left)el.classList.add('from-left');
    if(binding.modifiers.right)el.classList.add('from-right');
    if(binding.modifiers.zoom)el.classList.add('from-zoom');
    const index=Number(binding.value)||0;
    if(index)el.style.transitionDelay=Math.min(index,10)*70+'ms';
    const o=getObserver();
    if(o){el.classList.add('pending');o.observe(el);setTimeout(()=>{el.classList.remove('pending');el.classList.add('in');},2200);}else el.classList.add('in');
  },
  unmounted(el){observer?.unobserve(el);}
};

import {createRouter,createWebHistory} from 'vue-router';
import {useAuth,useUi} from '../stores/index.js';
const home=()=>import('../views/HomeView.vue'),list=()=>import('../views/RestaurantsView.vue'),detail=()=>import('../views/RestaurantDetailView.vue'),form=()=>import('../views/RestaurantFormView.vue'),quality=()=>import('../views/BestQualityView.vue'),countries=()=>import('../views/CountriesView.vue'),country=()=>import('../views/CountryDetailView.vue'),guides=()=>import('../views/GuidesView.vue'),insights=()=>import('../views/InsightsView.vue'),collection=()=>import('../views/CollectionView.vue'),auth=()=>import('../views/AuthView.vue'),staticPage=()=>import('../views/StaticView.vue'),dashboard=()=>import('../views/DashboardView.vue'),account=()=>import('../views/AccountView.vue');
const routes=[];function add(path,component,title,meta={}){routes.push({path,component,meta:{title,...meta}});}add('/',home,'Home');add('/restaurants',list,'Restaurants');add('/restaurants/new',form,'Add a Restaurant',{manager:true});add('/restaurants/:id/edit',form,'Edit Restaurant',{manager:true});add('/restaurants/:id/claim',()=>import('../views/ClaimView.vue'),'Claim a Restaurant',{roles:['owner']});add('/restaurants/:id',detail,'Restaurant');add('/best-quality',quality,'Best Quality Restaurants');add('/countries',countries,'Countries');add('/countries/:slug',country,'Country Food Guide');add('/guides',guides,'Guides');add('/guides/:slug',guides,'Guide Article');add('/insights',insights,'Insights');
for(const [path,title,kind]of[['/surprise','Surprise Me','surprise'],['/saved','Saved','saved'],['/compare','Compare','compare'],['/recent','Recently Viewed','recent'],['/trending','Trending','trending'],['/new','Newly Added','new'],['/top-rated','Top Rated','top-rated'],['/most-viewed','Most Viewed','most-viewed']])add(path,collection,title,{kind});add('/collections/:id',collection,'Collection');
for(const [path,title,kind]of[['/cuisines','Cuisines','cuisines'],['/dishes','Dish Glossary','dishes'],['/festivals','Food Festivals','festivals']])add(path,()=>import('../views/ExploreView.vue'),title,{kind});
for(const [path,title,kind]of[['/login','Log in','login'],['/register','Register','register'],['/register/owner','Owner Registration','owner-register'],['/forgot-password','Forgot Password','forgot'],['/reset-password','Reset Password','reset'],['/reset-password/:token','Reset Password','reset'],['/verify-email','Verify Email','verify'],['/logged-out','Logged out','logout']])add(path,auth,title,{kind});
for(const path of ['/account','/account/favorites','/account/collections','/account/settings','/account/reviews','/account/notifications','/account/search-history'])add(path,account,'Your Account',{login:true});for(const path of ['/owner','/owner/restaurants','/owner/reviews','/owner/messages','/owner/analytics','/owner/notifications'])add(path,dashboard,'Owner Dashboard',{roles:['owner']});
for(const path of ['/admin','/admin/restaurants','/admin/trash','/admin/claims','/admin/owners','/admin/users','/admin/moderation','/admin/logs','/admin/login-history','/admin/stats','/admin/settings','/admin/messages','/admin/reviews','/admin/subscribers','/admin/import-export','/admin/quality'])add(path,dashboard,'Admin Dashboard',{roles:['admin','moderator']});
for(const [path,title]of[['/about','About BonAppé Find'],['/features','Features'],['/how-it-works','How It Works'],['/team','Meet the Team'],['/terms','Terms of Use'],['/privacy','Privacy Policy'],['/cookies','Cookie Policy'],['/accessibility','Accessibility Statement'],['/sitemap','Find your way around'],['/photo-credits','Photo credits'],['/thank-you','Thank you!']])add(path,staticPage,title);
add('/faqs',()=>import('../views/FaqView.vue'),'Frequently Asked Questions');add('/contact',()=>import('../views/ContactView.vue'),'Contact');add('/:pathMatch(.*)*',staticPage,'Page Not Found');
add('/suggest-restaurant',()=>import('../views/SuggestionView.vue'),'Suggest a Restaurant');add('/account/suggestions',()=>import('../views/SuggestionView.vue'),'Your restaurant suggestions',{login:true});add('/admin/suggestions',()=>import('../views/SuggestionView.vue'),'Review restaurant suggestions',{roles:['admin']});
const router=createRouter({history:createWebHistory(),routes,scrollBehavior:(to,from,saved)=>saved||(to.hash?{el:to.hash,top:180,behavior:matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches?'auto':'smooth'}:{top:0})});router.beforeEach(async to=>{const a=useAuth();if(!a.ready)await a.load();if((to.meta.login||to.meta.roles||to.meta.manager)&&!a.user)return{path:'/login',query:{redirect:to.fullPath}};if(to.meta.roles&&!to.meta.roles.includes(a.user?.role)){useUi().toast('You do not have permission to do that.','error');return'/account';}if(to.meta.manager&&!a.canManage){useUi().toast('Owners need verified email and administrator approval to manage listings.','error');return a.user?.role==='owner'?'/owner':'/account';}document.title=to.meta.title+' | BonAppé Find';});export default router;

client/src/services/api.js

import axios from 'axios';
export const api=axios.create({baseURL:import.meta.env.VITE_API_URL||'/api',timeout:20000});
api.interceptors.request.use(c=>{const token=localStorage.getItem('bonappefind_token');if(token)c.headers.Authorization='Bearer '+token;return c;});
api.interceptors.response.use(r=>r,e=>{if(e.response?.status===401){localStorage.removeItem('bonappefind_token');window.dispatchEvent(new Event('session-ended'));}return Promise.reject(e);});
export const get=async(url,params)=> (await api.get(url,{params})).data;
export const post=async(url,data)=> (await api.post(url,data)).data;
export const put=async(url,data)=> (await api.put(url,data)).data;
export const patch=async(url,data)=> (await api.patch(url,data)).data;
export const del=async(url,data)=> (await api.delete(url,{data})).data;
export const message=e=>e.response?.data?.message||'We could not connect. Check your connection and try again.';
export const imageUrl=url=>url?.startsWith('/uploads/')?api.defaults.baseURL.replace(/\/api\/?$/,'')+url:url;

client/src/stores/index.js

import {defineStore} from 'pinia';
import {ref,computed} from 'vue';
import {get,post,del,message} from '../services/api.js';
function stored(key,initial){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key))||initial;}catch{return initial;}}
export const useUi=defineStore('ui',()=>{const toasts=ref([]),verification=ref(null);function showVerification(delivery,email,purpose='verify'){if(delivery?.developmentCode)verification.value={code:delivery.developmentCode,email,purpose};}function toast(text,type='success',action){const id=Date.now()+Math.random();toasts.value.push({id,text,type,action});setTimeout(()=>{toasts.value=toasts.value.filter(t=>t.id!==id)},action?15000:6000);}return{toasts,toast,verification,showVerification};});
export const useAuth=defineStore('auth',()=>{const user=ref(null),ready=ref(false);async function load(){try{if(localStorage.getItem('bonappefind_token'))user.value=(await get('/auth/me')).data;else user.value=null;}catch{user.value=null;}ready.value=true;}function accept(data){localStorage.setItem('bonappefind_token',data.token);user.value=data.user;}async function logout(){try{if(user.value)await post('/auth/logout');}finally{localStorage.removeItem('bonappefind_token');user.value=null;useSaved().replaceRemote([]);useUi().verification=null;useUi().toast('You have been logged out.');}}const staff=computed(()=>['admin','moderator'].includes(user.value?.role));window.addEventListener('session-ended',()=>user.value=null);const canManage=computed(()=>user.value?.role==='admin'||(user.value?.role==='owner'&&user.value.emailVerified&&user.value.ownerProfile?.verificationStatus==='approved'));return{user,ready,load,accept,logout,staff,canManage};});
export const useSaved=defineStore('saved',()=>{const saved=ref(stored('bonappefind_saved',[])),compare=ref(stored('bonappefind_compare',[])),recent=ref(stored('bonappefind_recent',[]));function persist(){for(const[k,v]of[['saved',saved.value],['compare',compare.value],['recent',recent.value]])localStorage.setItem('bonappefind_'+k,JSON.stringify(v));}function replaceRemote(ids){saved.value=[...new Set(ids)];persist();}async function toggle(id){const auth=useAuth();if(!auth.user){useUi().toast('Log in or create a customer account to save restaurants.','error');const redirect=encodeURIComponent(location.pathname+location.search);location.href='/login?redirect='+redirect;return;}const existing=saved.value.includes(id);try{if(existing)await del('/me/favorites',{restaurant:id});else await post('/me/favorites',{restaurant:id});saved.value=existing?saved.value.filter(x=>x!==id):[...saved.value,id];persist();window.dispatchEvent(new Event('saved-changed'));useUi().toast(existing?'Removed from your list.':'Saved to your list.');}catch(e){useUi().toast(message(e),'error');}}function comparison(id){if(compare.value.includes(id))compare.value=compare.value.filter(x=>x!==id);else if(compare.value.length<3)compare.value.push(id);else{return useUi().toast('Choose up to three restaurants.','error');}persist();useUi().toast('Comparison updated.');}function visited(id){recent.value=[id,...recent.value.filter(x=>x!==id)].slice(0,6);persist();}async function sync(){if(!useAuth().user){replaceRemote([]);return;}try{const remote=(await get('/me/favorites')).data.map(r=>r._id);replaceRemote(remote);}catch(e){useUi().toast(message(e),'error');}}return{saved,compare,recent,toggle,comparison,visited,sync,replaceRemote};});

client/src/utils/formatters.js

import restaurantPhotos from './restaurant-photos.js';
const photosById=new Map(restaurantPhotos.map(p=>[p.id,p.image]));
const photosByName=new Map(restaurantPhotos.map(p=>[p.name,p.image]));
const cuisinePhotos=[["south indian", "BF-012"], ["northern thai", "BF-013"], ["thai street", "BF-014"], ["japanese curry", "BF-EXP-016"], ["japanese grill", "BF-EXP-014"], ["italian grill", "BF-EXP-024"], ["mexican grill", "BF-EXP-030"], ["french grill", "BF-EXP-040"], ["indian grill", "BF-EXP-044"], ["thai grill", "BF-EXP-052"], ["filipino fusion", "BF-EXP-002"], ["french fusion", "BF-EXP-039"], ["filipino", "BF-001"], ["korean", "BF-EXP-003"], ["chinese", "BF-EXP-004"], ["mediterranean", "BF-EXP-005"], ["seafood", "BF-EXP-064"], ["ramen", "BF-004"], ["noodle", "BF-EXP-009"], ["sushi", "BF-003"], ["japanese", "BF-EXP-011"], ["pizza", "BF-006"], ["italian", "BF-005"], ["pasta", "BF-EXP-021"], ["taco", "BF-008"], ["mexican", "BF-007"], ["bagel", "BF-015"], ["bakery", "BF-EXP-034"], ["cafe", "BF-EXP-007"], ["caf\u00e9", "BF-EXP-007"], ["coffee", "BF-EXP-020"], ["french", "BF-009"], ["indian", "BF-EXP-041"], ["curry", "BF-EXP-054"], ["thai", "BF-EXP-051"], ["burger", "BF-EXP-057"], ["barbecue", "BF-EXP-062"], ["american", "BF-EXP-060"], ["grill", "BF-EXP-052"], ["fusion", "BF-EXP-019"]];
export function dishImage(cuisine=''){
  const c=String(cuisine).toLowerCase();
  const hit=cuisinePhotos.find(([keyword])=>c.includes(keyword));
  return photosById.get(hit?.[1])||'/images/photos/table.jpg';
}
// Picks the best bundled or uploaded picture for a restaurant record.
export function restaurantImage(r,resolve=x=>x){
  const u=r?.profile?.imageUrl;
  const matched=photosById.get(r?.restaurant_id)||photosByName.get(r?.name);
  if(matched&&(!u||u.startsWith('/images/photos/')))return matched;
  if(u&&(u.startsWith('/uploads/')||u.startsWith('/images/photos/')||u.startsWith('/images/restaurants/')||/^https?:/.test(u)))return resolve(u);
  const id=String(r?.restaurant_id||'').toLowerCase();
  if(/^bf-0\d\d$/.test(id))return dishImage(r.cuisine);
  if(r?.cuisine)return dishImage(r.cuisine);
  return '/images/photos/dining.jpg';
}
export function flag(iso=''){return [...iso.toUpperCase()].map(x=>String.fromCodePoint(x.charCodeAt(0)+127397)).join('');}
export function initials(name=''){return name.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).slice(0,2).map(w=>w[0]).join('').toUpperCase();}

export function countryImage(slug){const map={"japan": "BF-003", "italy": "BF-005", "mexico": "BF-008", "india": "BF-012", "thailand": "BF-013", "france": "BF-010", "philippines": "BF-001", "united-states": "BF-EXP-057"};return photosById.get(map[slug])||'/images/photos/table.jpg';}

client/src/utils/interactions.js

// Site-wide micro-interactions: auto scroll-reveal with stagger, button ripple,
// card spotlight, and scroll-spy for "On this page" lists.
// Everything is progressive: with reduced motion or no IntersectionObserver it does nothing.
const reduce=()=>matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
const fine=()=>matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)').matches;

const REVEAL='.route-page .page-head,.route-page .card,.route-page .info-section,.route-page .acc,.route-page .rcard,.route-page .pod,.route-page .editorial-photo,.route-page .process-cover,.route-page .guide-cover';

function setupReveal(){
  if(reduce()||typeof IntersectionObserver==='undefined')return;
  const io=new IntersectionObserver(entries=>{
    for(const e of entries){
      if(!e.isIntersecting)continue;
      const el=e.target;io.unobserve(el);
      el.classList.add('fx-in');
      setTimeout(()=>{el.classList.remove('fx-reveal','fx-in');el.style.removeProperty('--fx-delay');},1100);
    }
  },{threshold:.08,rootMargin:'0px 0px -5% 0px'});
  const seen=new WeakSet();
  function scan(root=document){
    const list=root.querySelectorAll?root.querySelectorAll(REVEAL):[];
    const batch=[];
    list.forEach(el=>{
      if(seen.has(el)||el.classList.contains('reveal')||el.closest('dialog,.consent,.toast-host'))return;
      seen.add(el);
      const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
      if(r.top<innerHeight*0.9&&r.bottom>0)return;        // already on screen: leave alone, no flash
      batch.push(el);
    });
    batch.forEach((el,i)=>{
      el.style.setProperty('--fx-delay',Math.min(i%6,5)*70+'ms');
      el.classList.add('fx-reveal');
      io.observe(el);
      setTimeout(()=>{el.classList.add('fx-in');},2600);   // safety net
    });
  }
  let t;
  const mo=new MutationObserver(()=>{clearTimeout(t);t=setTimeout(()=>scan(),90);});
  const main=document.querySelector('main');
  if(main)mo.observe(main,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  scan();
}

function setupRipple(){
  if(reduce())return;
  document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
    const b=e.target.closest?.('.btn,button:not(.icon-link):not(.acc-head):not(.avatar-btn):not(.top)');
    if(!b||b.disabled)return;
    const r=b.getBoundingClientRect(),d=Math.max(r.width,r.height)*1.6;
    const s=document.createElement('span');
    s.className='fx-ripple';
    s.style.cssText=`width:${d}px;height:${d}px;left:${e.clientX-r.left-d/2}px;top:${e.clientY-r.top-d/2}px`;
    if(getComputedStyle(b).position==='static')b.style.position='relative';
    b.style.overflow='hidden';
    b.appendChild(s);
    setTimeout(()=>s.remove(),650);
  },{passive:true});
}

function setupSpotlight(){
  if(reduce()||!fine())return;
  document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
    const c=e.target.closest?.('a.card,.rcard,.hoverable,.guide-card,.country-card');
    if(!c)return;
    const r=c.getBoundingClientRect();
    c.style.setProperty('--mx',(e.clientX-r.left)+'px');
    c.style.setProperty('--my',(e.clientY-r.top)+'px');
    c.classList.add('fx-spot');
  },{passive:true});
}

function setupScrollSpy(){
  if(typeof IntersectionObserver==='undefined')return;
  let io,links=[];
  function bind(){
    io?.disconnect();
    const nav=document.querySelector('.info-sidebar nav');
    const secs=[...document.querySelectorAll('.info-section,#additional-details')];
    if(!nav||!secs.length)return;
    links=[...nav.querySelectorAll('a')];
    const visible=new Map();
    io=new IntersectionObserver(es=>{
      es.forEach(e=>visible.set(e.target.id,e.isIntersecting?e.boundingClientRect.top:null));
      let best=null;
      visible.forEach((top,id)=>{if(top!==null&&(best===null||top<best.top))best={id,top};});
      if(!best)return;
      links.forEach(a=>{const on=a.getAttribute('href')==='#'+best.id;a.classList.toggle('current',on);if(on)a.setAttribute('aria-current','true');else a.removeAttribute('aria-current');});
    },{rootMargin:'-20% 0px -60% 0px',threshold:[0,1]});
    secs.forEach(s=>io.observe(s));
  }
  let t;
  new MutationObserver(()=>{clearTimeout(t);t=setTimeout(bind,150);}).observe(document.querySelector('main')||document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true});
  bind();
}

export function initInteractions(){
  setupRipple();setupSpotlight();
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{setupReveal();setupScrollSpy();});
}

client/src/views/AccountView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onMounted,onUnmounted,watch} from 'vue';import {get,post,put,patch,del,message} from '../services/api.js';import {useAuth,useSaved,useUi} from '../stores/index.js';import {useRouter,useRoute} from 'vue-router';import RestaurantCard from '../components/restaurants/RestaurantCard.vue';import ConfirmDialog from '../components/ConfirmDialog.vue';import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';import DashShell from '../components/layout/DashShell.vue';
const route=useRoute(),auth=useAuth(),saved=useSaved(),ui=useUi(),router=useRouter(),tab=ref('Profile'),favorites=ref([]),reviews=ref([]),notifications=ref([]),collections=ref([]),error=ref(''),name=ref(''),collectionName=ref(''),searchHistory=ref([]),loading=ref(true),busy=ref(''),touched=ref(false),confirming=ref(''),filter=ref('');
const tabs=['Profile','Favorites','Reviews','Collections','Notifications','Search history','Settings'];
const nameErr=computed(()=>touched.value&&name.value.trim().length<2?'Enter a display name of at least 2 characters.':'');
const collErr=computed(()=>touched.value&&!collectionName.value.trim()?'Give your collection a name.':'');
let loadVersion=0,disposed=false,refreshTimer;
const lastUpdated=ref(null),refreshing=ref(false);
async function load(){
 const version=++loadVersion;refreshing.value=true;error.value='';
 try{
  await auth.load();if(!auth.user){router.push('/login');return false;}
  const results=await Promise.all(['/me/favorites','/me/reviews','/notifications','/me/collections','/me/search-history'].map(url=>get(url,{_refresh:Date.now()})));
  if(disposed||version!==loadVersion)return false;
  [favorites.value,reviews.value,notifications.value,collections.value,searchHistory.value]=results.map(r=>r.data);
  saved.replaceRemote(favorites.value.map(r=>r._id));
  if(!name.value||name.value===previousName)name.value=auth.user.name;
  previousName=auth.user.name;lastUpdated.value=new Date();return true;
 }catch(e){if(!disposed&&version===loadVersion)error.value=message(e);return false;}
 finally{if(!disposed&&version===loadVersion){loading.value=false;refreshing.value=false;}}
}
let previousName='';
function autoRefresh(){if(document.hidden||refreshing.value||busy.value||confirming.value||name.value!==previousName||collectionName.value||document.activeElement?.matches('input,textarea,select'))return;load();}
function fromPath(){tab.value={'/account/favorites':'Favorites','/account/reviews':'Reviews','/account/collections':'Collections','/account/notifications':'Notifications','/account/search-history':'Search history','/account/settings':'Settings'}[route.path]||'Profile';touched.value=false;filter.value='';}
function choose(x){const paths={Profile:'/account',Favorites:'/account/favorites',Reviews:'/account/reviews',Collections:'/account/collections',Notifications:'/account/notifications','Search history':'/account/search-history',Settings:'/account/settings'};router.push(paths[x]);}
onMounted(()=>{fromPath();load();refreshTimer=setInterval(autoRefresh,15000);window.addEventListener('focus',autoRefresh);window.addEventListener('saved-changed',load);document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',autoRefresh);});onUnmounted(()=>{disposed=true;++loadVersion;clearInterval(refreshTimer);window.removeEventListener('focus',autoRefresh);window.removeEventListener('saved-changed',load);document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange',autoRefresh);});watch(()=>route.path,()=>{fromPath();load();});
async function action(fn,key='',msg='Saved.'){if(busy.value)return false;busy.value=key||'action';try{await fn();ui.toast(msg);await load();return true;}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');return false;}finally{busy.value='';}}
async function save(){touched.value=true;if(nameErr.value){document.getElementById('account-name')?.focus();return;}await action(async()=>{auth.user=(await patch('/me',{name:name.value.trim()})).data;},'name','Display name saved.');touched.value=false;}
async function download(){busy.value='download';try{const data=(await get('/me/data')).data;const url=URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([JSON.stringify(data,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}));const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download='bonappefind-my-data.json';a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);ui.toast('Your data download has started.');}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}finally{busy.value='';}}
async function remove(){busy.value='delete';try{await del('/me');localStorage.removeItem('bonappefind_token');auth.user=null;confirming.value='';router.push('/');ui.toast('Account deleted.');}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}finally{busy.value='';}}
async function addCollection(){touched.value=true;if(collErr.value){document.getElementById('collection-name')?.focus();return;}if(!saved.saved.length){ui.toast('Save a few restaurants first, then create a collection from them.','error');return;}if(await action(()=>post('/me/collections',{name:collectionName.value.trim(),restaurants:saved.saved,isPublic:false}),'collection','Collection created.')){collectionName.value='';touched.value=false;}}
const shownHistory=computed(()=>searchHistory.value.filter(h=>!filter.value||h.query.toLowerCase().includes(filter.value.toLowerCase())));
const tabIcons={Profile:'user',Favorites:'heart',Reviews:'star',Collections:'book',Notifications:'calendar','Search history':'search',Settings:'sliders'};
const navItems=computed(()=>tabs.map(label=>({label,icon:tabIcons[label],badge:label==='Notifications'?(notifications.value.filter(n=>!n.isRead).length||''):''})));
const stripStats=computed(()=>[{label:'Saved restaurants',value:favorites.value.length},{label:'Reviews written',value:reviews.value.length},{label:'Collections',value:collections.value.length},{label:'Unread notifications',value:notifications.value.filter(n=>!n.isRead).length}]);
const profileStats=computed(()=>[{label:'Saved',value:favorites.value.length},{label:'Reviews',value:reviews.value.length},{label:'Lists',value:collections.value.length}]);
async function logout(){await auth.logout();router.push('/logged-out');}
</script>
<template>
<DashShell title="Customer dashboard" subtitle="Your saved restaurants, reviews and lists in one place." :nav="navItems" :active="tab" :stats="stripStats" :profile-stats="profileStats" role-label="Customer" @select="choose" @logout="logout">
  <template #actions><RouterLink to="/restaurants" class="btn d-primary"><Icon name="search" :size="16"/>Browse restaurants</RouterLink></template>

Scheduled newsletters are not sent by this starter.</p><div class="row"><button class="secondary" :class="{'is-loading':busy==='download'}" :disabled="!!busy" @click="download"><span>Download my data</span></button><button class="cta" :disabled="!!busy" @click="confirming='account'">Delete my account</button></div></section>

client/src/views/AuthView.vue

<script setup>
import ResetCode from '../components/ResetCode.vue';
import VerifyCode from '../components/VerifyCode.vue';
import {ref,computed,watch,onMounted,nextTick} from 'vue';import {useRoute,useRouter} from 'vue-router';import {post,get,message} from '../services/api.js';import {useAuth,useSaved,useUi} from '../stores/index.js';
const route=useRoute(),router=useRouter(),auth=useAuth(),saved=useSaved(),ui=useUi();
const form=ref({name:'',email:'',password:'',confirmPassword:'',businessName:'',phone:'',position:''}),busy=ref(false),error=ref(''),status=ref(''),showPassword=ref(false),showConfirm=ref(false),loginRole=ref('customer');
const mode=computed(()=>route.meta.kind),isRegister=computed(()=>['register','owner-register'].includes(mode.value)),passwordStrength=computed(()=>[form.value.password.length>=8,/[a-z]/.test(form.value.password),/[A-Z]/.test(form.value.password),/\d/.test(form.value.password),/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/.test(form.value.password)].filter(Boolean).length);
const title=computed(()=>({login:'Welcome back',register:'Create an account','owner-register':'Register your restaurant',forgot:'Forgot password?',reset:'Choose a new password',verify:'Verify your email',logout:"You're logged out"})[mode.value]);
const sub=computed(()=>({login:'Log in to see your saved restaurants, reviews and lists, wherever you are.',register:'Save restaurants, write reviews and build your own shortlist in one place.','owner-register':'Your owner account starts as pending. Verify your email and wait for administrator approval before managing listings.',forgot:"Enter your registered email, and we'll send you a six-digit code to reset your password.",reset:'Pick a strong password you have not used before.',verify:'Enter the verification code sent to your email.',logout:'Your saved list stays in this browser. Log in again whenever you are ready.'})[mode.value]);
const art=computed(()=>({login:['Welcome back','Pick up your saved list right where you left it.'],register:['Join BonAppé Find','Save the places worth eating at, and find your next favourite.'],'owner-register':['For restaurant owners','Claim your listing and keep every detail accurate.'],forgot:['No stress','Reset your password and get back to the table.'],reset:['Nearly there','Choose a new password and sign back in.'],verify:['Almost there','One quick check and your account is ready.'],logout:['See you soon','Your table will be here when you come back.']})[mode.value]||['','']);
const roles=[{role:'customer',label:'Customer',hint:'Browse freely and log in to save a personal list.'},{role:'owner',label:'Owner',hint:'Manage only the listings connected to your owner account.'},{role:'admin',label:'Admin',hint:'Authorized administrators only. Public admin sign-up is not available.'}];
const roleHint=computed(()=>roles.find(r=>r.role===loginRole.value)?.hint);
const switchLine=computed(()=>({login:['Don\'t have an account?','Sign up','/register'],register:['Already have an account?','Log in','/login'],'owner-register':['Already have an account?','Log in','/login'],forgot:['Remembered it?','Log in','/login'],reset:['Changed your mind?','Log in','/login'],verify:['Done here?','Log in','/login'],logout:['New here?','Sign up','/register']})[mode.value]);
const socialOn=computed(()=>['login','register'].includes(mode.value));
function social(name){ui.toast(name+' sign-in is not available in this demo. Use email and password instead.','error');}
const demos=[{role:'customer',label:'Customer',email:'customer@example.test',password:'ReviewOnly123',destination:'/account'},{role:'owner',label:'Restaurant Owner',email:'owner@example.test',password:'ReviewOnly123',destination:'/owner'},{role:'admin',label:'Administrator',email:'reviewer@example.test',password:'ReviewOnly123',destination:'/admin'}];
function useDemo(d){loginRole.value=d.role;form.value.email=d.email;form.value.password=d.password;error.value='';status.value='Demo credentials filled in. Select Log in when ready.';}
async function loginDemo(d){useDemo(d);await submit(d.destination);}
const touched=ref({}),shake=ref(false),mailRx=/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/;
const errs=computed(()=>{const f=form.value,e={},m=mode.value;
 if(isRegister.value&&!f.name.trim())e.name='Please tell us your name.';
 if(m!=='reset'&&m!=='verify'){if(!f.email.trim())e.email='Enter your email address.';else if(!mailRx.test(f.email.trim()))e.email='Enter an email address like name@example.com.';}
 if(m==='owner-register'){if(!f.businessName.trim())e.businessName='Enter your business name.';if(!f.phone.trim())e.phone='Enter a business phone number.';if(!f.position.trim())e.position='Enter your position or role.';}
 if(m!=='forgot'&&m!=='verify'){if(!f.password)e.password=m==='login'?'Enter your password.':'Choose a password.';else if(m!=='login'&&(f.password.length<8||!/[a-zA-Z]/.test(f.password)||!/\d/.test(f.password)))e.password='Use at least 8 characters with a letter and a number.';}
 if(isRegister.value||m==='reset'){if(!f.confirmPassword)e.confirmPassword='Re-enter your password to confirm it.';else if(f.confirmPassword!==f.password)e.confirmPassword='Passwords do not match yet.';}
 return e;});
const showErr=k=>touched.value[k]&&errs.value[k]?errs.value[k]:'';
const touch=k=>{touched.value={...touched.value,[k]:true};};
const ids={name:'auth-name',email:'auth-email',businessName:'owner-businessName',phone:'owner-phone',position:'owner-position',password:'auth-password',confirmPassword:'confirm-password'};
async function submit(forcedDestination=''){error.value='';status.value='';if(!forcedDestination){const keys=Object.keys(errs.value);touched.value=Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(ids).map(k=>[k,true]));if(keys.length){shake.value=false;await nextTick();shake.value=true;setTimeout(()=>shake.value=false,500);document.getElementById(ids[keys[0]])?.focus();return;}}busy.value=true;try{const path=mode.value==='login'?'/auth/login':mode.value==='register'?'/auth/register':mode.value==='owner-register'?'/owners/register':mode.value==='forgot'?'/auth/forgot-password':'/auth/reset-password';const r=await post(path,{...form.value,token:route.params.token});if(r.data.token){auth.accept(r.data);ui.showVerification(r.data.delivery,r.data.user.email);if(mode.value==='login'&&loginRole.value&&auth.user.role!==loginRole.value){await auth.logout();throw new Error(`This sample/login option is for ${loginRole.value} accounts, but the credentials belong to a ${r.data.user.role} account.`);}await saved.sync();ui.toast(isRegister.value?r.data.message||'Verify your email. Tap here to verify your email address.':'Welcome back!');router.push(forcedDestination||(auth.staff?'/admin':auth.user.role==='owner'?'/owner':route.query.redirect||'/account'));}else status.value=r.data.message;}catch(e){error.value=message(e)||e.message;}finally{busy.value=false;}}
watch(()=>route.fullPath,()=>{error.value='';status.value='';touched.value={};});
</script>

<p v-if="mode==='login'" class="role-hint">{{roleHint}}</p>

<form :aria-busy="busy" v-if="mode!=='verify'&&mode!=='logout'&&mode!=='forgot'&&mode!=='reset'" novalidate @submit.prevent="submit()" class="auth-form" :class="{shake}">
<div v-if="isRegister" class="field"><label for="auth-name">Your name</label><input id="auth-name" v-model="form.name" placeholder="Maria Santos" autocomplete="name" maxlength="120" :aria-invalid="!!showErr('name')" aria-describedby="auth-name-msg" @blur="touch('name')"><p id="auth-name-msg" v-if="showErr('name')" class="field-msg">{{showErr('name')}}</p></div>
<div v-if="mode!=='reset'" class="field"><label for="auth-email">{{mode==='login'?'Your email':'Email address'}}</label><input id="auth-email" v-model="form.email" type="email" placeholder="name@example.com" autocomplete="email" :aria-invalid="!!showErr('email')" aria-describedby="auth-email-msg" @blur="touch('email')"><p id="auth-email-msg" v-if="showErr('email')" class="field-msg">{{showErr('email')}}</p></div>
<template v-if="mode==='owner-register'"><div v-for="[key,label] in [['businessName','Business name'],['phone','Business phone'],['position','Position / role in business']]" :key="key" class="field"><label :for="'owner-'+key">{{label}}</label><input :id="'owner-'+key" v-model="form[key]" :aria-invalid="!!showErr(key)" :aria-describedby="'owner-'+key+'-msg'" @blur="touch(key)"><p :id="'owner-'+key+'-msg'" v-if="showErr(key)" class="field-msg">{{showErr(key)}}</p></div></template>
<div v-if="mode!=='forgot'" class="field"><div class="label-row"><label for="auth-password">{{mode==='reset'?'New password':'Password'}}</label><RouterLink v-if="mode==='login'" to="/forgot-password" class="mini-link">Forgot password?</RouterLink></div>
<div class="password-field"><input id="auth-password" v-model="form.password" :type="showPassword?'text':'password'" placeholder="••••••••••" maxlength="128" :autocomplete="mode==='login'?'current-password':'new-password'" :aria-invalid="!!showErr('password')" aria-describedby="auth-password-msg" @blur="touch('password')"><button type="button" class="eye" @click="showPassword=!showPassword" :aria-label="showPassword?'Hide password':'Show password'" :aria-pressed="showPassword"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2 12s3.6-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-3.6 7-10 7S2 12 2 12z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/><path v-if="!showPassword" d="M4 4l16 16"/></svg></button></div>
<p id="auth-password-msg" v-if="showErr('password')" class="field-msg">{{showErr('password')}}</p>
<template v-if="isRegister||mode==='reset'"><p v-if="!showErr('password')" class="field-msg hint">Use at least 8 characters with a letter and a number.</p><meter min="0" max="5" :value="passwordStrength" aria-label="Password strength"></meter><p class="small strength">Strength: {{['Very weak','Weak','Fair','Good','Strong','Very strong'][passwordStrength]}}</p></template></div>
<div v-if="isRegister||mode==='reset'" class="field"><label for="confirm-password">Confirm password</label><div class="password-field"><input id="confirm-password" v-model="form.confirmPassword" :type="showConfirm?'text':'password'" placeholder="••••••••••" autocomplete="new-password" :aria-invalid="!!showErr('confirmPassword')" aria-describedby="confirm-password-msg" @blur="touch('confirmPassword')" @input="touch('confirmPassword')"><button type="button" class="eye" @click="showConfirm=!showConfirm" :aria-label="showConfirm?'Hide password':'Show password'" :aria-pressed="showConfirm"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M2 12s3.6-7 10-7 10 7 10 7-3.6 7-10 7S2 12 2 12z"/><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3"/><path v-if="!showConfirm" d="M4 4l16 16"/></svg></button></div><p id="confirm-password-msg" v-if="showErr('confirmPassword')" class="field-msg">{{showErr('confirmPassword')}}</p><p v-else-if="form.confirmPassword&&form.confirmPassword===form.password" class="field-msg ok">Passwords match.</p></div>
<button class="primary-btn" :class="{'is-loading':busy}" :disabled="busy"><span>{{mode==='login'?'Log in':isRegister?'Get started':mode==='forgot'?'Send reset link':'Save password'}}</span></button></form>

client/src/views/BestQualityView.vue

<script setup>import {ref,onMounted} from 'vue';import {get,message} from '../services/api.js';import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';import {restaurantImage} from '../utils/formatters.js';const rows=ref([]),countries=ref([]),min=ref(90),country=ref(''),limit=ref(25),busy=ref(false),error=ref(''),minErr=ref('');async function load(){minErr.value=Number(min.value)>=0&&min.value!==''?'':'Enter a minimum score of 0 or more.';if(minErr.value)return;busy.value=true;error.value='';try{rows.value=(await get('/restaurants/stats/top-quality',{min:min.value,country:country.value,limit:limit.value})).data;}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{busy.value=false;}}function reset(){min.value=90;country.value='';limit.value=25;load();}onMounted(async()=>{await load();try{countries.value=(await get('/countries')).data;}catch{}});</script>
<template><div class="container section"><div class="page-head"><p class="eyebrow muted">The aggregation at work</p><h1>Best Quality Restaurants</h1><p>Restaurants whose combined score is greater than {{min}}.</p></div><div class="reading"><h2>How the Quality Score works</h2><p>We add every recorded grade score, keep totals greater than the minimum, and sort from highest to lowest. More grades can mean a higher total, so check the grades recorded column.</p><p class="muted small">This follows the course rubric. In raw New York inspection data, lower inspection scores are better. These totals are not an official safety or dining rating.</p></div><div v-if="rows.length>=3&&!busy&&!error" class="podium" aria-label="Top three restaurants"><RouterLink v-for="(r,i) in [rows[1],rows[0],rows[2]]" :key="r._id" :to="'/restaurants/'+r._id" class="pod" :class="'p'+[2,1,3][i]" v-reveal="i"><span class="medal"><Icon name="award" :size="22"/>#{{[2,1,3][i]}}</span><img :src="restaurantImage(r)" alt="" loading="lazy"><h3>{{r.name}}</h3><p class="small muted">{{r.cuisine}} · {{r.country}}</p><b class="badge A">{{r.totalScore}}</b></RouterLink></div><form class="card pad quality-form" novalidate @submit.prevent="load"><div class="field"><label for="quality-min">Minimum score</label><input id="quality-min" v-model="min" type="number" min="0" :aria-invalid="!!minErr" aria-describedby="quality-min-msg" @input="minErr=''"><p id="quality-min-msg" v-if="minErr" class="field-msg">{{minErr}}</p></div><div class="field"><label for="quality-country">Country</label><select id="quality-country" v-model="country" @change="load"><option value="">All countries</option><option v-for="c in countries" :key="c.slug">{{c.name}}</option></select></div><div class="field"><label for="quality-limit">Show</label><select id="quality-limit" v-model="limit" @change="load"><option>10</option><option>25</option><option>50</option></select></div><div class="qf-actions"><button class="cta" :class="{'is-loading':busy}" :disabled="busy"><span>Apply</span></button><button type="button" class="secondary" :disabled="busy||(min==90&&!country&&limit==25)" @click="reset">Reset</button></div></form><p v-if="busy" role="status" class="small muted" style="margin-top:16px"><span class="spinner-inline"></span> Calculating the top scores…</p><p v-if="error" class="error-box" role="alert">{{error}}</p><div v-else class="card table-wrap" style="margin-top:24px"><table><thead><tr><th>Rank</th><th>Name</th><th>Region</th><th>Cuisine</th><th>Country</th><th>Grades recorded</th><th>Quality Score</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr v-for="(r,i) in rows" :key="r._id" :style="{'--i':i}" class="row-in"><td>{{i+1}}</td><td><RouterLink :to="'/restaurants/'+r._id">{{r.name}}</RouterLink></td><td>{{r.borough}}</td><td>{{r.cuisine}}</td><td>{{r.country}}</td><td>{{r.gradeCount}}</td><td><b class="badge A">{{r.totalScore}}</b></td></tr></tbody></table><div v-if="!rows.length&&!busy" class="empty"><h2>No restaurants above this score</h2><p>Lower the minimum score or choose another country.</p></div></div><p class="small muted" style="margin-top:18px">Calculated in MongoDB using $unwind, $group with $sum, $match and $sort. Closed and deleted restaurants are excluded.</p></div></template>
