client/src/views/ClaimView.vue

<script setup>import {ref,onMounted} from 'vue';import {useRoute,useRouter} from 'vue-router';import {get,post,message} from '../services/api.js';import {useUi} from '../stores/index.js';const route=useRoute(),router=useRouter(),ui=useUi(),r=ref(null),error=ref(''),busy=ref(false),touched=ref(false),b=ref({evidenceType:'business_email',evidenceUrl:'',note:''});onMounted(async()=>{try{r.value=(await get('/restaurants/'+route.params.id)).data;}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}});const noteErr=()=>touched.value&&b.value.note.trim().length<10?'Please explain in at least 10 characters how we can verify your claim.':'';const urlErr=()=>{const v=b.value.evidenceUrl;if(!v)return '';try{return /^https?:$/.test(new URL(v).protocol)?'':'Enter a full link starting with https://';}catch{return 'Enter a full link starting with https://';}};async function submit(){touched.value=true;if(noteErr()||urlErr()){document.querySelector('[aria-invalid=true]')?.focus();return;}busy.value=true;try{await post('/restaurants/'+route.params.id+'/claim',b.value);ui.toast('Your ownership claim is awaiting review.');router.push('/owner');}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{busy.value=false;}}</script>
<template><div class="container section reading"><div class="page-head"><h1>Claim {{r?.name||'a restaurant'}}</h1><p>Tell the team how to verify your relationship with this restaurant. Your email must be verified.</p></div><p v-if="error" class="error-box">{{error}}</p><form class="card pad stack" novalidate @submit.prevent="submit"><div class="field"><label for="claim-type">Evidence type</label><select id="claim-type" v-model="b.evidenceType"><option value="business_email">Business email</option><option value="document_link">Business document link</option><option value="phone">Business phone (manual review)</option></select></div><div class="field"><label for="claim-link">Evidence link (optional)</label><input id="claim-link" v-model="b.evidenceUrl" type="url" placeholder="https://" :aria-invalid="!!urlErr()" aria-describedby="claim-link-msg" @blur="touched=true"><p id="claim-link-msg" v-if="urlErr()" class="field-msg">{{urlErr()}}</p></div><div class="field"><label for="claim-note">How can the team verify this claim? <span class="req">*</span></label><textarea id="claim-note" v-model="b.note" maxlength="1000" :aria-invalid="!!noteErr()" aria-describedby="claim-note-msg" @blur="touched=true"></textarea><p id="claim-note-msg" v-if="noteErr()" class="field-msg">{{noteErr()}}</p></div><p class="small muted">Claims are manually reviewed. Phone codes and document OCR are not configured.</p><button class="cta" :class="{'is-loading':busy}" :disabled="busy"><span>Submit claim</span></button></form></div></template>

client/src/views/CollectionView.vue

<script setup>import {ref,onMounted,watch} from 'vue';import {useRoute} from 'vue-router';import {get,message} from '../services/api.js';import {useSaved} from '../stores/index.js';import RestaurantCard from '../components/restaurants/RestaurantCard.vue';const route=useRoute(),saved=useSaved(),data=ref([]),error=ref(''),busy=ref(false),title=ref('');async function load(){busy.value=true;error.value='';try{const kind=route.meta.kind;if(kind==='saved'||kind==='compare'||kind==='recent'){title.value={saved:'Your saved restaurants',compare:'Compare restaurants',recent:'Recently viewed'}[kind];const ids=kind==='saved'?saved.saved:kind==='compare'?saved.compare:saved.recent;data.value=ids.length?(await get('/restaurants/'+(kind==='compare'&&ids.length>=2?'compare':'by-ids'),{ids:ids.join(',')})).data:[];}else if(kind==='surprise'){title.value='Let us pick your next table';data.value=[(await get('/restaurants/random')).data];}else if(route.params.id){const c=(await get('/collections/'+route.params.id)).data;title.value=c.name;data.value=c.restaurants;}else{title.value={trending:'Trending this week',new:'Newly added restaurants','top-rated':'Top rated by diners','most-viewed':'Most viewed restaurants'}[kind];data.value=(await get('/restaurants/'+kind)).data;}}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{busy.value=false;}}onMounted(load);watch(()=>route.fullPath,load);watch(()=>[saved.saved.join(),saved.compare.join()],load);</script>
<template><div class="container section"><div class="page-head"><h1>{{title}}</h1><p v-if="route.meta.kind==='compare'">Choose two or three restaurants using the Compare button.</p><p v-if="route.meta.kind==='saved'">Your shortlist is stored in this browser. Log in to sync it to your account.</p><p v-if="route.meta.kind==='top-rated'">At least two published reviews are required to enter this list.</p><button v-if="route.meta.kind==='surprise'" class="cta" @click="load" :disabled="busy">{{busy?'Picking...':'Surprise Me again'}}</button></div><p v-if="error" class="error-box">{{error}}</p><div v-else-if="busy&&!data.length" class="skeleton"></div><template v-else-if="data.length"><div v-if="route.meta.kind==='compare'" class="card table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Compare</th><th v-for="r in data" :key="r._id"><RouterLink :to="'/restaurants/'+r._id">{{r.name}}</RouterLink></th></tr></thead><tbody><tr v-for="[label,key] in [['Cuisine','cuisine'],['Borough / Region','borough'],['Country','country'],['Quality Score','totalScore'],['Rating','avgRating'],['Reviews','reviewCount']]" :key="key"><th>{{label}}</th><td v-for="r in data" :key="r._id">{{r[key]}}</td></tr><tr v-for="key in ['priceRange','openingHours','dietaryOptions']" :key="key"><th>{{key}}</th><td v-for="r in data" :key="r._id">{{Array.isArray(r.profile?.[key])?r.profile[key].join(', '):r.profile?.[key]}}</td></tr><tr><th>Remove</th><td v-for="r in data" :key="r._id"><button class="secondary" @click="saved.comparison(r._id)">Remove</button></td></tr></tbody></table></div><div v-else class="grid stagger"><RestaurantCard v-for="(r,i) in data" :key="r._id" :restaurant="r" :index="i%3" :style="{'--i':i%9}"/></div></template><div v-else class="card empty-results"><h2>Nothing on this list yet</h2><p class="muted">Browse restaurants to find your next table.</p><div class="row"><RouterLink to="/restaurants" class="btn cta">Browse Restaurants</RouterLink></div></div></div></template>

client/src/views/ContactView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed} from 'vue';import {useRouter} from 'vue-router';import {post,message} from '../services/api.js';import {useFormValidation,required,email,minLength} from '../composables/useFormValidation.js';import {useUnsavedGuard} from '../composables/useUnsavedGuard.js';import ConfirmDialog from '../components/ConfirmDialog.vue';
const router=useRouter(),b=ref({name:'',email:'',subject:'',message:'',website:''}),busy=ref(false),error=ref(''),sent=ref(false);
const {errors,touched,shake,validateField,onInput,validateAll,setServerErrors}=useFormValidation(b,{name:[required('Please tell us your name.')],email:[required('Please enter your email address.'),email()],subject:[required('Choose a subject so we can route your message.')],message:[required('Write a short message.'),minLength(10,'Please write at least 10 characters so we can help.')]},{idPrefix:'contact-'});
const dirty=computed(()=>!sent.value&&(b.value.name||b.value.email||b.value.subject||b.value.message));
const guard=useUnsavedGuard(()=>!!dirty.value);
async function submit(){error.value='';if(!await validateAll())return;busy.value=true;try{await post('/contact',b.value);sent.value=true;router.push('/thank-you');}catch(e){error.value=message(e);setServerErrors(e.response?.data?.errors);}finally{busy.value=false;}}
const bad=k=>!!(touched[k]&&errors[k]);
</script>
<template>
<div class="container section"><div class="page-head"><p class="eyebrow muted">We read every message</p><h1>Say hello</h1><p>Questions, corrections and ideas are always welcome.</p></div>
<div class="split"><form novalidate :aria-busy="busy" class="card pad stack" :class="{shake}" @submit.prevent="submit">
  <p v-if="error" class="form-status error" role="alert">{{error}}</p>
  <div class="form-grid">
    <div class="field"><label for="contact-name">Your name <span class="req" aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><input id="contact-name" v-model="b.name" autocomplete="name" :aria-invalid="bad('name')" aria-describedby="contact-name-msg" @blur="validateField('name')" @input="onInput('name')"><p id="contact-name-msg" v-if="bad('name')" class="field-msg">{{errors.name}}</p></div>
    <div class="field"><label for="contact-email">Email address <span class="req" aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><input id="contact-email" v-model="b.email" type="email" autocomplete="email" :aria-invalid="bad('email')" aria-describedby="contact-email-msg" @blur="validateField('email')" @input="onInput('email')"><p id="contact-email-msg" v-if="bad('email')" class="field-msg">{{errors.email}}</p></div>
  </div>
  <div class="field"><label for="contact-subject">Subject <span class="req" aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><select id="contact-subject" v-model="b.subject" :aria-invalid="bad('subject')" aria-describedby="contact-subject-msg" @blur="validateField('subject')" @change="validateField('subject')"><option value="">Choose a subject</option><option v-for="x in ['General question','Report wrong information','Add or remove a restaurant','Feedback or idea','Bug report','Partnership','Something else']" :key="x">{{x}}</option></select><p id="contact-subject-msg" v-if="bad('subject')" class="field-msg">{{errors.subject}}</p></div>
  <div class="field"><label for="contact-message">Message <span class="req" aria-hidden="true">*</span></label><textarea id="contact-message" v-model="b.message" maxlength="1000" placeholder="Tell us how we can help" :aria-invalid="bad('message')" aria-describedby="contact-message-msg" @blur="validateField('message')" @input="onInput('message')"></textarea><div class="msg-row"><p id="contact-message-msg" v-if="bad('message')" class="field-msg">{{errors.message}}</p><span class="char-count" :class="{near:b.message.length>900}">{{b.message.length}} / 1000</span></div></div>
  <div class="honeypot" aria-hidden="true"><label for="contact-website">Leave this empty</label><input id="contact-website" v-model="b.website" tabindex="-1" autocomplete="off"></div>
  <p class="small muted">By sending this form you agree that we may use your details to reply to you. See our <RouterLink to="/privacy">Privacy Policy</RouterLink>.</p>
  <button class="cta" :class="{'is-loading':busy}" :disabled="busy"><span>Send message</span></button>
</form>
<aside class="card contact-aside"><img class="contact-photo" src="/images/photos/bistro.jpg" alt="A comfortable restaurant dining area" loading="lazy"><div class="pad"><h2>Good to know</h2><p>Please do not include passwords or private details in your message.</p><p>This form stores messages in the project database for the team to review.</p><p class="small muted">hello@bonappefind.example is a placeholder address. Replace it before public launch.</p></div></aside></div>
<ConfirmDialog v-if="guard.pending.value" title="Leave without sending?" text="Your message has not been sent. If you leave now, what you typed will be lost." confirm-label="Leave page" cancel-label="Keep writing" @cancel="guard.stay" @confirm="guard.leave"/>
</div>
</template>

client/src/views/CountriesView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onMounted} from 'vue';
import {get,message} from '../services/api.js';
import CountryCard from '../components/countries/CountryCard.vue';
import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';
const data=ref([]),continent=ref(''),search=ref(''),error=ref(''),loading=ref(true);
const continents=computed(()=>['',...new Set(data.value.map(c=>c.continent))]);
const filtered=computed(()=>{const t=search.value.trim().toLowerCase();return data.value.filter(c=>(!continent.value||c.continent===continent.value)&&(!t||[c.name,c.capital,c.tagline,c.continent].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(t)));});
function reset(){search.value='';continent.value='';}
onMounted(async()=>{try{data.value=(await get('/countries')).data;}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{loading.value=false;}});
</script>
<template>
  <div class="container section">
    <div class="page-head"><p class="eyebrow">A seat at every table</p><h1>Eat your way around the world</h1><p>Eight country guides with dishes, dining etiquette, tipping and food festivals.</p></div>
    <div class="toolbar"><div class="search-wrap"><label class="sr-only" for="country-search">Search countries</label><input id="country-search" v-model="search" type="search" placeholder="Search countries, capitals or dishes" autocomplete="off"><button v-if="search" type="button" class="clear-x" aria-label="Clear search" @click="search=''"><Icon name="x" :size="16"/></button></div><span class="meta-count" role="status" aria-live="polite">{{filtered.length}} of {{data.length}} countries</span></div>
    <div class="row cont-row" role="group" aria-label="Filter by continent"><Icon name="globe" :size="20"/><button v-for="c in continents" :key="c" class="ghost pill small toggle" :class="{active:continent===c}" :aria-pressed="continent===c" @click="continent=c">{{c||'All'}}</button><button v-if="search||continent" class="link-reset" @click="reset">Reset</button></div>
    <p v-if="error" class="error-box" role="alert">{{error}}</p>
    <div v-else-if="loading" class="countries-grid"><div v-for="n in 4" :key="n" class="skeleton" style="min-height:320px"></div></div>
    <TransitionGroup v-else name="list" tag="div" class="countries-grid">
      <CountryCard v-for="(c,i) in filtered" :key="c.slug" :country="c" :index="i%4" :style="{'--i':i}"/>
    </TransitionGroup>
    <div v-if="!filtered.length&&!error&&!loading" class="card empty-results"><span class="empty-icon"><Icon name="globe" :size="28"/></span><h2>No countries match</h2><p class="muted">Try a different word or choose another continent.</p><div class="row"><button class="cta" @click="reset">Reset filters</button></div></div>
  </div>
</template>
<style scoped>
.countries-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,minmax(0,1fr));gap:22px}
.cont-row{margin-bottom:28px}.list-enter-active,.list-leave-active{transition:all .4s var(--ease)}.list-leave-active{position:absolute}.list-move{transition:transform .4s var(--ease)}.list-enter-from,.list-leave-to{opacity:0;transform:scale(.9)}
@media(max-width:1000px){.countries-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,minmax(0,1fr))}}@media(max-width:600px){.countries-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}}

<script setup>import {countryImage} from '../utils/formatters.js';import {ref,onMounted,watch} from 'vue';import {useRoute} from 'vue-router';import {get,message} from '../services/api.js';import RestaurantCard from '../components/restaurants/RestaurantCard.vue';const route=useRoute(),c=ref(null),restaurants=ref([]),error=ref('');async function load(){try{const [a,b]=await Promise.all([get('/countries/'+route.params.slug),get('/countries/'+route.params.slug+'/restaurants')]);c.value=a.data;restaurants.value=b.data;document.title=c.value.name+' Food Guide | BonAppé Find';}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}}watch(()=>route.params.slug,load);onMounted(load);</script>

client/src/views/DashboardView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onMounted,onUnmounted,watch} from 'vue';import {useRoute,useRouter} from 'vue-router';import {get,post,patch,put,del,message} from '../services/api.js';import {useAuth,useUi} from '../stores/index.js';import ConfirmDialog from '../components/ConfirmDialog.vue';import StatChart from '../components/StatChart.vue';import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';import DashShell from '../components/layout/DashShell.vue';
const router=useRouter(),route=useRoute(),auth=useAuth(),ui=useUi(),summary=ref({}),rows=ref([]),meta=ref({}),error=ref(''),busy=ref(false),tab=ref('Restaurants'),charts=ref({}),settings=ref({banner:'',maintenance:false,bannerExpiresAt:''}),deleting=ref(null),reviews=ref([]),reports=ref([]),suggestions=ref([]),userForm=ref({name:'',email:'',password:'',role:'moderator'}),importText=ref(''),importResult=ref(null),filter=ref(''),showUser=ref(false),query=ref(''),acting=ref(false),ask=ref(null),userTouched=ref(false),importErr=ref('');const owner=computed(()=>route.path.startsWith('/owner'));const tabs=computed(()=>owner.value?['Restaurants','Reviews','Messages','Analytics','Notifications']:auth.user?.role==='moderator'?['Restaurants','Trash','Claims','Moderation','Reviews','Logs','Statistics']:['Restaurants','Trash','Claims','Owners','Users','Suggestions','Moderation','Messages','Subscribers','Reviews','Logs','Login history','Statistics','Settings','Import / Export','Data quality']);const title=computed(()=>owner.value?'Restaurant owner dashboard':'Admin dashboard');
const endpoints={'Restaurants':'/admin/restaurants','Trash':'/admin/restaurants?trash=true','Claims':'/claims','Owners':'/admin/users','Users':'/admin/users','Messages':'/contact','Subscribers':'/admin/subscribers','Reviews':'/admin/reviews','Logs':'/admin/logs','Login history':'/admin/login-history','Data quality':'/admin/quality-report','Notifications':'/notifications'};
let loadVersion=0,disposed=false,refreshTimer,countTimer,countVersion=0,countBusy=false;
const lastUpdated=ref(null),countsUpdated=ref(null),countError=ref('');
async function refreshCounts(){
 if(disposed||document.hidden||countBusy)return;
 countBusy=true;const version=++countVersion,isOwner=owner.value;
 try{
  const result=await get(isOwner?'/owner/stats':'/admin/stats/summary',{_refresh:Date.now()});
  if(!disposed&&version===countVersion&&isOwner===owner.value){summary.value=result.data;countsUpdated.value=new Date();countError.value='';}
 }catch(e){if(!disposed&&version===countVersion)countError.value='Counts could not refresh. Retrying automatically.';}
 finally{countBusy=false;}
}
function refreshVisible(){refreshCounts();autoRefresh();}
async function load({silent=false}={}){
 const version=++loadVersion,currentTab=tab.value,isOwner=owner.value,currentFilter=filter.value;
 if(!silent)busy.value=true;
 error.value='';
 const fresh=(url,params={})=>get(url,{...params,_refresh:Date.now()});
 try{
  await auth.load();
  await refreshCounts();
  let nextRows=[],nextMeta={},nextCharts={},nextReviews=[],nextReports=[],nextSuggestions=[],nextSettings;
  const restricted=isOwner&&!auth.canManage&&['Restaurants','Reviews','Messages'].includes(currentTab);
  if(!restricted){
   if(['Statistics','Analytics'].includes(currentTab)){
    const grouped=await Promise.all(['borough','cuisine','country'].map(key=>fresh('/restaurants/stats/by-'+key)));
    ['borough','cuisine','country'].forEach((key,index)=>nextCharts[key]=grouped[index].data);
    if(!isOwner)nextReviews=(await fresh('/admin/stats/reviews')).data;
   }else if(currentTab==='Settings')nextSettings=(await fresh('/settings')).data;
   else if(currentTab==='Moderation'){
    const a=await Promise.all([fresh('/admin/reviews'),fresh('/reports'),fresh('/suggestions')]);
    nextReviews=a[0].data;nextReports=a[1].data;nextSuggestions=a[2].data;
   }else if(currentTab!=='Import / Export'){
    const endpoint=isOwner&&['Restaurants','Reviews','Messages'].includes(currentTab)?'/owner/'+currentTab.toLowerCase():endpoints[currentTab];
    if(endpoint){const x=await fresh(endpoint,{limit:100,status:currentFilter||undefined,role:currentTab==='Owners'?'owner':undefined});nextRows=x.data;nextMeta=x.meta||{};}
   }
  }
  if(disposed||version!==loadVersion)return false;
  rows.value=nextRows;meta.value=nextMeta;charts.value=nextCharts;reviews.value=nextReviews;reports.value=nextReports;suggestions.value=nextSuggestions;
  if(nextSettings)settings.value={...settings.value,...nextSettings};
  lastUpdated.value=new Date();return true;
 }catch(e){if(!disposed&&version===loadVersion)error.value=message(e);return false;}
 finally{if(!disposed&&version===loadVersion)busy.value=false;}
}
function autoRefresh(){
 if(document.hidden||busy.value||acting.value||showUser.value||ask.value||deleting.value||['Settings','Import / Export'].includes(tab.value)||document.activeElement?.matches('input,textarea,select'))return;
 load({silent:true});
}
async function action(fn,msg='Changes saved.'){if(acting.value)return false;acting.value=true;try{await fn();ui.toast(msg);const refreshed=await load();if(!refreshed)ui.toast('The action was saved, but the dashboard could not refresh. Use Refresh dashboard.','error');return true;}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');return false;}finally{acting.value=false;}}
const shownRows=computed(()=>{const t=query.value.trim().toLowerCase();return t?rows.value.filter(r=>JSON.stringify(r).toLowerCase().includes(t)):rows.value;});const searchable=computed(()=>!['Settings','Statistics','Analytics','Import / Export','Moderation'].includes(tab.value));
async function confirmed(){const a=ask.value;if(!a)return;const ok=await action(a.fn,a.done);if(ok)ask.value=null;}
const statLabel=k=>({approved:'Approved listings',pending:'Pending approval',deleted:'In trash',cuisines:'Cuisines',owners:'Owners',reviews:'Reviews',messages:'Messages',subscribers:'Subscribers',pendingRequests:'Pending requests',pendingOwners:'Pending owners',approvedOwners:'Approved owners',users:'Total users',rejected:'Rejected listings',unreadNotifications:'Unread notifications',claims:'Pending claims',restaurantSuggestions:'Restaurant suggestions'}[k]||k.replace(/([A-Z])/g,' $1').replace(/^./,c=>c.toUpperCase()));
const userErr=computed(()=>({name:userTouched.value&&!userForm.value.name.trim()?'Enter a name.':'',email:userTouched.value&&!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(userForm.value.email)?'Enter an email address like name@example.com.':'',password:userTouched.value&&userForm.value.password.length<8?'Use at least 8 characters.':''}));
function chosenFromPath(){const key=route.path.split('/')[2]||'';const map={restaurants:'Restaurants',trash:'Trash',claims:'Claims',owners:'Owners',users:'Users',moderation:'Moderation',logs:'Logs','login-history':'Login history',stats:'Statistics',settings:'Settings',reviews:'Reviews',messages:'Messages',analytics:'Analytics',notifications:'Notifications',subscribers:'Subscribers','import-export':'Import / Export',quality:'Data quality'};tab.value=map[key]||'Restaurants';}
function choose(x){filter.value='';query.value='';const slugs={'Restaurants':'restaurants','Trash':'trash','Claims':'claims','Owners':'owners','Users':'users','Suggestions':'suggestions','Moderation':'moderation','Messages':'messages','Subscribers':'subscribers','Reviews':'reviews','Logs':'logs','Login history':'login-history','Statistics':'stats','Analytics':'analytics','Notifications':'notifications','Settings':'settings','Import / Export':'import-export','Data quality':'quality'};router.push((owner.value?'/owner/':'/admin/')+slugs[x]);}function columns(row){return Object.entries(row).filter(([k])=>!['_id','__v','updatedAt','passwordHash','tokenVersion'].includes(k));}function readable(value){if(value===null||value===undefined)return '—';if(typeof value==='object')return JSON.stringify(value);return String(value);}async function remove(){const r=deleting.value;if(await action(()=>del('/restaurants/'+r._id),'Restaurant moved to Trash.'))deleting.value=null;}async function exportData(format){try{if(format==='csv'){const {api}=await import('../services/api.js');const r=await api.get('/admin/restaurants/export',{params:{format},responseType:'blob'});download(r.data,'restaurants.csv');}else{const r=await get('/admin/restaurants/export');download(new Blob([JSON.stringify(r.data,null,2)],{type:'application/json'}),'restaurants.json');}}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}function download(blob,name){const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob),a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=name;a.click();URL.revokeObjectURL(url);}async function importData(){importErr.value='';try{const records=JSON.parse(importText.value);if(!Array.isArray(records)){importErr.value='The JSON must be an array of restaurant records, like [ {...}, {...} ].';return;}importResult.value=(await post('/admin/restaurants/import',{records})).data;await load();}catch(e){if(e instanceof SyntaxError)importErr.value='That is not valid JSON. Check for a missing comma or bracket.';else ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}async function userCreate(){userTouched.value=true;if(Object.values(userErr.value).some(Boolean))return;if(await action(()=>post('/admin/users',userForm.value),'Account created.')){showUser.value=false;userTouched.value=false;userForm.value={name:'',email:'',password:'',role:'moderator'};}}
const tabIcons={Restaurants:'utensils',Trash:'trash',Claims:'shield',Owners:'user',Users:'user',Suggestions:'book',Moderation:'shield',Messages:'mail',Subscribers:'mail',Reviews:'star',Logs:'book','Login history':'clock',Statistics:'trending',Analytics:'trending',Settings:'sliders','Import / Export':'table','Data quality':'check',Notifications:'calendar'};
const navItems=computed(()=>tabs.value.map(label=>({label,icon:tabIcons[label]||'grid',badge:label==='Notifications'?(summary.value.unreadNotifications||''):''})));
const adminKeys=['restaurants','approved','pending','deleted','cuisines','users','owners','pendingOwners','approvedOwners','claims','restaurantSuggestions','reviews','messages','subscribers','pendingRequests'];
const stripStats=computed(()=>Object.entries(summary.value).filter(([k])=>owner.value?true:adminKeys.includes(k)).map(([k,v])=>({label:statLabel(k),value:v})));
const profileStats=computed(()=>{const m=summary.value,pick=owner.value?['restaurants','views','saves']:['approved','pending','reviews'];return pick.filter(k=>m[k]!==undefined).map(k=>({label:statLabel(k).replace('Approved listings','Approved').replace('Pending approval','Pending'),value:m[k]}));});
const breakdown=computed(()=>{const m=summary.value;if(owner.value)return m.restaurants?[{label:'Approved',value:m.approved||0},{label:'Pending',value:m.pending||0},{label:'Rejected',value:m.rejected||0}]:[];return m.approved!==undefined?[{label:'Approved',value:m.approved||0},{label:'Pending',value:m.pending||0},{label:'In trash',value:m.deleted||0}]:[];});
async function logoutNow(){await auth.logout();router.push('/logged-out');}
onMounted(()=>{chosenFromPath();load();refreshTimer=setInterval(autoRefresh,15000);countTimer=setInterval(refreshCounts,5000);window.addEventListener('focus',refreshVisible);document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',refreshVisible);});onUnmounted(()=>{disposed=true;++loadVersion;clearInterval(refreshTimer);clearInterval(countTimer);++countVersion;window.removeEventListener('focus',refreshVisible);document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange',refreshVisible);});watch(()=>route.path,()=>{chosenFromPath();load();});
</script>
<template><DashShell :class="{acting}" :title="title" subtitle="Manage listings, community feedback and account activity." :nav="navItems" :active="tab" :stats="stripStats" :profile-stats="profileStats" :role-label="owner?'Owner workspace':'Admin workspace'" :add-link="auth.canManage" :breakdown="breakdown" breakdown-title="Listings by approval" @select="choose" @logout="logoutNow"><section v-if="owner" class="notice" role="status"><h2>Owner application: {{auth.user?.ownerProfile?.verificationStatus||'pending'}}</h2><p v-if="!auth.user?.emailVerified">Verify your email before managing restaurant listings. <RouterLink to="/verify-email">Verify email</RouterLink></p><p v-if="auth.user?.ownerProfile?.verificationStatus==='pending'">Your application is awaiting administrator review. Import upserts by restaurant_id.</p><label for="import-json">Records</label><textarea id="import-json" v-model="importText" style="min-height:220px" :aria-invalid="!!importErr" aria-describedby="import-msg" @input="importErr=''"></textarea><p id="import-msg" v-if="importErr" class="field-msg">{{importErr}}</p><button class="cta" :disabled="!importText.trim()">Validate and import</button></form><pre v-if="importResult" class="card pad" style="overflow:auto">{{JSON.stringify(importResult,null,2)}}</pre></section><section v-else-if="['Statistics','Analytics'].includes(tab)" class="stack"><p v-if="owner" class="muted">Your listing counters appear above. client/src/views/ExploreView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onMounted,watch} from 'vue';
import {useRoute} from 'vue-router';
import {get,message} from '../services/api.js';
import {dishImage} from '../utils/formatters.js';
import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';
const route=useRoute(),data=ref([]),error=ref(''),loading=ref(true),search=ref('');
async function load(){loading.value=true;try{data.value=(await get(route.meta.kind==='cuisines'?'/restaurants/stats/by-cuisine':'/countries')).data;}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{loading.value=false;}}
const items=computed(()=>{const t=search.value.trim().toLowerCase();const k=route.meta.kind;if(k==='cuisines')return data.value.filter(c=>!t||String(c._id).toLowerCase().includes(t));return data.value.flatMap(c=>(k==='dishes'?c.signatureDishes:c.festivals).map(item=>({c,item}))).filter(({c,item})=>!t||[c.name,item.name,item.description].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(t));});
onMounted(load);watch(()=>route.path,()=>{search.value='';load();});
</script>
<template>
  <div class="container section">
    <div class="page-head"><p class="eyebrow">Taste the world</p><h1>{{route.meta.kind==='cuisines'?'Find a cuisine you love':route.meta.kind==='dishes'?'Dish glossary':'Food festival calendar'}}</h1><p>Explore the food culture behind every plate.</p></div>
    <div class="toolbar"><div class="search-wrap"><label class="sr-only" for="explore-search">Filter this page</label><input id="explore-search" v-model="search" type="search" :placeholder="route.meta.kind==='cuisines'?'Search cuisines':route.meta.kind==='dishes'?'Search dishes or countries':'Search festivals or countries'" autocomplete="off"><button v-if="search" type="button" class="clear-x" aria-label="Clear search" @click="search=''"><Icon name="x" :size="16"/></button></div><span class="meta-count" role="status" aria-live="polite">{{items.length}} shown</span></div>
    <p v-if="error" class="error-box" role="alert">{{error}}</p>
    <div v-else-if="loading" class="grid"><div v-for="n in 6" :key="n" class="skeleton"></div></div>
    <div v-else-if="!items.length" class="card empty-results"><span class="empty-icon"><Icon name="search" :size="28"/></span><h2>Nothing matches “{{search}}”</h2><p class="muted">Check the spelling or clear the search.</p><div class="row"><button class="cta" @click="search=''">Clear search</button></div></div>
    <div v-else-if="route.meta.kind==='cuisines'" class="cuisine-grid">
      <RouterLink v-for="(c,i) in items" :key="c._id" :to="{path:'/restaurants',query:{cuisine:c._id}}" class="cuisine-card" v-reveal="i%4">
        <img :src="dishImage(c._id)" :alt="c._id+' food inspiration'" loading="lazy"><div class="cc-text"><h2>{{c._id}}</h2><p><Icon name="utensils" :size="14"/> {{c.count}} {{c.count===1?'restaurant':'restaurants'}}</p><span>Browse <Icon name="arrow-right" :size="16"/></span></div>
      </RouterLink>
    </div>
    <div v-else class="grid stagger"><article v-for="({c,item},i) in items" :key="c.slug+item.name" class="card pad info-card" :style="{'--i':i%9}">
      <span class="info-icon"><Icon :name="route.meta.kind==='dishes'?'bowl':'calendar'" :size="22"/></span>
      <p class="eyebrow muted">{{c.name}}</p><h2>{{item.name}}</h2><p v-if="item.when" class="muted"><Icon name="clock" :size="14"/> {{item.when}}</p><p>{{item.description}}</p>
      <RouterLink v-if="route.meta.kind==='dishes'" class="link-arrow" :to="{path:'/restaurants',query:{search:item.name,field:'dish'}}">Find restaurants serving this dish <Icon name="arrow-right" :size="16"/></RouterLink>
      <RouterLink v-else class="link-arrow" :to="'/countries/'+c.slug">Read the country guide <Icon name="arrow-right" :size="16"/></RouterLink>
    </article></div>
  </div>
</template>

client/src/views/FaqView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onMounted} from 'vue';import {get,message} from '../services/api.js';import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';
const data=ref([]),search=ref(''),category=ref(''),error=ref(''),loading=ref(true),open=ref(new Set());
const categories=computed(()=>['',...new Set(data.value.map(f=>f.category))]);
const term=computed(()=>search.value.trim().toLowerCase());
const filtered=computed(()=>data.value.filter(x=>(!category.value||x.category===category.value)&&(!term.value||(x.question+' '+x.answer).toLowerCase().includes(term.value))));
function parts(text){const t=term.value;if(!t)return [{t:text}];const out=[];let rest=text,low=text.toLowerCase(),i;while((i=low.indexOf(t))>=0){if(i)out.push({t:rest.slice(0,i)});out.push({t:rest.slice(i,i+t.length),hit:true});rest=rest.slice(i+t.length);low=low.slice(i+t.length);}if(rest)out.push({t:rest});return out;}
function toggle(id){const s=new Set(open.value);s.has(id)?s.delete(id):s.add(id);open.value=s;}
function reset(){search.value='';category.value='';}
onMounted(async()=>{try{data.value=(await get('/faqs')).data;}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{loading.value=false;}});
</script>
<template>
<div class="container section reading">
  <div class="page-head"><h1>Frequently asked questions</h1><p>Quick answers to the things people ask us most.</p></div>
  <img class="faq-photo" src="/images/photos/coffee.jpg" alt="A cozy café dining area" loading="lazy">
  <div class="toolbar"><div class="field" style="flex:1;margin:0"><label for="faq-search">Search the FAQs</label><div class="search-wrap"><input id="faq-search" v-model="search" type="search" placeholder="Try “review” or “owner”" autocomplete="off"><button v-if="search" type="button" class="clear-x" aria-label="Clear search" @click="search=''"><Icon name="x" :size="16"/></button></div></div></div>
  <div class="row cat-row" role="group" aria-label="Filter by category"><button v-for="x in categories" :key="x" class="secondary toggle pill small" :class="{active:category===x}" :aria-pressed="category===x" @click="category=x">{{x||'All'}}</button></div>
  <p class="small muted faq-count" role="status" aria-live="polite" v-if="!loading&&!error">{{filtered.length}} {{filtered.length===1?'answer':'answers'}}<template v-if="search||category"> · <button type="button" class="link-reset" @click="reset">Reset</button></template></p>
  <p v-if="error" class="error-box" role="alert">{{error}}</p>
  <div v-if="loading" class="skeleton" aria-label="Loading FAQs"></div>
  <TransitionGroup v-else name="acc-list" tag="div" class="acc-list">
    <div v-for="f in filtered" :key="f._id" class="acc" :class="{open:open.has(f._id)}">
      <h2 class="acc-h"><button type="button" class="acc-head" :id="'faq-h-'+f._id" :aria-expanded="open.has(f._id)" :aria-controls="'faq-p-'+f._id" @click="toggle(f._id)"><span><template v-for="(p,i) in parts(f.question)" :key="i"><mark v-if="p.hit">{{p.t}}</mark><template v-else>{{p.t}}</template></template></span><Icon name="chevron-down" :size="20"/></button></h2>
      <div class="acc-panel" :id="'faq-p-'+f._id" role="region" :aria-labelledby="'faq-h-'+f._id"><div><p><template v-for="(p,i) in parts(f.answer)" :key="i"><mark v-if="p.hit">{{p.t}}</mark><template v-else>{{p.t}}</template></template></p></div></div>
    </div>
  </TransitionGroup>
  <div v-if="!loading&&!error&&!filtered.length" class="card empty-results"><span class="empty-icon"><Icon name="search" :size="28"/></span><h2>No answers match</h2><p class="muted">Try another word, or send us a message and we will help.</p><div class="row"><button class="cta" @click="reset">Reset search</button><RouterLink to="/contact" class="btn secondary">Contact us</RouterLink></div></div>
  <RouterLink v-else to="/contact" class="btn secondary" style="margin-top:24px">Still stuck? Contact us</RouterLink>
</div>
</template>
<style scoped>.cat-row{margin:18px 0 12px}.faq-count{margin:0 0 18px}.acc-h{margin:0;font:inherit}.acc-list{position:relative}.acc-list-enter-active,.acc-list-leave-active{transition:opacity .25s var(--ease),transform .25s var(--ease)}.acc-list-enter-from,.acc-list-leave-to{opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)}.acc-list-leave-active{position:absolute;width:100%}.acc-list-move{transition:transform .3s var(--ease)}</style>

client/src/views/GuidesView.vue

<script setup>import {ref,computed,onMounted,watch} from 'vue';import {useRoute} from 'vue-router';import {get,message} from '../services/api.js';const route=useRoute(),search=ref(''),data=ref([]),article=ref(null),error=ref(''),loading=ref(true);async function load(){loading.value=true;error.value='';try{if(route.params.slug)article.value=(await get('/guides/'+route.params.slug)).data;else{article.value=null;data.value=(await get('/guides')).data;}}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{loading.value=false;}}const shown=computed(()=>{const t=search.value.trim().toLowerCase();return data.value.filter(g=>!t||[g.title,g.excerpt,...(g.tags||[])].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(t));});watch(()=>route.fullPath,load);onMounted(load);</script>
<template><div class="container section"><p v-if="error" class="error-box">{{error}}</p><div v-else-if="loading" class="grid" aria-label="Loading guides"><div v-for="n in 3" :key="n" client/src/views/HomeView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onMounted} from 'vue';
import {useRouter} from 'vue-router';
import {get,message} from '../services/api.js';
import {BOROUGHS} from '../utils/constants.js';
import {dishImage} from '../utils/formatters.js';
import RestaurantCard from '../components/restaurants/RestaurantCard.vue';
import CountryCard from '../components/countries/CountryCard.vue';
import StatCard from '../components/common/StatCard.vue';
import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';
const router=useRouter();
const search=ref(''),field=ref('name'),countries=ref([]),restaurants=ref([]),filters=ref({}),total=ref(0),error=ref(''),loading=ref(true);
const popular=['Sushi','Pizza','Bakery','Ramen','Tacos','Thai','Indian','French'];
const cuisineTiles=computed(()=>{
  const list=filters.value.cuisines?.length?filters.value.cuisines:['Filipino','Sushi','Ramen','Italian','Pizza','Mexican','Tacos','French','Bakery','Indian','Thai'];
  return list.slice(0,14);
});
const stats=computed(()=>[
  [total.value,'Restaurants listed','utensils'],
  [countries.value.length,'Country guides','globe'],
  [filters.value.cuisines?.length||0,'Cuisines to explore','bowl'],
  [filters.value.boroughs?.length||0,'Regions to filter by','pin']
]);
const steps=[
  ['01','Search','search','Type a name or cuisine. Narrow your search by region, price and dietary needs.'],
  ['02','Compare','compare','Check the recorded grades, review ratings and the dishes worth ordering.'],
  ['03','Enjoy','chef','Read the story and table manners, then confirm the details before you go.']
];
onMounted(async()=>{
  try{
    const [a,b,c,d]=await Promise.all([get('/countries'),get('/restaurants/featured'),get('/restaurants/meta/filters'),get('/restaurants',{limit:1})]);
    countries.value=a.data;restaurants.value=b.data.slice(0,6);filters.value=c.data;total.value=d.meta.total;
  }catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{loading.value=false;}
});
function submit(){router.push({path:'/restaurants',query:{search:search.value,field:field.value}});}
</script>
<template>
  <div class="home-page">
  <section class="hero">
    <div class="container hero-grid">
      <div class="hero-copy">

<script setup>import {ref,onMounted} from 'vue';import {get,message} from '../services/api.js';import StatChart from '../components/StatChart.vue';const data=ref({}),error=ref('');onMounted(async()=>{try{for(const k of ['borough','cuisine','country'])data.value[k]=(await get('/restaurants/stats/by-'+k)).data;}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}});</script>
<template><div class="container section"><div class="page-head"><p class="eyebrow muted">The collection, in context</p><h1>Insights</h1><p>See how restaurants are grouped by borough, cuisine and country.</p></div><p v-if="error" class="error-box">{{error}}</p><div class="grid"><StatChart v-for="k in ['borough','cuisine','country']" :key="k" :title="'Restaurants by '+k" :rows="data[k]||[]"/></div></div></template>

client/src/views/RestaurantDetailView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onMounted,watch,nextTick} from 'vue';import {useRoute,useRouter} from 'vue-router';import {get,post,patch,del,message,imageUrl,api} from '../services/api.js';import {useAuth,useSaved,useUi} from '../stores/index.js';import RestaurantCard from '../components/restaurants/RestaurantCard.vue';import ConfirmDialog from '../components/ConfirmDialog.vue';import QRCode from 'qrcode';import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';import BaseModal from '../components/common/BaseModal.vue';import GradeBadge from '../components/restaurants/GradeBadge.vue';import StarRating from '../components/common/StarRating.vue';import {restaurantImage,dishImage} from '../utils/formatters.js';
const route=useRoute(),router=useRouter(),auth=useAuth(),saved=useSaved(),ui=useUi(),r=ref(null),reviews=ref([]),similar=ref([]),error=ref(''),busy=ref(false),review=ref({rating:5,title:'',comment:''}),sort=ref('newest'),editing=ref(null),deleting=ref(false),reporting=ref(false),report=ref({reason:'',type:'restaurant_info',details:''}),suggesting=ref(false),suggestion=ref({field:'profile.openingHours',proposedValue:''}),ownerMessage=ref(''),qr=ref(''),lightbox=ref(''),promptM=ref(null),delReview=ref(null),reviewTouched=ref(false),reviewDone=ref(false),reportErr=ref(''),suggestErr=ref(''),ownerErr=ref(''),acting=ref(false);const canEdit=computed(()=>auth.canManage&&(auth.user?.role==='admin'||auth.user?._id===r.value?.ownerId));const address=computed(()=>r.value?[r.value.address?.building,r.value.address?.street,r.value.borough,r.value.address?.zipcode,r.value.country].filter(Boolean).join(' '):'');
async function loadReviews(){try{const id=route.params.id;const [reviewList,record]=await Promise.all([get('/restaurants/'+id+'/reviews',{sort:sort.value}),get('/restaurants/'+id)]);if(route.params.id!==id)return;reviews.value=reviewList.data;r.value=record.data;}catch{}}
async function load(){error.value='';r.value=null;try{r.value=(await get('/restaurants/'+route.params.id)).data;saved.visited(r.value._id);document.title=r.value.name+' | BonAppé Find';let session=sessionStorage.getItem('bonappefind_session');if(!session){session=crypto.randomUUID();sessionStorage.setItem('bonappefind_session',session);}post('/restaurants/'+r.value._id+'/view',{sessionId:session}).catch(()=>{});await loadReviews();similar.value=(await get('/restaurants/'+r.value._id+'/similar')).data;qr.value=await QRCode.toDataURL(location.href,{width:140,margin:1});const script=document.getElementById('restaurant-jsonld')||document.createElement('script');script.id='restaurant-jsonld';script.type='application/ld+json';script.textContent=JSON.stringify({'@context':'https://schema.org','@type':'Restaurant',name:r.value.name,description:r.value.profile.story,servesCuisine:r.value.cuisine,address:{'@type':'PostalAddress',streetAddress:address.value,addressCountry:r.value.country},priceRange:r.value.profile.priceRange});document.head.append(script);}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}}
const commentErr=computed(()=>reviewTouched.value&&review.value.comment.trim().length<5?'Tell other diners a little about your meal (at least 5 characters).':'');
async function submitReview(){reviewTouched.value=true;if(commentErr.value){document.getElementById('review-comment')?.focus();return;}busy.value=true;try{if(editing.value)await patch('/reviews/'+editing.value,review.value);else await post('/restaurants/'+r.value._id+'/reviews',review.value);ui.toast('Thank you! Your review has been saved.');editing.value=null;review.value={rating:5,title:'',comment:''};reviewTouched.value=false;reviewDone.value=true;setTimeout(()=>reviewDone.value=false,2500);await loadReviews();}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}finally{busy.value=false;}}
async function reviewAction(v,action){if(action==='report'||action==='reply'){promptM.value={type:action,v,text:'',err:''};return;}if(action==='delete'){delReview.value=v;return;}try{if(action==='helpful'){await post('/reviews/'+v._id+'/helpful');ui.toast('Marked as helpful.');}await loadReviews();}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}
async function confirmDelReview(){acting.value=true;try{await del('/reviews/'+delReview.value._id);delReview.value=null;await loadReviews();ui.toast('Review deleted.');}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}finally{acting.value=false;}}
async function sendPrompt(){const m=promptM.value;if(m.text.trim().length<3){m.err=m.type==='report'?'Tell us what looks wrong (at least 3 characters).':'Write a short reply (at least 3 characters).';document.getElementById('prompt-text')?.focus();return;}acting.value=true;try{await post('/reviews/'+m.v._id+'/'+m.type,m.type==='report'?{reason:m.text.trim()}:{text:m.text.trim()});promptM.value=null;await loadReviews();ui.toast(m.type==='report'?'Report sent for review.':'Reply posted.');}catch(e){m.err=message(e);}finally{acting.value=false;}}
async function remove(){try{const record=r.value;await del('/restaurants/'+record._id);deleting.value=false;ui.toast('Restaurant deleted.','success',async()=>{await post('/restaurants/'+record._id+'/restore');ui.toast('Restaurant restored.');});router.push('/restaurants');}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}
async function share(){try{if(navigator.share)await navigator.share({title:r.value.name,url:location.href});else{await navigator.clipboard.writeText(location.href);ui.toast('Link copied to your clipboard.');}}catch{ui.toast('We could not copy the link. Please copy it from the address bar.','error');}}
async function submitReport(){reportErr.value=report.value.reason.trim().length<5?'Tell us what needs checking (at least 5 characters).':'';if(reportErr.value)return;try{await post('/restaurants/'+r.value._id+'/reports',report.value);reporting.value=false;ui.toast('Report sent for review.');}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}
async function submitSuggestion(){suggestErr.value=suggestion.value.proposedValue.trim()?'':'Enter the correct value.';if(suggestErr.value)return;try{await post('/restaurants/'+r.value._id+'/suggestions',suggestion.value);suggesting.value=false;ui.toast('Suggested edit sent.');}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}
async function sendOwner(){ownerErr.value=ownerMessage.value.trim().length<10?'Please write at least 10 characters.':'';if(ownerErr.value)return;try{await post('/restaurants/'+r.value._id+'/contact-owner',{message:ownerMessage.value});ownerMessage.value='';ui.toast('Message sent to the owner.');}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}
async function upload(e){const file=e.target.files[0];if(!file)return;const fd=new FormData();fd.append('image',file);fd.append('alt','Photo supplied for '+r.value.name);try{await api.post('/restaurants/'+r.value._id+'/images',fd);await load();ui.toast('Image uploaded.');}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}
watch(()=>route.params.id,load);onMounted(load);function print(){window.print();}
const lightboxDialog=ref(null);let lightboxFocus;watch(lightbox,async value=>{if(value){lightboxFocus=document.activeElement;await nextTick();lightboxDialog.value?.showModal();}else lightboxFocus?.focus();});
</script>
<template><div class="container section"><p v-if="error" class="error-box" role="alert">{{error}}</p><div v-else-if="!r" class="skeleton" style="min-height:420px"></div><template v-else><nav class="bread" aria-label="Breadcrumb"><RouterLink to="/">Home</RouterLink> / <RouterLink to="/restaurants">Restaurants</RouterLink> / {{r.name}}</nav><div class="page-head row between"><div><p class="eyebrow muted">{{r.country}} · {{r.cuisine}}</p><h1>{{r.name}}</h1><p>{{r.profile.tagline}}</p><div class="row"><span class="badge">{{r.borough}}</span><span class="badge">{{r.profile.priceRange||'$'.repeat(r.priceLevel)}}</span><span v-if="r.isClaimed" class="badge">Claimed listing</span><span v-if="r.openNow!==null" class="badge">{{r.openNow?'Open now':'Closed now'}}</span></div></div><div class="row no-print"><button @click="saved.toggle(r._id)" class="secondary heart-btn" :class="{saved:saved.saved.includes(r._id)}" :aria-pressed="saved.saved.includes(r._id)"><Icon name="heart" :size="18" :fill="saved.saved.includes(r._id)"/>{{saved.saved.includes(r._id)?'Saved':'Save'}}</button><button @click="saved.comparison(r._id)" class="secondary toggle" :class="{active:saved.compare.includes(r._id)}" :aria-pressed="saved.compare.includes(r._id)"><Icon name="compare" :size="18"/>{{saved.compare.includes(r._id)?'In comparison':'Compare'}}</button><button @click="share" class="secondary"><Icon name="share" :size="18"/>Share</button><RouterLink v-if="canEdit" :to="'/restaurants/'+r._id+'/edit'" class="btn">Edit</RouterLink><button v-if="canEdit" class="cta" @click="deleting=true">Delete</button></div></div><p v-if="r.status!=='open'" class="notice">This restaurant is {{r.status.replaceAll('_',' ')}}.</p><p v-if="r.approvalStatus!=='approved'" class="notice">Approval status: {{r.approvalStatus}}. This listing is not yet public.</p><div class="split"><div class="stack"><div class="detail-hero"><img :src="restaurantImage(r,imageUrl)" :alt="r.cuisine+' dining inspiration for '+r.name" class="profile-cover" @error="$event.target.src=dishImage(r.cuisine)"><div class="hero-meta"><span class="badge"><Icon name="pin" :size="14"/>{{r.borough}}</span><span class="badge"><Icon name="dollar" :size="14"/>{{r.profile.priceRange||'$'.repeat(r.priceLevel)}}</span><StarRating v-if="r.reviewCount" :value="r.avgRating||0" :count="r.reviewCount" class="on-photo"/></div></div><section class="card pad"><h2>The story behind {{r.name}}</h2><p>{{r.profile.story}}</p><h3>Order these first</h3><ul class="list"><li v-for="dish in r.profile.signatureDishes" :key="dish">{{dish}}</li></ul><h3>Did you know?</h3><p>{{r.profile.funFact||'Ask the staff about the story behind the house specialty.'}}</p></section><section v-if="r.gallery?.length" class="card pad"><h2>Gallery</h2><div class="grid"><button v-for="image in r.gallery" :key="image.url" class="secondary" @click="lightbox=imageUrl(image.url)"><img :src="imageUrl(image.url)" :alt="image.alt" loading="lazy"></button></div></section><section v-if="r.menuPreview?.length" class="card pad"><h2>Menu preview</h2><p v-for="m in r.menuPreview" :key="m.name">{{m.name}} <b>{{m.price}}</b> · {{m.description}}</p><p class="small muted">Sample prices only. This is not an official rating.</p></section><section class="card pad"><div class="row between"><h2>What diners say</h2><select v-model="sort" aria-label="Sort reviews" style="width:160px" @change="loadReviews"><option value="newest">Newest</option><option value="highest">Highest rating</option><option value="lowest">Lowest rating</option></select></div><div v-for="stars in [5,4,3,2,1]" :key="stars" class="row small" style="margin-bottom:8px"><span>{{stars}} ★</span><div class="bar" :style="{width:(reviews.filter(v=>v.rating===stars).length/Math.max(1,reviews.length)*70)+'%'}"></div><span>{{reviews.filter(v=>v.rating===stars).length}}</span></div><article v-for="(v,vi) in reviews" :key="v._id" class="review" :style="{'--i':vi}"><b>{{v.author}} · {{'★'.repeat(v.rating)}}</b><p>{{v.title}}</p><p>{{v.comment}}</p><p v-if="v.ownerReply?.text" class="notice">Owner reply: {{v.ownerReply.text}}</p><div v-if="auth.user" class="row no-print"><button class="secondary small" @click="reviewAction(v,'helpful')">Helpful ({{v.helpfulUsers?.length||0}})</button><button class="secondary small" @click="reviewAction(v,'report')">Report</button><button v-if="auth.user._id===v.user" class="secondary small" @click="editing=v._id;review={rating:v.rating,title:v.title,comment:v.comment}">Edit review</button><button v-if="auth.user._id===v.user||auth.staff" class="secondary small" @click="reviewAction(v,'delete')">Delete review</button><button v-if="canEdit" class="secondary small" @click="reviewAction(v,'reply')">Reply</button></div></article><p v-if="!reviews.length">No reviews yet. :key="n" :value="n">{{n}} stars</option></select></div><div><label for="review-comment">Your comment</label><textarea id="review-comment" v-model="review.comment" maxlength="300" methods',r.paymentMethods?.join(', ')]]" :key="label"><dt class="small muted">{{label}}</dt><dd>{{value||'Please confirm with the restaurant.'}}</dd></template></dl><div class="row"><span v-for="x in r.dietary" :key="x" class="badge">{{x}}</span></div><p class="small muted">Last updated: {{r.updatedAt?.slice(0,10)}}</p></section><section class="card pad"><h2>Find it here</h2><p>{{address}}</p><div client/src/views/RestaurantFormView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,onMounted,watch,nextTick,onUnmounted} from 'vue';import {useRoute,useRouter} from 'vue-router';import ConfirmDialog from '../components/ConfirmDialog.vue';import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';import {useUnsavedGuard} from '../composables/useUnsavedGuard.js';import {get,post,put,message} from '../services/api.js';import {useUi,useAuth} from '../stores/index.js';
const route=useRoute(),router=useRouter(),ui=useUi(),auth=useAuth(),edit=computed(()=>!!route.params.id),countries=ref([]),busy=ref(false),loading=ref(false),error=ref(''),errors=ref({}),dirty=ref(false),done=ref(false);const b=ref({name:'',cuisine:'',country:'',borough:'',city:'',address:{building:'',street:'',zipcode:''},grades:[],isFeatured:false,featuredStart:null,featuredEnd:null,profile:{tagline:'',story:'',signatureDishes:[],priceRange:'$$',openingHours:'',dressCode:'',reservations:'',dietaryOptions:[],tags:[],funFact:'',imageUrl:'',website:'',phone:'',email:''},priceLevel:2,status:'open',timezone:'Asia/Manila',hours:[],dietary:[],allergens:[],amenities:[],occasions:[],paymentMethods:[],social:{},menuPreview:[],gallery:[]});const imageFailed=ref(false);const lng=ref(''),lat=ref(''),dishes=ref(''),tags=ref(''),dietaryText=ref('');const hasDraft=ref(false),shake=ref(false);const draftKey='bonappefind_draft_'+(route.params.id||'new');let timer;
watch([b,lng,lat,dishes,tags,dietaryText],()=>{if(!loading.value){dirty.value=true;clearTimeout(timer);timer=setTimeout(()=>localStorage.setItem(draftKey,JSON.stringify({...b.value,address:{...b.value.address,...(lng.value!==''&&lat.value!==''?{coord:[Number(lng.value),Number(lat.value)]}:{coord:undefined})},profile:{...b.value.profile,signatureDishes:arr(dishes.value),tags:arr(tags.value),dietaryOptions:arr(dietaryText.value)}})),500);}},{deep:true});
function validate(){const e={},v=b.value;if(!v.name.trim())e.name='Enter the restaurant name.';if(!v.cuisine.trim())e.cuisine='Enter a cuisine, for example Filipino.';if(!v.borough.trim())e.borough='Enter the borough or region.';if(!v.country)e.country='Choose a country.';if(!v.address.street.trim())e['address.street']='Enter the street name.';
 if((lng.value==='')!==(lat.value===''))e['address.coord']='Enter both longitude and latitude, or leave both empty.';else if(lng.value!==''&&(!Number.isFinite(Number(lng.value))||Math.abs(Number(lng.value))>180||!Number.isFinite(Number(lat.value))||Math.abs(Number(lat.value))>90))e['address.coord']='Longitude must be −180 to 180; latitude must be −90 to 90.';
 v.grades.forEach((g,i)=>{if(!g.date)e['grades.'+i+'.date']='Choose a date.';if(g.score===''||g.score===null||!Number.isFinite(Number(g.score))||Number(g.score)<0||Number(g.score)>200)e['grades.'+i+'.score']='Score must be 0 to 200.';});
 for(const k of ['website','imageUrl']){const u=v.profile[k];if(u){try{if(k==='imageUrl'&&/^\/images\/(?:photos\/)?[a-z0-9-]+\.(?:svg|jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/.test(u))continue;if(!/^https?:$/.test(new URL(u).protocol))throw 0;}catch{e['profile.'+k]='Enter a full link starting with https://';}}}
 if(v.profile.email&&!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(v.profile.email))e['profile.email']='Enter an email address like name@example.com.';
 for(const k of ['facebook','instagram','tiktok','x']){const u=v.social[k];if(u){try{if(!/^https?:$/.test(new URL(u).protocol))throw 0;}catch{e['social.'+k]='Enter a full link starting with https://';}}}
 try{new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en',{timeZone:v.timezone});}catch{e.timezone='Choose a valid time zone.';}
 const used=new Set();v.hours.forEach((h,i)=>{if(used.has(h.day))e['hours.'+i]='This weekday already has a row. Use one row per day.';used.add(h.day);if(!h.closed&&(!/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(h.open)||!/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(h.close)))e['hours.'+i]='Choose opening and closing times, or mark this day closed.';});
 v.menuPreview.forEach((item,i)=>{if(!item.name?.trim())e['menuPreview.'+i+'.name']='Enter a dish name.';if(item.price===''||item.price==null||!Number.isFinite(Number(item.price))||Number(item.price)<0)e['menuPreview.'+i+'.price']='Enter a price of zero or more.';});
 if(v.isFeatured&&v.featuredStart&&v.featuredEnd&&v.featuredEnd<v.featuredStart)e.featuredEnd='End date must be on or after the start date.';
 return e;}
watch([b,lng,lat],()=>{if(Object.keys(errors.value).length){const now=validate();errors.value=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(errors.value).filter(([k])=>now[k]).map(([k])=>[k,now[k]]));}},{deep:true});
const guard=useUnsavedGuard(()=>dirty.value&&!done.value);
function restoreDraft(){try{b.value=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(draftKey));if(b.value.address.coord)[lng.value,lat.value]=b.value.address.coord;else{lng.value='';lat.value='';}dishes.value=b.value.profile.signatureDishes?.join(', ')||'';tags.value=b.value.profile.tags?.join(', ')||'';dietaryText.value=b.value.profile.dietaryOptions?.join(', ')||'';ui.toast('Draft restored.');}catch{}hasDraft.value=false;}function discardDraft(){localStorage.removeItem(draftKey);hasDraft.value=false;}
function arr(s){return s.split(',').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);}async function submit(){if(busy.value)return;error.value='';const found=validate();errors.value=found;if(Object.keys(found).length){shake.value=false;await nextTick();shake.value=true;setTimeout(()=>shake.value=false,500);const first=Object.keys(found)[0];const id=first.startsWith('grades.')?'grade-'+first.split('.')[2]+'-'+first.split('.')[1]:first.startsWith('hours.')?'hours-day-'+first.split('.')[1]:first.startsWith('menuPreview.')?'menu-'+first.split('.')[2]+'-'+first.split('.')[1]:{name:'restaurant-name',cuisine:'restaurant-cuisine',borough:'restaurant-borough',country:'restaurant-country','address.street':'address-street','address.coord':'longitude'}[first]||('profile-'+first.split('.').pop());(document.getElementById(id)||document.querySelector('[aria-invalid=true]'))?.focus();error.value='Please fix the highlighted fields and try again.';return;}busy.value=true;try{const body=structuredClone(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(b.value)));body.profile.signatureDishes=arr(dishes.value);body.profile.tags=arr(tags.value);body.profile.dietaryOptions=arr(dietaryText.value);body.profile.priceRange='$'.repeat(Number(body.priceLevel));if(lng.value!==''||lat.value!=='')body.address.coord=[Number(lng.value),Number(lat.value)];else delete body.address.coord;for(const g of body.grades)g.score=Number(g.score);body.menuPreview=body.menuPreview.map(item=>({...item,name:item.name.trim(),price:Number(item.price)}));const r=edit.value?await put('/restaurants/'+route.params.id,body):await post('/restaurants',body);done.value=true;dirty.value=false;clearTimeout(timer);localStorage.removeItem(draftKey);ui.toast(edit.value?'Changes saved.':r.data.approvalStatus==='pending'?'Restaurant saved. It is awaiting approval.':'Restaurant added. It is now saved in the database.');router.push('/restaurants/'+r.data._id);}catch(e){error.value=message(e);for(const x of e.response?.data?.errors||[])errors.value[x.field]=x.message;}finally{busy.value=false;}}
function addGrade(){b.value.grades.push({date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),grade:'A',score:0});}function addHours(){const day=[1,2,3,4,5,6,0].find(day=>!b.value.hours.some(h=>h.day===day));if(day===undefined)return;b.value.hours.push({day,open:'10:00',close:'21:00',closed:false});}
onUnmounted(()=>clearTimeout(timer));watch(()=>b.value.profile.imageUrl,()=>imageFailed.value=false);
onMounted(async()=>{loading.value=true;try{countries.value=(await get('/countries')).data;if(edit.value){const data=(await get('/restaurants/'+route.params.id)).data;b.value={...b.value,...data,profile:{...b.value.profile,...data.profile},address:{...b.value.address,...data.address},social:data.social||{}};for(const key of ['featuredStart','featuredEnd'])b.value[key]=b.value[key]?.slice(0,10)||null;b.value.grades=b.value.grades.map(g=>({...g,date:g.date?.slice(0,10)}));}else if(localStorage.getItem(draftKey))hasDraft.value=true;if(b.value.address.coord){[lng.value,lat.value]=b.value.address.coord;}dishes.value=b.value.profile.signatureDishes?.join(', ')||'';tags.value=b.value.profile.tags?.join(', ')||'';dietaryText.value=b.value.profile.dietaryOptions?.join(', ')||'';}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{loading.value=false;dirty.value=false;}});
const basic=[['name','Restaurant name *','Kusina ni Lola Pacing'],['cuisine','Cuisine *','Filipino'],['borough','Borough or region *','Pampanga'],['city','City','San Fernando']];const profile=[['tagline','Tagline',80],['story','Story',600],['openingHours','Opening hours',200],['dressCode','Dress code',100],['reservations','Reservations',200],['funFact','Fun fact',200],['imageUrl','Image link',1000],['website','Website',1000],['phone','Phone',100],['email','Restaurant email',150]];
</script>
<template><div class="container section restaurant-form"><div class="page-head"><h1>{{edit?'Edit '+b.name:'Add a restaurant'}}</h1><p>Share a place worth eating at. Fields marked with an asterisk are required.</p><p v-if="!auth.user" class="muted">Please <RouterLink to="/login">log in</RouterLink> as an owner or administrator to save a restaurant.</p></div><p v-if="error" role="alert" class="form-status error">{{error}}</p><div v-if="loading" class="skeleton" aria-busy="true" style="min-height:320px"></div><div v-if="hasDraft&&!loading" class="form-status success draft-bar" role="status"><span>You have an unsaved draft of this form.</span><span class="row"><button type="button" class="small" @click="restoreDraft">Restore draft</button><button type="button" class="secondary small" @click="discardDraft">Discard</button></span></div><form v-if="!loading" novalidate @submit.prevent="submit" class="stack" :class="{shake}" :aria-busy="busy"><fieldset :disabled="busy" class="form-fields"><section class="card pad"><h2>The basics</h2><div class="form-grid"><div v-for="[key,label,placeholder] in basic" :key="key"><label :for="'restaurant-'+key">{{label}}</label><input :id="'restaurant-'+key" v-model="b[key]" :placeholder="placeholder" :maxlength="key==='name'?120:150" :aria-invalid="!!errors[key]"><p v-if="errors[key]" class="field-msg">{{errors[key]}}</p></div><div><label for="restaurant-country">Country *</label><select id="restaurant-country" v-model="b.country" :aria-invalid="!!errors.country"><option value="">Choose a country</option><option v-for="c in countries" :key="c.slug">{{c.name}}</option><option v-if="b.country&&!countries.some(c=>c.name===b.country)">{{b.country}}</option></select><p class="field-msg">{{errors.country}}</p></div><div><label for="restaurant-status">Status</label><select id="restaurant-status" v-model="b.status"><option value="open">Open</option><option value="temporarily_closed">Temporarily Closed</option><option value="permanently_closed">Permanently Closed</option></select></div></div></section><section class="card pad"><h2>Address</h2><div class="form-grid"><div v-for="[key,label] in [['building','Building number'],['street','Street *'],['zipcode','Zip or postal code']]" :key="key"><label :for="'address-'+key">{{label}}</label><input :id="'address-'+key" v-model="b.address[key]" :aria-invalid="!!errors['address.'+key]"><p class="field-msg">{{errors['address.'+key]}}</p></div><div><label for="longitude">Longitude</label><input id="longitude" v-model="lng" :aria-invalid="!!errors['address.coord']" type="number" max="90"></div></div><p class="field-msg">{{errors['address.coord']}}</p></section><section class="card pad"><div class="row between"><h2>Grades and scores</h2><button type="button" class="secondary" @click="addGrade">Add a grade</button></div><div v-for="(g,i) in b.grades" :key="i" class="grade-row"><div style="flex:1"><label :for="'grade-date-'+i">Date</label><input :id="'grade-date-'+i" v-model="g.date" type="date" :aria-invalid="!!errors['grades.'+i+'.date']"><p class="field-msg">{{errors['grades.'+i+'.date']}}</p></div><div style="width:90px"><label :for="'grade-letter-'+i">Grade</label><select :id="'grade-letter-'+i" v-model="g.grade"><option>A</option><option>B</option><option>C</option><option>Z</option></select></div><div style="width:100px"><label :for="'grade-score-'+i">Score</label><input :id="'grade-score-'+i" v-model="g.score" type="number" min="0" max="200" :aria-invalid="!!errors['grades.'+i+'.score']"><p class="field-msg">{{errors['grades.'+i+'.score']}}</p></div><button type="button" class="secondary" @click="b.grades.splice(i,1)">Remove</button></div></section><section class="card pad"><h2>Profile and story</h2><div v-for="[key,label,max] in profile" :key="key" class="field"><label :for="'profile-'+key">{{label}}</label><textarea v-if="key==='story'" :id="'profile-'+key" v-model="b.profile[key]" :maxlength="max"></textarea><input v-else :type="key==='email'?'email':key==='phone'?'tel':'text'" :inputmode="['website','imageUrl'].includes(key)?'url':undefined" :placeholder="key==='openingHours'?'Mon–Sat, 10:00 AM–9:00 PM':key==='website'?'https://example.com':undefined" :id="'profile-'+key" v-model="b.profile[key]" :maxlength="max" :aria-invalid="!!errors['profile.'+key]"><p class="field-msg">{{errors['profile.'+key]}}</p></div><figure v-if="b.profile.imageUrl&&!imageFailed" class="image-preview"><img :src="b.profile.imageUrl" alt="Restaurant image preview" @error="imageFailed=true"><figcaption>Image preview</figcaption></figure><p v-if="imageFailed" class="field-msg" role="status">This image could not be loaded. Check the image link.</p><div class="field"><label for="signature-dishes">Signature dishes (separate with commas)</label><input id="signature-dishes" v-model="dishes"></div><div class="field"><label for="profile-tags">Tags (separate with commas)</label><input id="profile-tags" v-model="tags"></div><div class="field"><label for="dietary-text">Dietary information (separate with commas)</label><input id="dietary-text" v-model="dietaryText"></div><label for="price-level">Price range</label><select id="price-level" v-model.number="b.priceLevel"><option v-for="n in 4" :key="n" :value="n">{{'$'.repeat(n)}}</option></select></section><section class="card pad"><h2>Extra details</h2><div v-if="auth.staff" class="field"><label><input v-model="b.isFeatured" type="checkbox"> Feature on the home page</label><label for="featured-start">Featured start (optional)</label><input id="featured-start" v-model="b.featuredStart" type="date"><label for="featured-end">Featured end (optional)</label><input id="featured-end" v-model="b.featuredEnd" type="date" :min="b.featuredStart||undefined" :aria-invalid="!!errors.featuredEnd"><p v-if="errors.featuredEnd" class="field-msg">{{errors.featuredEnd}}</p></div><div class="field"><label for="timezone">Time zone (IANA name)</label><select id="timezone" v-model="b.timezone" :aria-invalid="!!errors.timezone"><option v-for="zone in ['Asia/Manila','Asia/Singapore','Asia/Tokyo','America/New_York','America/Los_Angeles','Europe/London','UTC']" :key="zone" :value="zone">{{zone.replaceAll('_',' ')}}</option><option v-if="b.timezone&&!['Asia/Manila','Asia/Singapore','Asia/Tokyo','America/New_York','America/Los_Angeles','Europe/London','UTC'].includes(b.timezone)" :value="b.timezone">{{b.timezone}}</option></select><p class="field-msg">{{errors.timezone}}</p></div><div class="row between"><h3>Weekly opening hours</h3><button type="button" class="secondary" :disabled="b.hours.length>=7" @click="addHours">Add hours</button></div><p class="small muted">Add one row per day. Mark days closed to disable time fields. A closing time earlier than the opening time means the restaurant closes the next day.</p><div v-for="(h,i) in b.hours" :key="i" class="hours-row"><div class="field"><label :for="'hours-day-'+i">Day</label><select :id="'hours-day-'+i" v-model.number="h.day" :aria-invalid="!!errors['hours.'+i]"><option v-for="(day,n) in ['Sunday','Monday','Tuesday','Wednesday','Thursday','Friday','Saturday']" :key="day" :value="n" :disabled="b.hours.some((row,index)=>index!==i&&row.day===n)">{{day}}</option></select></div><div class="field"><label :for="'hours-open-'+i">Opens</label><input :id="'hours-open-'+i" v-model="h.open" type="time" :disabled="h.closed"></div><div class="field"><label :for="'hours-close-'+i">Closes</label><input :id="'hours-close-'+i" v-model="h.close" type="time" :disabled="h.closed"></div><label class="check-option"><input v-model="h.closed" type="checkbox"> Closed</label><button type="button" class="secondary" :aria-label="'Remove hours row '+(i+1)" @click="b.hours.splice(i,1)">Remove</button><p v-if="errors['hours.'+i]" class="field-msg row-error">{{errors['hours.'+i]}}</p></div><h3>Dietary tags</h3><div class="option-grid"><label class="check-option" v-for="x in ['Halal','Vegan','Vegetarian','Gluten-Free','Dairy-Free','Nut-Free','Pescatarian','Kosher']" :key="x"><input v-model="b.dietary" type="checkbox" :value="x"> {{x}}</label></div><h3>Amenities</h3><div class="option-grid"><label class="check-option" v-for="x in ['Wi-Fi','Parking','Outdoor seating','Kid-friendly','Pet-friendly','Air-conditioned','Private room']" :key="x"><input v-model="b.amenities" type="checkbox" :value="x"> {{x}}</label></div><h3>Payment methods</h3><div class="option-grid"><label class="check-option" v-for="x in ['Cash','Card','E-wallet']" :key="x"><input v-model="b.paymentMethods" type="checkbox" :value="x"> {{x}}</label></div><h3>Social links</h3><div v-for="key in ['facebook','instagram','tiktok','x']" :key="key" class="field"><label :for="'social-'+key">{{key}}</label><input :id="'social-'+key" v-model="b.social[key]" type="text" inputmode="url" :aria-invalid="!!errors['social.'+key]"><p v-if="errors['menuPreview.'+i+'.name']" class="field-msg">{{errors['menuPreview.'+i+'.name']}}</p></div><div class="field"><label :for="'menu-price-'+i">Price (local currency)</label><input :id="'menu-price-'+i" v-model="item.price" type="number" min="0" step=".01" placeholder="0.00" :aria-invalid="!!errors['menuPreview.'+i+'.price']"><p v-if="errors['menuPreview.'+i+'.price']" class="field-msg">{{errors['menuPreview.'+i+'.price']}}</p></div><div class="field"><label :for="'menu-description-'+i">Description (optional)</label><input :id="'menu-description-'+i" @click="b.menuPreview.splice(i,1)">Remove</button></div><button type="button" class="secondary" @click="b.menuPreview.push({name:'',price:0,description:''})">Add menu item</button></section><div class="row"><button class="cta" :class="{'is-loading':busy}" :disabled="busy||loading"><span>{{edit?'Save changes':'Save restaurant'}}</span></button><RouterLink class="btn secondary" to="/restaurants">Cancel</RouterLink></div></fieldset></form><ConfirmDialog v-if="guard.pending.value" title="Leave without saving?" text="You have unsaved changes on this form.
