client/src/views/RestaurantsView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,computed,watch,onMounted,onUnmounted,nextTick} from 'vue';import {useRoute,useRouter} from 'vue-router';import {get,del,post,message} from '../services/api.js';import {useAuth,useSaved,useUi} from '../stores/index.js';import RestaurantCard from '../components/restaurants/RestaurantCard.vue';import ConfirmDialog from '../components/ConfirmDialog.vue';import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';import Pagination from '../components/common/Pagination.vue';import EmptyState from '../components/common/EmptyState.vue';import GradeBadge from '../components/restaurants/GradeBadge.vue';import L from 'leaflet';import 'leaflet/dist/leaflet.css';
const route=useRoute(),router=useRouter(),auth=useAuth(),saved=useSaved(),ui=useUi(),data=ref([]),filters=ref({boroughs:[],cuisines:[],countries:[]}),meta=ref({}),busy=ref(false),loaded=ref(false),advOpen=ref(false),flash=ref(false),error=ref(''),deleting=ref(null),suggestions=ref([]);const q=ref({search:'',field:'all',borough:'',country:'',cuisine:'',price:'',dietary:'',minRating:'',grade:'',open:'',sort:'name',limit:20,page:1,view:'grid'});let map,timer,request=0;const mapHost=ref(null);
const DEFAULTS={limit:20,page:1,sort:'name',view:'grid',field:'all'};const LABELS={borough:'Region',country:'Country',cuisine:'Cuisine',price:'Price',dietary:'Dietary',minRating:'Min rating',grade:'Grade',open:'Hours',search:'Search'};const ADV=['country','cuisine','price','dietary','minRating','grade','open'];
function fromUrl(){for(const k of Object.keys(q.value)){if(k==='search'&&document.activeElement?.id==='list-search'&&q.value.search===(route.query.search??''))continue;if(k==='search'&&document.activeElement?.id==='list-search')continue;q.value[k]=route.query[k]??(DEFAULTS[k]??'');}}async function load(){const current=++request;busy.value=true;error.value='';try{const params={...q.value};const [r,choices]=await Promise.all([get('/restaurants',params),get('/restaurants/meta/filters',params)]);if(current!==request)return;filters.value=choices.data;data.value=r.data;meta.value=r.meta;loaded.value=true;flash.value=false;await nextTick();flash.value=true;if(current===request)busy.value=false;await nextTick();if(q.value.view==='map')drawMap();}catch(e){if(current===request)error.value=message(e);}finally{if(current===request)busy.value=false;}}
function apply(page=1){q.value.page=page;const query=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(q.value).filter(([,v])=>v!==''));router.push({path:'/restaurants',query});if(q.value.search){const history=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('bonappefind_searches')||'[]');localStorage.setItem('bonappefind_searches',JSON.stringify([q.value.search,...history.filter(x=>x!==q.value.search)].slice(0,8)));if(auth.user)post('/me/search-history',{query:q.value.search,filters:query}).catch(()=>{});}}
function clear(){q.value.search='';router.push('/restaurants');}function dropFilter(k){q.value[k]='';apply();}const activeList=computed(()=>[...(q.value.search?['search']:[]),...['borough',...ADV].filter(k=>q.value[k])]);const advCount=computed(()=>ADV.filter(k=>q.value[k]).length);const popularCuisines=computed(()=>(filters.value.cuisines||[]).slice(0,5));function chipText(k){return k==='price'?'$'.repeat(Number(q.value[k])):k==='minRating'?q.value[k]+'+ stars':q.value[k];}function available(key,value){return filters.value.availability?.[key]?.[value];}function borough(b){q.value.borough=q.value.borough===b?'':b;apply();}async function remove(){const r=deleting.value;try{await del('/restaurants/'+r._id);deleting.value=null;ui.toast('Restaurant deleted.','success',auth.user?.role==='admin'?async()=>{try{await post('/restaurants/'+r._id+'/restore');ui.toast('Restaurant restored.');await load();}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}:undefined);await load();}catch(e){ui.toast(message(e),'error');}}
function drawMap(){map?.remove();map=null;if(!mapHost.value)return;map=L.map(mapHost.value).setView([20,0],2);L.tileLayer('https://tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',{attribution:'© OpenStreetMap contributors',maxZoom:19}).addTo(map);const bounds=[];for(const r of data.value){const c=r.address?.coord;if(c?.length!==2)continue;const p=[c[1],c[0]];bounds.push(p);const label=document.createElement('a');label.href='/restaurants/'+r._id;label.textContent=r.name;L.circleMarker(p,{radius:9,color:'#7e2625',fillOpacity:.8}).addTo(map).bindPopup(label);}if(bounds.length)map.fitBounds(bounds,{padding:[40,40],maxZoom:13});}
watch(()=>route.fullPath,()=>{fromUrl();load();});let live;watch(()=>q.value.search,(n)=>{clearTimeout(live);if(n!==(route.query.search??''))live=setTimeout(()=>{if(q.value.search.length===0||q.value.search.length>=2)apply();},450);clearTimeout(timer);timer=setTimeout(async()=>{if(q.value.search.length<2){suggestions.value=[];return;}try{suggestions.value=(await get('/restaurants/suggest',{...q.value,q:q.value.search})).data;}catch{}},250);});onMounted(async()=>{fromUrl();advOpen.value=ADV.some(k=>q.value[k]);try{filters.value=(await get('/restaurants/meta/filters')).data;}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}await load();});onUnmounted(()=>{map?.remove();clearTimeout(timer);clearTimeout(live);});
</script>
<template>
<div class="container section">
  <div class="page-head row between"><div><p class="eyebrow muted">Find your next table</p><h1>Restaurants</h1><p class="muted">Search by name or cuisine. Filter by region, country, price and more. Results update as you type.</p></div><RouterLink v-if="!auth.user||auth.user.role==='customer'" to="/suggest-restaurant" class="btn secondary small">Suggest a Restaurant</RouterLink><RouterLink v-if="auth.canManage" to="/restaurants/new" class="btn cta"><Icon name="plus" :size="18"/>Add a Restaurant</RouterLink></div>

  <form class="card filter-card" role="search" aria-label="Search and filter restaurants" @submit.prevent="apply()">
    <div class="filter-main">
      <div class="field"><label for="list-search">Search a restaurant or cuisine</label>
        <div class="search-wrap"><input id="list-search" v-model="q.search" type="search" placeholder="Try Bakery, Sushi or Green Mango" list="search-suggestions" autocomplete="off"><button v-if="q.search" type="button" class="clear-x" aria-label="Clear search" @click="q.search='';apply()"><Icon name="x" :size="16"/></button></div>
        <datalist id="search-suggestions"><option v-for="s in suggestions" :key="s._id" :value="s.name"/></datalist></div>
      <div class="field"><label for="list-field">Search in</label><select id="list-field" v-model="q.field" @change="q.search&&apply()"><option value="all">All fields</option><option value="name">Name</option><option value="cuisine">Cuisine</option><option value="dish">Dish name</option></select></div>
    </div>
    <button type="button" class="adv-toggle" :aria-expanded="advOpen" aria-controls="adv-panel" @click="advOpen=!advOpen"><Icon name="sliders" :size="18"/>More filters<span v-if="advCount" class="count-pill" :key="advCount">{{advCount}}</span><Icon name="chevron-down" :size="16"/></button>
    <div id="adv-panel" class="collapse" :class="{open:advOpen}" :inert="!advOpen"><div><div class="adv-grid">
      <div v-for="[key,label,options] in [['country','Country',filters.countries],['cuisine','Cuisine',filters.cuisines],['price','Price range',['1','2','3','4']],['dietary','Dietary options',['Halal','Vegan','Vegetarian','Gluten-Free']],['minRating','Minimum review rating',['3','4','5']],['grade','Inspection grade',['A','B','C','Z']]]" :key="key" class="field"><label :for="'filter-'+key">{{label}}</label><select :id="'filter-'+key" v-model="q[key]" @change="apply()"><option value="">Any</option><option v-for="o in options" :key="o" :value="o" :disabled="available(key,o)===0&&q[key]!==o">{{key==='price'?'$'.repeat(Number(o)):key==='minRating'?o+'+ stars':o}}{{available(key,o)!==undefined?' ('+available(key,o)+')':''}}</option></select></div>
      <div class="field"><label for="sort">Sort by</label><select id="sort" v-model="q.sort" @change="apply()"><option value="name">Name A–Z</option><option value="name-desc">Name Z–A</option><option value="newest">Newest</option><option value="rating">Rating high to low</option><option value="rating-asc">Rating low to high</option><option value="views">Most viewed</option></select></div>
      <div class="field"><label for="open">Opening hours</label><select id="open" v-model="q.open" @change="apply()"><option value="">Any time</option><option value="now" :disabled="available('open','now')===0&&q.open!=='now'">Open now ({{available('open','now')??0}})</option></select></div>
    </div></div></div>
    <div class="filter-actions"><button class="cta" :class="{'is-loading':busy}" :disabled="busy"><Icon name="search" :size="18"/><span>Search</span></button><button type="button" class="secondary" :disabled="!activeList.length&&q.sort==='name'" @click="clear"><Icon name="x" :size="16"/>Reset all</button><span class="live" role="status" aria-live="polite"><span v-if="busy" class="spinner-inline"></span>{{busy?'Updating results…':(meta.total||0)+' matching'}}</span></div>
  </form>

  <div class="borough-row" role="group" aria-label="Filter by borough or region"><button v-for="b in filters.boroughs" :key="b" class="secondary pill toggle small" :class="{active:q.borough===b}" :aria-pressed="q.borough===b" :disabled="available('borough',b)===0&&q.borough!==b" @click="borough(b)"><Icon name="pin" :size="14"/>{{b}} <span class="small">({{available('borough',b)??0}})</span></button></div>

  <div v-if="activeList.length" class="active-filters" aria-label="Active filters"><span class="small muted">Active:</span><TransitionGroup name="chip"><button v-for="k in activeList" :key="k" type="button" class="chip-x" :aria-label="'Remove '+LABELS[k]+' filter '+chipText(k)" @click="k==='search'?(q.search='',apply()):dropFilter(k)"><span>{{LABELS[k]}}: <b>{{chipText(k)}}</b></span><span class="x" aria-hidden="true">×</span></button></TransitionGroup><button type="button" class="link-reset" @click="clear">Clear all</button></div>

  <div class="results-bar"><p class="count" :class="{flash}" role="status" aria-live="polite">{{meta.total||0}} {{meta.total===1?'restaurant':'restaurants'}} <span class="muted small">· Page {{meta.page||1}} of {{meta.pages||1}}</span></p>
    <div class="controls"><div class="seg" role="group" aria-label="Choose a layout"><button v-for="view in ['table','grid','map']" :key="view" class="ghost small toggle view-btn" :class="{active:q.view===view}" :aria-pressed="q.view===view" @click="q.view=view;apply(meta.page||1)"><Icon :name="view" :size="16"/>{{view}}</button></div><label class="sr-only" for="per-page">Restaurants per page</label><select id="per-page" v-model="q.limit" @change="apply()"><option>20</option><option>50</option><option>100</option></select></div></div>

  <div v-if="error" role="alert" class="error-box">{{error}} <button class="small" @click="load">Try again</button></div>
  <div v-else-if="!loaded" class="grid" aria-label="Loading restaurants" aria-busy="true"><div v-for="n in 3" :key="n" class="skeleton" style="min-height:380px"></div></div>
  <div v-else class="results-area" :class="{updating:busy}" :aria-busy="busy">
    <div v-if="q.view==='map'" class="stack"><div ref="mapHost" class="map"></div><p class="muted small">Only restaurants with recorded coordinates appear. Tiles require internet access; fictional seed addresses have no map coordinates.</p></div>
    <div v-else-if="!data.length" class="card empty-results"><span class="empty-icon"><Icon name="search" :size="28"/></span><h2>No restaurants found</h2><p class="muted">Nothing matches {{q.search?'“'+q.search+'”':'these filters'}}{{advCount||q.borough?' with the selected filters':''}}. Try a broader word, or remove a filter.</p><div class="row"><button class="cta" @click="clear">Reset search and filters</button></div><div v-if="popularCuisines.length" class="suggest-list"><span class="small muted">Popular cuisines:</span><RouterLink v-for="c in popularCuisines" :key="c" class="chip-link" :to="{path:'/restaurants',query:{cuisine:c}}">{{c}}</RouterLink></div></div>
    <div v-else-if="q.view==='grid'" class="grid stagger"><RestaurantCard v-for="(r,i) in data" :key="r._id" :restaurant="r" :index="i%3" :style="{'--i':i%9}"/></div>
    <div v-else class="card table-wrap"><table class="restaurant-table"><thead><tr><th>Name</th><th>Borough / Region</th><th>Cuisine</th><th>Address</th><th>Grades</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr v-for="r in data" :key="r._id"><td data-label="Name"><RouterLink :to="'/restaurants/'+r._id"><b>{{r.name}}</b></RouterLink><p class="small muted">{{r.country}}</p></td><td data-label="Region">{{r.borough}}</td><td data-label="Cuisine">{{r.cuisine}}</td><td data-label="Address">{{r.address?.building}} {{r.address?.street}}, {{r.address?.zipcode}}</td><td data-label="Grades"><GradeBadge v-for="(g,i) in r.grades" :key="i" :grade="g.grade" :score="g.score"/><p class="small">Quality {{r.totalScore}}</p></td><td data-label="Actions"><div class="row"><button class="secondary small heart-btn" :class="{saved:saved.saved.includes(r._id)}" :aria-pressed="saved.saved.includes(r._id)" @click="saved.toggle(r._id)" :aria-label="(saved.saved.includes(r._id)?'Remove ':'Save ')+r.name"><Icon name="heart" :size="16" :fill="saved.saved.includes(r._id)"/></button><RouterLink v-if="auth.canManage&&(auth.user?.role==='admin'||auth.user?._id===r.ownerId)" :to="'/restaurants/'+r._id+'/edit'" class="btn secondary small">Edit</RouterLink><button v-if="auth.canManage&&(auth.user?.role==='admin'||auth.user?._id===r.ownerId)" class="cta small" @click="deleting=r">Delete</button></div></td></tr></tbody></table></div>
  </div>
  <Pagination :meta="meta" :busy="busy" @page="apply"/>
  <ConfirmDialog v-if="deleting" title="Delete this restaurant?" :text="deleting.name+' will be moved to Trash. An administrator can restore it.'" confirm-label="Delete" @cancel="deleting=null" @confirm="remove"/>
</div>
</template>

<script setup>
import {computed} from 'vue';import {useRoute} from 'vue-router';import source from '../content/blueprint-pages.json';import team from '../content/team.json';import photos from '../content/photo-sources.json';

const sections=computed(()=>{const result=[];let current={title:'Overview',lines:[]};for(const line of blocks.value){if((/^\d+\. |^(Our story|Why the name\?|Our mission|Our vision|Our values|Data|What |Your choices|Known limitations|Tell us)/.test(line))&&line.length<100){if(current.lines.length)result.push(current);current={title:line,lines:[]};}else current.lines.push(line);}if(current.lines.length)result.push(current);return result;});
function contentParts(lines){const parts=[];for(const line of lines){if(line.includes(' | ')){const last=parts[parts.length-1];if(last?.type==='table')last.rows.push(line.split(' | '));else parts.push({type:'table',rows:[line.split(' | ')]});}else parts.push({type:'paragraph',text:line});}return parts;}
const features=[['Search and filter','Search by restaurant name, cuisine or dish and narrow by region, country, price, dietary tags and review rating.'],['Best Quality','MongoDB adds recorded grade scores and ranks totals greater than 90.'],['Country guides','Eight guides explain signature dishes, meals, etiquette, tipping, festivals and useful phrases.'],['Owner tools','Owners add listings, claim restaurants and edit their own profiles. client/src/views/SuggestionView.vue

<script setup>
import {ref,onMounted,onUnmounted,watch,computed,nextTick} from 'vue';
import {useRoute,useRouter} from 'vue-router';
import {get,post,patch,message,api,imageUrl} from '../services/api.js';
import {useAuth,useUi} from '../stores/index.js';
import Icon from '../components/common/Icon.vue';
import DashShell from '../components/layout/DashShell.vue';
const auth=useAuth(),ui=useUi(),route=useRoute(),router=useRouter();
const admin=computed(()=>route.path.startsWith('/admin')),canSuggest=computed(()=>auth.user?.role==='customer'&&auth.user.emailVerified),historyOnly=computed(()=>route.path==='/account/suggestions');
const fields=[['name','Restaurant name','e.g. Kusina sa Kanto'],['cuisine','Cuisine','e.g. Filipino, Japanese or Italian'],['address','Complete address','Building, street, city, region and country'],['contact','Contact details','Phone number or email address'],['website','Website (optional)','https://example.com'],['openingHours','Opening hours','e.g. Mon–Sat, 10:00 AM–9:00 PM'],['description','Why recommend this restaurant?','Describe the food, atmosphere or what makes it worth visiting.'],['photo','Photo link (optional)','/images/photos/dining.jpg']];
const blank=()=>Object.fromEntries(fields.map(([key])=>[key,'']));
const form=ref(blank()),rows=ref([]),summary=ref({}),busy=ref(false),uploading=ref(false),error=ref(''),status=ref(''),errors=ref({}),reviewErrors=ref({}),refreshing=ref(false),loaded=ref(false),statusFilter=ref('all'),search=ref(''),lastUpdated=ref(null),edited=ref(new Set()),photoFailed=ref(false);
let loadVersion=0,disposed=false,refreshTimer;
const counts=computed(()=>({total:rows.value.length,pending:rows.value.filter(s=>s.status==='pending').length,approved:rows.value.filter(s=>s.status==='approved').length,rejected:rows.value.filter(s=>s.status==='rejected').length}));
const stats=computed(()=>[{label:'Suggestions',value:counts.value.total},{label:'Pending review',value:counts.value.pending},{label:'Approved',value:counts.value.approved},{label:'Rejected',value:counts.value.rejected}]);
const shown=computed(()=>rows.value.filter(s=>(statusFilter.value==='all'||s.status===statusFilter.value)&&(!search.value.trim()||[s.details.name,s.details.cuisine,s.details.address,s.user?.name].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(search.value.trim().toLowerCase()))));
const nav=[['Restaurants','utensils','/admin/restaurants'],['Trash','trash','/admin/trash'],['Claims','shield','/admin/claims'],['Owners','user','/admin/owners'],['Users','user','/admin/users'],['Suggestions','book','/admin/suggestions'],['Moderation','shield','/admin/moderation'],['Messages','mail','/admin/messages'],['Subscribers','mail','/admin/subscribers'],['Reviews','star','/admin/reviews'],['Logs','book','/admin/logs'],['Statistics','trending','/admin/stats'],['Settings','sliders','/admin/settings'],['Import / Export','table','/admin/import-export']];
const navItems=computed(()=>nav.map(([label,icon])=>({label,icon,badge:label==='Suggestions'?(counts.value.pending||''):''})));
function choose(label){if(!admin.value||typeof label!=='string')return;router.push(nav.find(([name])=>name===label)?.[2]||'/admin');}
async function logout(){await auth.logout();router.push('/logged-out');}
function validate(details){const out={};for(const key of ['name','cuisine','address','contact','openingHours','description'])if(!String(details[key]||'').trim())out[key]='Please complete this field.';if(String(details.name||'').length>120)out.name='Use 120 characters or fewer.';if(String(details.description||'').length>600)out.description='Use 600 characters or fewer.';
 for(const key of ['website','photo'])if(details[key]){if(key==='photo'&&(/^\/images\/photos\/(bakery|bistro|burger|coffee|curry|dining|grill|pasta|pizza|ramen|salad|sushi|table|taco)\.jpg$/.test(details[key])||/^\/uploads\/[a-f0-9-]+\.(jpg|png|webp)$/.test(details[key])))continue;try{if(!['http:','https:'].includes(new URL(details[key]).protocol))throw Error();}catch{out[key]='Enter a complete link starting with https://.';}}
 return out;}
function updateField(key){if(errors.value[key])errors.value={...errors.value,[key]:validate(form.value)[key]};}
function editRow(s){edited.value=new Set([...edited.value,s._id]);delete reviewErrors.value[s._id];}
async function load(){if(!auth.user)return;const version=++loadVersion;refreshing.value=true;
 try{const [result,overview]=await Promise.all([get(admin.value?'/admin/restaurant-suggestions':'/me/restaurant-suggestions',{_refresh:Date.now()}),admin.value?get('/admin/stats/summary',{_refresh:Date.now()}):Promise.resolve(null)]);if(disposed||version!==loadVersion)return;
 const drafts=new Map(rows.value.filter(s=>edited.value.has(s._id)).map(s=>[s._id,s]));rows.value=result.data.map(s=>drafts.has(s._id)&&s.status==='pending'?drafts.get(s._id):s);if(overview)summary.value=overview.data;lastUpdated.value=new Date();loaded.value=true;
 }catch(e){if(!disposed&&version===loadVersion)error.value=message(e);}finally{if(!disposed&&version===loadVersion)refreshing.value=false;}}
function autoRefresh(){if(!auth.user||document.hidden||busy.value||uploading.value||refreshing.value||document.activeElement?.matches('input,textarea,select'))return;load();}
async function uploadPhoto(event){const file=event.target.files?.[0];if(!file)return;uploading.value=true;error.value='';try{if(file.size>5*1024*1024)throw new Error('Choose a photo smaller than 5 MB.');if(!['image/jpeg','image/png','image/webp'].includes(file.type))throw new Error('Choose a JPEG, PNG or WebP photo.');const data=new FormData();data.append('image',file);form.value.photo=(await api.post('/restaurant-suggestions/photo',data)).data.data.url;errors.value.photo='';status.value='Photo uploaded. It will be included when you submit.';}catch(e){error.value=e.response?message(e):e.message;event.target.value='';}finally{uploading.value=false;}}
function removePhoto(){form.value.photo='';const input=document.getElementById('suggest-photo-file');if(input)input.value='';}
async function submit(){if(busy.value||uploading.value)return;errors.value=validate(form.value);if(Object.keys(errors.value).length){error.value='Please fix the highlighted fields.';await nextTick();document.getElementById('suggest-'+Object.keys(errors.value)[0])?.focus();return;}busy.value=true;error.value='';status.value='';try{const payload=Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(form.value).map(([key,value])=>[key,value.trim()]));const result=(await post('/restaurant-suggestions',payload)).data;status.value=result.message+(result.duplicateWarning?' '+result.duplicateWarning:'');form.value=blank();errors.value={};const input=document.getElementById('suggest-photo-file');if(input)input.value='';await load();ui.toast('Your restaurant suggestion was submitted for review.');}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{busy.value=false;}}
async function decide(s,decision){if(busy.value)return;const found=decision==='approved'?validate(s.details):{};if(decision==='rejected'&&!s.rejectReason?.trim())found.reason='Explain why this suggestion is being rejected.';reviewErrors.value={...reviewErrors.value,[s._id]:found};if(Object.keys(found).length)return;busy.value=true;error.value='';try{await patch('/admin/restaurant-suggestions/'+s._id,{status:decision,details:s.details,reason:s.rejectReason});const next=new Set(edited.value);next.delete(s._id);edited.value=next;await load();ui.toast(decision==='approved'?'Restaurant approved and published.':'Suggestion rejected and the user notified.');}catch(e){error.value=message(e);}finally{busy.value=false;}}
watch(()=>form.value.photo,()=>photoFailed.value=false);
onMounted(()=>{load();refreshTimer=setInterval(autoRefresh,15000);window.addEventListener('focus',autoRefresh);document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',autoRefresh);});
onUnmounted(()=>{disposed=true;++loadVersion;clearInterval(refreshTimer);window.removeEventListener('focus',autoRefresh);document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange',autoRefresh);});
watch(()=>route.path,()=>{edited.value=new Set();rows.value=[];loaded.value=false;load();});
</script>
<template>
<component :is="admin?DashShell:'div'" v-bind="admin?{title:'Restaurant suggestions',subtitle:'Review community recommendations and publish approved restaurants.',nav:navItems,active:'Suggestions',stats,profileStats:[{label:'Pending',value:counts.pending},{label:'Approved',value:counts.approved}],roleLabel:'Admin workspace',addLink:auth.canManage}:{}" :class="{'container section suggestion-page':!admin}" @select="choose" @logout="logout">
  <div class="suggestion-content">
    <header v-if="!admin" class="suggestion-head"><div><p class="eyebrow muted">Community recommendations</p><h1>{{historyOnly?'Your restaurant suggestions':'Suggest a restaurant'}}</h1><p class="muted">Recommend a place worth visiting and follow its review status here.</p></div><RouterLink to="/account" class="btn secondary"><Icon name="arrow-left" :size="16"/>Back to dashboard</RouterLink></header>
    <p v-if="error" role="alert" class="error-box">{{error}}</p><p v-if="status" role="status" class="notice success-note">{{status}}</p>
    <div v-if="!auth.user" class="card pad empty-panel"><Icon name="user" :size="32"/><h2>Sign in to recommend a restaurant</h2><p>Browse freely, then create an account to submit and track your recommendations.</p><div class="row"><RouterLink :to="{path:'/login',query:{redirect:route.fullPath}}" class="btn">Log in</RouterLink><RouterLink to="/register" class="btn secondary">Create an account</RouterLink></div></div>
    <template v-else>
      <div v-if="!admin&&!historyOnly" class="submission-layout">
        <form v-if="canSuggest" class="card pad suggestion-form" novalidate :aria-busy="busy||uploading" @submit.prevent="submit"><fieldset :disabled="busy||uploading"><div class="section-heading"><h2>Restaurant details</h2><p class="small muted">Fields marked * are required. Optional links and photos help the administrator review your suggestion.</p></div>
          <div class="suggestion-grid"><div v-for="[key,label,placeholder] in fields.filter(([key])=>key!=='photo')" :key="key" class="field" :class="{'full-width':['address','description'].includes(key)}"><label :for="'suggest-'+key">{{label}}{{key==='website'?'':' *'}}</label><textarea v-if="key==='description'" :id="'suggest-'+key" v-model="form[key]" rows="4" maxlength="600" :placeholder="placeholder" :aria-invalid="!!errors[key]" :aria-describedby="'suggest-'+key+'-help'" @input="updateField(key)"></textarea><input v-else :id="'suggest-'+key" v-model="form[key]" :type="key==='website'?'url':'text'" :maxlength="key==='name'?120:500" :placeholder="placeholder" :aria-invalid="!!errors[key]" :aria-describedby="'suggest-'+key+'-help'" @input="updateField(key)"><p :id="'suggest-'+key+'-help'" v-if="errors[key]" class="field-msg">{{errors[key]}}</p><span v-if="key==='description'" class="char-count">{{form.description.length}} / 600</span></div></div>
          <section class="photo-section"><h3>Restaurant photo <span class="small muted">Optional</span></h3><p class="small muted">Enter a bundled photo path, paste an image link or upload a photo. Uploading replaces the current link.</p><div class="field"><label for="suggest-photo">Photo link</label><input id="suggest-photo" v-model="form.photo" type="text" inputmode="url" maxlength="500" placeholder="/images/photos/dining.jpg" :aria-invalid="!!errors.photo" @input="updateField('photo')"><p v-if="errors.photo" class="field-msg">{{errors.photo}}</p></div><div class="upload-box"><label for="suggest-photo-file">{{uploading?'Uploading photo…':'Choose a photo'}}</label><input id="suggest-photo-file" type="file" accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp" @change="uploadPhoto"><p class="small muted">JPEG, PNG or WebP, up to 5 MB.</p></div><figure v-if="form.photo&&!photoFailed" class="photo-preview"><img :src="imageUrl(form.photo)" alt="Suggested restaurant photo preview" @error="photoFailed=true"><figcaption class="small muted">Photo preview <button type="button" class="secondary small" @click="removePhoto">Remove photo</button></figcaption></figure><p v-if="photoFailed" class="field-msg">The image could not be loaded. Check the link or upload a photo.</p></section>
          <div class="submit-bar"><p class="small muted">Your suggestion appears publicly after administrator approval.</p><button type="submit" :disabled="busy||uploading"><Icon name="plus" :size="16"/>{{busy?'Submitting…':uploading?'Uploading photo…':'Submit suggestion'}}</button></div>
        </fieldset></form>
        <div v-else class="card pad"><h2>{{!auth.user.emailVerified?'Verify your email first':'Use your owner dashboard'}}</h2><p>{{!auth.user.emailVerified?'Email verification is required before submitting a recommendation.':'Restaurant owners add and manage listings through their owner dashboard.'}}</p><RouterLink :to="!auth.user.emailVerified?'/verify-email':auth.user.role==='owner'?'/owner':'/admin'" class="btn">{{!auth.user.emailVerified?'Verify email':'Open dashboard'}}</RouterLink></div>
        <aside class="suggestion-guide card pad"><p class="eyebrow muted">How it works</p><h2>From recommendation to listing</h2><ol><li><b>Share the details</b><p>Include the location, cuisine, contact information and opening hours.</p></li><li><b>Administrator review</b><p>The administrator checks the details and possible duplicates.</p></li><li><b>Follow your status</b><p>An approved suggestion becomes a public restaurant listing. Rejected suggestions show a reason.</p></li></ol><p class="small muted">Suggesting a restaurant does not give you ownership or editing access.</p><RouterLink to="/account/suggestions" class="btn secondary">View your suggestions</RouterLink></aside>
      </div>
      <section v-if="auth.user" class="suggestion-history" aria-labelledby="suggestion-list-title">
        <div v-if="!admin" class="suggestion-stats"><article v-for="stat in stats" :key="stat.label" class="card"><span class="small muted">{{stat.label}}</span><b>{{stat.value}}</b></article></div>
        <div class="history-head"><div><h2 id="suggestion-list-title">{{admin?'Review queue':'Your suggestions'}}</h2><p class="small muted">{{lastUpdated?'Updated '+lastUpdated.toLocaleTimeString():'Checking the latest recommendations…'}}</p></div><div class="row"><RouterLink v-if="historyOnly&&canSuggest" to="/suggest-restaurant" class="btn">Suggest a restaurant</RouterLink><button type="button" class="secondary" :disabled="busy||uploading||refreshing" @click="load">{{refreshing?'Refreshing…':'Refresh suggestions'}}</button></div></div>
        <div class="history-toolbar"><div class="field"><label for="suggestion-search">Search suggestions</label><input id="suggestion-search" v-model="search" type="search" placeholder="Restaurant, cuisine or location"></div><div class="field"><label for="suggestion-status">Status</label><select id="suggestion-status" v-model="statusFilter"><option value="all">All statuses</option><option value="pending">Pending review</option><option value="approved">Approved</option><option value="rejected">Rejected</option></select></div><span class="small muted" role="status">{{shown.length}} of {{rows.length}}</span></div>
        <div v-if="!loaded&&refreshing" class="skeleton" aria-label="Loading suggestions"></div>
        <div v-else-if="!shown.length" class="card pad empty-panel"><Icon name="utensils" :size="36"/><h3>{{rows.length?'No matching suggestions':admin?'No recommendations to review yet':'Your next food discovery starts here'}}</h3><p class="muted">{{rows.length?'Try another word or choose All statuses.':admin?'Community recommendations will appear here with their details and review actions.':'Submit a restaurant above, and its approval status will appear here.'}}</p><button v-if="rows.length" class="secondary" @click="search='';statusFilter='all'">Clear filters</button><RouterLink v-else-if="historyOnly&&canSuggest" to="/suggest-restaurant" class="btn">Suggest a restaurant</RouterLink></div>
        <div v-else class="suggestion-list"><article v-for="s in shown" :key="s._id" class="card pad suggestion-card"><div class="suggestion-card-head"><div><h3>{{s.details.name}}</h3><p class="small muted">{{s.details.cuisine}} · {{s.user?.name||'Community member'}} · {{s.createdAt?.slice(0,10)}}</p></div><span class="badge status-badge" :class="s.status">{{s.status==='pending'?'Pending review':s.status}}</span></div><p v-if="s.duplicateWarning" class="notice">{{s.duplicateWarning}}</p>
          <template v-if="admin&&s.status==='pending'"><div class="suggestion-grid"><div v-for="[key,label] in fields" :key="key" class="field" :class="{'full-width':['description','address'].includes(key)}"><label :for="s._id+'-'+key">{{label}}</label><textarea v-if="key==='description'" :id="s._id+'-'+key" v-model="s.details[key]" rows="3" maxlength="600" @input="editRow(s)"></textarea><input v-else :id="s._id+'-'+key" v-model="s.details[key]" :maxlength="key==='name'?120:500" :type="key==='website'?'url':'text'" @input="editRow(s)"><p v-if="reviewErrors[s._id]?.[key]" class="field-msg">{{reviewErrors[s._id][key]}}</p></div></div><div class="field rejection-field"><label :for="s._id+'-reason'">Rejection reason <span class="small muted">Required only when rejecting</span></label><textarea :id="s._id+'-reason'" v-model="s.rejectReason" rows="2" maxlength="1000" @input="editRow(s)"></textarea><p v-if="reviewErrors[s._id]?.reason" class="field-msg">{{reviewErrors[s._id].reason}}</p></div><div class="review-actions"><span v-if="edited.has(s._id)" class="small muted">Unsaved review edits</span><button type="button" :disabled="busy" @click="decide(s,'approved')">Approve and publish</button><button type="button" class="secondary" :disabled="busy||!s.rejectReason?.trim()" @click="decide(s,'rejected')">Reject suggestion</button></div></template>
          <template v-else><p class="muted">{{s.details.address}}</p><p>{{s.details.description}}</p><dl class="suggestion-facts"><div><dt>Contact</dt><dd>{{s.details.contact}}</dd></div><div><dt>Hours</dt><dd>{{s.details.openingHours}}</dd></div></dl><p v-if="s.status==='rejected'" class="notice">Reason: {{s.rejectReason}}</p><RouterLink v-if="s.listing" :to="'/restaurants/'+s.listing" class="btn secondary">View published restaurant</RouterLink></template>
        </article></div>
      </section>
    </template>
  </div>
</component>
</template>

server/src/app.js

import express from 'express';
import swaggerUi from 'swagger-ui-express';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import helmet from 'helmet';
import cors from 'cors';
import path from 'node:path';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import {env} from './config/env.js';
import api,{uploads} from './routes/index.js';
import {optionalAuth} from './middleware/auth.js';
import {generalLimit} from './middleware/rateLimiter.js';
import {errorHandler} from './middleware/errorHandler.js';
import {notFound} from './middleware/notFound.js';
import ApiError from './utils/ApiError.js';
export const app=express();app.disable('x-powered-by');
app.use(helmet({contentSecurityPolicy:{directives:{'img-src':["'self'",'data:','https:','http://localhost:5000'],'connect-src':["'self'",env.origin,'https://tile.openstreetmap.org']}}}));
app.use(cors({origin:env.origin}));app.use(express.json({limit:'2mb'}));
app.use('/uploads',helmet({crossOriginResourcePolicy:{policy:'cross-origin'}}),express.static(uploads));
const contract=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(new URL('./openapi.json',import.meta.url),'utf8'));app.get('/api/openapi.json',(req,res)=>res.json(contract));app.use('/api/docs',swaggerUi.serve,swaggerUi.setup(contract));
app.use('/api',generalLimit,optionalAuth,api);app.use('/api',notFound);
const client=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../client/dist',import.meta.url));app.use(express.static(client));app.get('*',(req,res)=>res.sendFile(path.join(client,'index.html'),err=>{if(err)res.status(404).send('Frontend is not built yet. Run npm run build, or open http://localhost:5173 during development.');}));app.use(errorHandler);
export default app;
