server/src/config/env.js

import dotenv from 'dotenv';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
dotenv.config({path:fileURLToPath(new URL('../../.env',import.meta.url)),quiet:true});
export const env={port:Number(process.env.PORT||5000),nodeEnv:process.env.NODE_ENV||'development',uri:process.env.MONGODB_URI,origin:process.env.CLIENT_ORIGIN||'http://localhost:5173',secret:process.env.JWT_SECRET,expires:process.env.JWT_EXPIRES_IN||'7d',min:Number(process.env.TOP_QUALITY_MIN||90),requireAuth:process.env.REQUIRE_AUTH_FOR_WRITES!=='false',appUrl:process.env.APP_URL||'http://localhost:5173'};
export function checkEnv(){if(!env.uri)throw new Error('MONGODB_URI is missing. Copy server/.env.example to server/.env.');if(!env.secret||env.secret.length<32||env.secret.startsWith('replace-'))throw new Error('Set JWT_SECRET to a random string with at least 32 characters.');if(env.nodeEnv==='production'&&!env.requireAuth)throw new Error('REQUIRE_AUTH_FOR_WRITES must be true in production.');}

server/src/controllers/authController.js

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import {User,LoginHistory,Review,Favorite,Collection,Notification,SearchHistory,Claim} from '../models/index.js';
import {env} from '../config/env.js';
import {ok,check,clean,safeUser} from '../utils/http.js';
import {sendMail} from '../utils/mail.js';
import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
import {audit,notifyStaff} from '../utils/audit.js';
const hash=x=>crypto.createHash('sha256').update(x).digest('hex');
const passValid=p=>typeof p==='string'&&p.length>=8&&p.length<=128&&/[A-Za-z]/.test(p)&&/\d/.test(p);
const token=u=>jwt.sign({sub:String(u._id),ver:u.tokenVersion},env.secret,{expiresIn:env.expires});
export async function register(req,res){const e=[];if(!req.body.name?.trim())e.push({field:'name',message:'Please enter your name.'});if(!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(req.body.email||''))e.push({field:'email',message:'Please enter a valid email address.'});if(!passValid(req.body.password))e.push({field:'password',message:'Password must be at least 8 characters with a letter and a number.'});if(req.body.confirmPassword!==req.body.password)e.push({field:'confirmPassword',message:'Passwords do not match.'});check(e);const email=req.body.email.trim().toLowerCase();if(await User.exists({email}))throw new ApiError(409,'This email is already registered.');const owner=req.path.includes('owners');if(owner&&(!req.body.businessName?.trim()||!req.body.phone?.trim()||!req.body.position?.trim()))throw new ApiError(422,'Please enter your business name, phone and position.');const u=await User.create({name:clean(req.body.name).slice(0,120),email,passwordHash:await bcrypt.hash(req.body.password,12),role:owner?'owner':'customer',ownerProfile:owner?{businessName:clean(req.body.businessName),phone:clean(req.body.phone),position:clean(req.body.position),verificationStatus:'pending'}:undefined});const delivery=await issueCode(u);await Notification.create({user:u._id,title:'Verify your email',message:'Verify your email. Tap here to verify your email address.',link:'/verify-email'});if(owner)await notifyStaff('New owner account',u.name,'/admin/owners');ok(res,{user:safeUser(u),token:token(u),message:delivery.message,delivery},{},201);}
export async function login(req,res){const email=String(req.body.email||'').trim().toLowerCase();const u=await User.findOne({email}).select('+passwordHash');const history={email,user:u?._id,ip:req.ip,userAgent:req.get('user-agent')?.slice(0,300)};if(u?.lockUntil>Date.now())throw new ApiError(429,'Account locked. Please try again in 15 minutes.');if(!u||!await bcrypt.compare(String(req.body.password||''),u.passwordHash)){if(u){u.failedLoginCount++;if(u.failedLoginCount>=5){u.lockUntil=new Date(Date.now()+15*60000);u.failedLoginCount=0;}await u.save();}await LoginHistory.create({...history,success:false});throw new ApiError(401,'Email or password is incorrect.');}if(u.status!=='active')throw new ApiError(403,'This account is suspended.');u.failedLoginCount=0;u.lockUntil=undefined;u.lastLoginAt=new Date();await u.save();await LoginHistory.create({...history,success:true});ok(res,{user:safeUser(u),token:token(u)});}
export async function logout(req,res){await User.updateOne({_id:req.user._id},{$inc:{tokenVersion:1}});ok(res,{message:'You have been logged out.'});}
export async function me(req,res){ok(res,safeUser(req.user));}
// Each code is bound to its account; conditional updates make consumption atomic.
export async function issueCode(u){
 const code=String(crypto.randomInt(100000,1000000));
 const result=await sendMail(u.email,'Your BonAppé Find verification code',`Your verification code is ${code}. It expires in 10 minutes. Do not share it.`);
 const sentAt=new Date();await User.updateOne({_id:u._id},{verifyHash:hash(String(u._id)+':'+code),verifyUntil:new Date(Date.now()+600000),verifySentAt:sentAt,verifyAttempts:0});u.verifySentAt=sentAt;
 return {delivered:result.delivered,...(!result.delivered&&env.nodeEnv!=='production'?{developmentCode:code}:{}),cooldown:60,message:result.delivered?'Verification code sent to your email.':'Simulated verification: no email was sent. Your verification code is shown in the popup and server terminal.'};
}
export async function verify(req,res){
 if(req.user.emailVerified)throw new ApiError(409,'This email is already verified.');
 const u=await User.findById(req.user._id).select('+verifyHash +verifyUntil +verifyAttempts');
 if(!u.verifyHash||!u.verifyUntil||u.verifyUntil<=new Date())throw new ApiError(400,'This code has expired or was already used. Please resend a code.');
 if(u.verifyAttempts>=5)throw new ApiError(429,'Too many incorrect attempts. Please resend a code.');
 const code=String(req.body.code||'');
 if(!/^\d{6}$/.test(code)||hash(String(u._id)+':'+code)!==u.verifyHash){await User.updateOne({_id:u._id,verifyHash:u.verifyHash,verifyAttempts:{$lt:5}},{$inc:{verifyAttempts:1}});throw new ApiError(400,'Incorrect verification code. Check the six digits in your email.');}
 const verified=await User.findOneAndUpdate({_id:u._id,email:u.email,emailVerified:false,verifyHash:u.verifyHash,verifyUntil:{$gt:new Date()},verifyAttempts:{$lt:5}},{$set:{emailVerified:true},$unset:{verifyHash:1,verifyUntil:1,verifyAttempts:1}},{new:true});
 if(!verified)throw new ApiError(400,'This code is expired, already used, or has too many attempts.');
 ok(res,{user:safeUser(verified),message:verified.role==='owner'?'Email verified. Your owner account is awaiting administrator approval. You can manage restaurant listings once approved.':'Email verified. You can now post reviews and suggest restaurants.'});
}
export async function resend(req,res){
 if(req.user.emailVerified)throw new ApiError(409,'This email is already verified.');
 const reserved=await User.findOneAndUpdate({_id:req.user._id,$or:[{verifySentAt:{$lte:new Date(Date.now()-60000)}},{verifySentAt:null}]},{$set:{verifySentAt:new Date()}},{new:true});
 if(!reserved)throw new ApiError(429,'Please wait 60 seconds before requesting another code.');
 try{ok(res,await issueCode(reserved));}catch(e){await User.updateOne({_id:reserved._id},{$unset:{verifySentAt:1}});throw e;}
}
export async function correctEmail(req,res){
 if(req.user.emailVerified)throw new ApiError(409,'Your email is already verified.');
 const email=String(req.body.email||'').trim().toLowerCase();
 if(!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email))throw new ApiError(422,'Enter a valid email address.');
 const u=await User.findById(req.user._id).select('+passwordHash');
 if(!await bcrypt.compare(String(req.body.password||''),u.passwordHash))throw new ApiError(403,'Enter your current password to correct your email.');
 if(await User.exists({email,_id:{$ne:u._id}}))throw new ApiError(409,'This email is already registered.');
 if(u.verifySentAt&&Date.now()-u.verifySentAt.getTime()<60000)throw new ApiError(429,'Please wait 60 seconds before changing the email and requesting a code.');
 u.email=email;u.verifyHash=undefined;u.verifyUntil=undefined;await u.save();const delivery=await issueCode(u);ok(res,{user:safeUser(u),...delivery});
}
export async function forgot(req,res){
 const email=String(req.body.email||'').trim().toLowerCase();
 if(!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email))throw new ApiError(422,'Enter a valid email address.');
 const response={message:'If an account exists, a six-digit reset code has been sent. Codes expire in 10 minutes.',cooldown:60};
 const u=await User.findOneAndUpdate({email,$or:[{resetSentAt:{$lte:new Date(Date.now()-60000)}},{resetSentAt:null}]},{$set:{resetSentAt:new Date()}},{new:true});
 if(u){
  const code=String(crypto.randomInt(100000,1000000));
  try{
   const delivery=await sendMail(email,'Your BonAppé Find password reset code',`Your password reset code is ${code}. It expires in 10 minutes. Do not share it.`);
   await User.updateOne({_id:u._id},{resetHash:hash(String(u._id)+':reset:'+code),resetUntil:new Date(Date.now()+600000),resetAttempts:0});
   if(!delivery.delivered&&env.nodeEnv!=='production'){response.developmentCode=code;response.message='Email delivery is simulated. Your reset code is shown in the popup and server terminal.';}
  }catch(e){await User.updateOne({_id:u._id},{$unset:{resetSentAt:1}});throw e;}
 }
 ok(res,response);
}
export async function reset(req,res){
 if(!passValid(req.body.password))throw new ApiError(422,'Password must be at least 8 characters with a letter and a number.');
 if(req.body.password!==req.body.confirmPassword)throw new ApiError(422,'Passwords do not match.');
 const email=String(req.body.email||'').trim().toLowerCase(),code=String(req.body.code||'');
 const u=await User.findOne({email}).select('+resetHash +resetUntil +resetAttempts');
 const invalid='Invalid or expired reset code. Request a new code and try again.';
 if(!u||!u.resetHash||!u.resetUntil||u.resetUntil<=new Date())throw new ApiError(400,invalid);
 if(u.resetAttempts>=5)throw new ApiError(429,'Too many incorrect attempts. Request a new reset code.');
 if(!/^\d{6}$/.test(code)||hash(String(u._id)+':reset:'+code)!==u.resetHash){await User.updateOne({_id:u._id,resetHash:u.resetHash,resetAttempts:{$lt:5}},{$inc:{resetAttempts:1}});throw new ApiError(400,invalid);}
 const updated=await User.findOneAndUpdate({_id:u._id,email,resetHash:u.resetHash,resetUntil:{$gt:new Date()},resetAttempts:{$lt:5}},{$set:{passwordHash:await bcrypt.hash(req.body.password,12),failedLoginCount:0},$inc:{tokenVersion:1},$unset:{resetHash:1,resetUntil:1,resetAttempts:1,lockUntil:1}},{new:true});
 if(!updated)throw new ApiError(400,invalid);
 ok(res,{message:'Password updated. Please log in with your new password.'});
}
export async function profile(req,res){for(const k of ['name','avatar'])if(typeof req.body[k]==='string')req.user[k]=clean(req.body[k]).slice(0,300);if(req.body.preferences)for(const k of ['emailAlerts','language','theme'])if(req.body.preferences[k]!=null)req.user.set('preferences.'+k,req.body.preferences[k]);await req.user.save();ok(res,safeUser(req.user));}
export async function download(req,res){const user=req.user._id;const [reviews,favorites,collections,searchHistory]=await Promise.all([Review.find({user}),Favorite.find({user}),Collection.find({user}),SearchHistory.find({user})]);ok(res,{user:safeUser(req.user),reviews,favorites,collections,searchHistory});}
export async function deleteAccount(req,res){if(req.user.role==='admin')throw new ApiError(403,'Ask another administrator to remove an admin account.');const user=req.user._id;await Promise.all([Favorite.deleteMany({user}),Collection.deleteMany({user}),Notification.deleteMany({user}),SearchHistory.deleteMany({user}),Claim.deleteMany({user,status:'pending'})]);await Review.updateMany({user},{author:'Deleted user',comment:'[Removed by author]',status:'hidden'});await User.deleteOne({_id:user});await audit(req,'delete-account','user',user,null,null);ok(res,{message:'Account deleted.'});}

server/src/controllers/contentController.js

import {Country,Guide,Faq,Restaurant,Message,Subscriber,Setting} from '../models/index.js';
import {ok,clean,check} from '../utils/http.js';
import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
import {publicFilter,presentRestaurant} from '../utils/restaurant.js';
import {notifyStaff,audit} from '../utils/audit.js';
export const content=(Model)=>({list:async(req,res)=>ok(res,await Model.find().sort(Model===Faq?{order:1}:{name:1,publishedAt:-1})),detail:async(req,res)=>{const x=await Model.findOne({slug:req.params.slug});if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Guide not found.');ok(res,x);},save:async(req,res)=>{const allowed=Object.keys(Model.schema.paths).filter(k=>!['_id','__v','createdAt','updatedAt'].includes(k));const b=Object.fromEntries(allowed.filter(k=>req.body[k]!==undefined).map(k=>[k,req.body[k]]));if(!b.slug||!(b.name||b.title))throw new ApiError(422,'A slug and name or title are required.');let x;if(req.method==='POST')x=await Model.create(b);else{x=await Model.findOneAndUpdate({slug:req.params.slug},{$set:b},{new:true,runValidators:true});if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Record not found.');}await audit(req,'content-save',Model.modelName,x._id,null,b);ok(res,x,{},req.method==='POST'?201:200);},remove:async(req,res)=>{const x=await Model.findOneAndDelete({slug:req.params.slug});if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Record not found.');await audit(req,'content-delete',Model.modelName,x._id,x.toObject(),null);ok(res,{deleted:true});}});
export async function countryRestaurants(req,res){ok(res,(await Restaurant.find({...publicFilter(),countrySlug:req.params.slug}).limit(100).lean()).map(presentRestaurant));}
export async function contact(req,res){if(req.body.website)throw new ApiError(422,'We could not send your message.');const e=[];for(const [field,valid,message]of[['name',!!req.body.name?.trim(),'Please enter your name.'],['email',/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(req.body.email||''),'Please enter a valid email address.'],['subject',!!req.body.subject?.trim(),'Please choose a subject.'],['message',req.body.message?.trim().length>=10,'Please write a message of at least 10 characters.']])if(!valid)e.push({field,message});if(req.body.message?.length>1000)e.push({field:'message',message:'Please write a message of 1,000 characters or fewer.'});check(e);const x=await Message.create(Object.fromEntries(['name','email','subject','message'].map(k=>[k,clean(req.body[k])])));await notifyStaff('New contact message',x.subject,'/admin/messages');ok(res,{message:'Thank you! Your message has been sent. We will reply soon.'},{},201);}
export async function newsletter(req,res){const email=String(req.body.email||'').trim().toLowerCase();if(!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email))throw new ApiError(422,'Please enter a valid email address.');if(req.method==='DELETE'){await Subscriber.updateOne({email},{isActive:false});return ok(res,{message:'You have been unsubscribed.'});}const existing=await Subscriber.findOne({email});if(existing?.isActive)throw new ApiError(409,'This email is already subscribed. Thank you!');await Subscriber.findOneAndUpdate({email},{$set:{isActive:true,subscribedAt:new Date()}},{upsert:true});ok(res,{message:'You are on the list.'},{},201);}
export async function settings(req,res){let x=await Setting.findOne({key:'site'});if(req.method==='PUT'){x=await Setting.findOneAndUpdate({key:'site'},{$set:{banner:clean(req.body.banner||'').slice(0,300),bannerExpiresAt:req.body.bannerExpiresAt||null,maintenance:!!req.body.maintenance}},{upsert:true,new:true});await audit(req,'settings','site','site',null,x.toObject());}ok(res,x||{banner:'',maintenance:false});}

server/src/controllers/dashboardController.js

import * as M from '../models/index.js';
import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {ok,id,pagination,safeUser,clean} from '../utils/http.js';
import {publicFilter,presentRestaurant,groupPipeline} from '../utils/restaurant.js';
import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
import {audit,notify} from '../utils/audit.js';
import {isStaff} from '../middleware/auth.js';
import {validateRestaurant} from '../validators/restaurantValidators.js';
export async function summary(req,res){
 const [restaurants,approved,pending,deleted,cuisines,owners,pendingOwners,approvedOwners,users,reviews,messages,subscribers,claims,reports,suggestions,restaurantSuggestions]=await Promise.all([
 M.Restaurant.countDocuments({isDeleted:{$ne:true}}),M.Restaurant.countDocuments({approvalStatus:'approved',isDeleted:{$ne:true}}),M.Restaurant.countDocuments({approvalStatus:'pending',isDeleted:{$ne:true}}),M.Restaurant.countDocuments({isDeleted:true}),M.Restaurant.distinct('cuisine',publicFilter()),M.User.countDocuments({role:'owner'}),M.User.countDocuments({role:'owner','ownerProfile.verificationStatus':'pending'}),M.User.countDocuments({role:'owner','ownerProfile.verificationStatus':'approved'}),M.User.countDocuments(),M.Review.countDocuments(),M.Message.countDocuments({status:'new'}),M.Subscriber.countDocuments({isActive:true}),M.Claim.countDocuments({status:'pending'}),M.Report.countDocuments({status:'open'}),M.Suggestion.countDocuments({status:'pending'}),M.RestaurantSuggestion.countDocuments({status:'pending'})]);
 res.set('Cache-Control','no-store');
 ok(res,{restaurants,approved,pending,deleted,cuisines:cuisines.length,owners,pendingOwners,approvedOwners,users,reviews,messages,subscribers,claims,reports,suggestions,restaurantSuggestions,pendingRequests:pending+pendingOwners+claims+reports+suggestions+restaurantSuggestions});
}
export async function reviewStats(req,res){const days=[7,30,90].includes(Number(req.query.days))?Number(req.query.days):30;ok(res,await M.Review.aggregate([{$match:{createdAt:{$gte:new Date(Date.now()-days*86400000)}}},{$group:{_id:{$dateToString:{format:'%Y-%m-%d',date:'$createdAt'}},count:{$sum:1},avgRating:{$avg:'$rating'}}},{$sort:{_id:1}}]));}
export const records=(Model,filter={})=>async(req,res)=>{const p=pagination(req.query);const f=typeof filter==='function'?filter(req):filter;const [rows,total]=await Promise.all([Model.find(f).sort({createdAt:-1}).skip(p.skip).limit(p.limit),Model.countDocuments(f)]);ok(res,rows.map(r=>Model===M.User?safeUser(r):r),{total,page:p.page,limit:p.limit,pages:Math.ceil(total/p.limit)});};
export async function claims(req,res){ok(res,await M.Claim.find(req.query.status?{status:req.query.status}:{}).populate('restaurant','name ownerId isClaimed').populate('user','name email ownerProfile').sort({createdAt:-1}).limit(100));}
export async function decideClaim(req,res){const x=await M.Claim.findById(id(req.params.id));if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Claim not found.');if(x.status!=='pending')throw new ApiError(409,'This claim has already been reviewed.');if(!['approved','rejected'].includes(req.body.status))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose approved or rejected.');if(req.body.status==='approved'){const r=await M.Restaurant.findOneAndUpdate({_id:x.restaurant,isClaimed:{$ne:true},isDeleted:{$ne:true}},{$set:{ownerId:x.user,isClaimed:true,lastVerifiedAt:new Date()}},{new:true});if(!r)throw new ApiError(409,'This restaurant is unavailable or already claimed.');}x.status=req.body.status;x.reviewedBy=req.user._id;x.reviewedAt=new Date();x.rejectReason=clean(req.body.reason||'');await x.save();await notify(x.user,'Ownership claim reviewed',x.status+(x.rejectReason?': '+x.rejectReason:''),'/owner');await audit(req,'claim-decision','claim',x._id,null,x.toObject());ok(res,x);}
export async function ownerStats(req,res){const restaurants=await M.Restaurant.find({ownerId:req.user._id,isDeleted:{$ne:true}}).lean();const ids=restaurants.map(r=>r._id);const [reviews,messages,unreadNotifications]=await Promise.all([M.Review.countDocuments({restaurant:{$in:ids}}),M.OwnerMessage.countDocuments({restaurant:{$in:ids},status:'new'}),M.Notification.countDocuments({user:req.user._id,isRead:false})]);ok(res,{restaurants:restaurants.length,views:restaurants.reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.viewCount||0),0),saves:restaurants.reduce((s,r)=>s+(r.saveCount||0),0),reviews,messages,unreadNotifications,approved:restaurants.filter(r=>r.approvalStatus==='approved').length,rejected:restaurants.filter(r=>r.approvalStatus==='rejected').length,pending:restaurants.filter(r=>r.approvalStatus==='pending').length});}
export async function ownerRecords(req,res){const rs=await M.Restaurant.find({ownerId:req.user._id,isDeleted:{$ne:true}}).lean(),ids=rs.map(r=>r._id);const kind=req.params.kind;if(kind==='restaurants')return ok(res,rs.map(presentRestaurant));const model=kind==='reviews'?M.Review:M.OwnerMessage;ok(res,await model.find({restaurant:{$in:ids}}).sort({createdAt:-1}).limit(100));}
export async function messageAction(req,res){const model=req.path.includes('owner')?M.OwnerMessage:M.Message;const x=await model.findById(id(req.params.id));if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Message not found.');if(model===M.OwnerMessage){const r=await M.Restaurant.findById(x.restaurant);if(String(r?.ownerId)!==String(req.user._id))throw new ApiError(403,'You do not have permission to do that.');}if(req.method==='DELETE'){await x.deleteOne();return ok(res,{deleted:true});}x.status=req.body.status==='replied'?'replied':'read';x.reply=clean(req.body.reply||'').slice(0,1000);await x.save();ok(res,x);}
export async function userAction(req,res){if(req.method==='POST'){if(!['admin','moderator','owner','customer'].includes(req.body.role))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose a valid role.');if(!req.body.name||!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(req.body.email||'')||!req.body.password||req.body.password.length<8||!/[A-Za-z]/.test(req.body.password)||!/[0-9]/.test(req.body.password))throw new ApiError(422,'Enter a name, valid email, and password with at least 8 characters including a letter and number.');const x=await M.User.create({name:clean(req.body.name),email:req.body.email.toLowerCase(),role:req.body.role,passwordHash:await bcrypt.hash(req.body.password,12),emailVerified:true});await audit(req,'create-user','user',x._id,null,safeUser(x));return ok(res,safeUser(x),{},201);}const u=await M.User.findById(id(req.params.id));if(!u)throw new ApiError(404,'User not found.');if(String(u._id)===String(req.user._id))throw new ApiError(422,'You cannot suspend or change your own role here.');if(req.body.status){if(!['active','suspended'].includes(req.body.status))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose active or suspended.');u.status=req.body.status;u.tokenVersion++;}if(req.body.role&&['customer','owner','moderator','admin'].includes(req.body.role))u.role=req.body.role;if(req.body.verificationStatus)throw new ApiError(422,'Use the owner application decision endpoint.');await u.save();await audit(req,'update-user','user',u._id,null,safeUser(u));ok(res,safeUser(u));}
export async function reports(req,res){const x=await M.Report.findById(id(req.params.id));if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Report not found.');if(!['resolved','dismissed'].includes(req.body.status))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose resolved or dismissed.');x.status=req.body.status;x.handledBy=req.user._id;await x.save();await audit(req,'report-decision','report',x._id,null,x.toObject());ok(res,x);}
export async function favorites(req,res){const user=req.user._id;if(req.method==='GET'){const rows=await M.Favorite.find({user});return ok(res,(await M.Restaurant.find({...publicFilter(),_id:{$in:rows.map(r=>r.restaurant)}}).lean()).map(presentRestaurant));}const restaurant=id(req.body.restaurant);if(req.method==='DELETE'){const deleted=await M.Favorite.deleteOne({user,restaurant});if(deleted.deletedCount)await M.Restaurant.updateOne({_id:restaurant},{$inc:{saveCount:-1}});return ok(res,{saved:false});}if(!await M.Restaurant.exists({_id:restaurant,...publicFilter()}))throw new ApiError(404,'Restaurant not found.');try{await M.Favorite.create({user,restaurant});await M.Restaurant.updateOne({_id:restaurant},{$inc:{saveCount:1}});}catch(e){if(e.code!==11000)throw e;}ok(res,{saved:true});}
export async function collections(req,res){const user=req.user._id;if(req.method==='GET')return ok(res,await M.Collection.find({user}));if(req.method==='DELETE'){await M.Collection.deleteOne({_id:id(req.params.id),user});return ok(res,{deleted:true});}if(!req.body.name?.trim())throw new ApiError(422,'Please name your collection.');const ids=(req.body.restaurants||[]).slice(0,100);ids.forEach(id);const b={name:clean(req.body.name).slice(0,80),isPublic:!!req.body.isPublic,restaurants:ids};const x=req.method==='POST'?await M.Collection.create({...b,user}):await M.Collection.findOneAndUpdate({_id:id(req.params.id),user},{$set:b},{new:true});if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Collection not found.');ok(res,x);}
export async function publicCollection(req,res){const c=await M.Collection.findOne({_id:id(req.params.id),isPublic:true});if(!c)throw new ApiError(404,'Collection not found.');ok(res,{name:c.name,restaurants:(await M.Restaurant.find({...publicFilter(),_id:{$in:c.restaurants}}).lean()).map(presentRestaurant)});}
export async function searchHistory(req,res){const user=req.user._id;if(req.method==='DELETE'){await M.SearchHistory.deleteMany({user});return ok(res,[]);}if(req.method==='POST'){await M.SearchHistory.create({user,query:clean(req.body.query||'').slice(0,120),filters:req.body.filters||{}});return ok(res,{saved:true});}ok(res,await M.SearchHistory.find({user}).sort({createdAt:-1}).limit(10));}
export async function notifications(req,res){if(req.method==='PATCH'){const x=await M.Notification.findOneAndUpdate({_id:id(req.params.id),user:req.user._id},{$set:{isRead:true}},{new:true});if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Notification not found.');return ok(res,x);}ok(res,await M.Notification.find({user:req.user._id}).sort({createdAt:-1}).limit(50));}
export async function exportRestaurants(req,res){const rows=await M.Restaurant.find().lean();if(req.query.format==='csv'){const cols=['restaurant_id','name','cuisine','borough','country','city'];res.type('text/csv').attachment('restaurants.csv').send([cols.join(','),...rows.map(r=>cols.map(k=>'"'+String(r[k]||'').replaceAll('"','""')+'"').join(','))].join('\r\n'));}else ok(res,rows);}
export async function importRestaurants(req,res){const records=req.body.records;if(!Array.isArray(records)||records.length>1000)throw new ApiError(422,'Supply a records array of at most 1,000 restaurants.');const results=[];for(const [index,b]of records.entries()){try{const data=validateRestaurant(b);data.restaurant_id=typeof b.restaurant_id==='string'?b.restaurant_id:'IMPORT-'+crypto.randomUUID();data.countrySlug=data.country.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'-');await M.Restaurant.updateOne({restaurant_id:data.restaurant_id},{$set:data},{upsert:true,runValidators:true});results.push({index,success:true});}catch(e){results.push({index,success:false,message:e.message,errors:e.errors||[]});}}await audit(req,'import','restaurants','bulk',null,{count:records.length});ok(res,results);}
export async function qualityReport(req,res){ok(res,await M.Restaurant.find({...publicFilter(),$or:[{'address.street':{$in:['',null]}},{'profile.openingHours':{$in:['',null]}},{'profile.imageUrl':{$in:['',null]}},{cuisine:{$in:['',null]}}]}).select('name cuisine address profile').limit(100));}

server/src/controllers/restaurantController.js

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';
import {Restaurant,Review,ViewEvent,Claim,Suggestion,Report,OwnerMessage} from '../models/index.js';
import {ok,id,pagination,clean} from '../utils/http.js';
import {buildRestaurantQuery,sorting,presentRestaurant,isOpenNow,publicFilter,filterAvailability} from '../utils/restaurant.js';
import {env} from '../config/env.js';
import {canEdit,isStaff} from '../middleware/auth.js';
import {validateRestaurant} from '../validators/restaurantValidators.js';
import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
import {audit,notify,notifyStaff} from '../utils/audit.js';
export async function getRecord(req,{deleted=false}={}){const r=await Restaurant.findOne({_id:id(req.params.id),...(deleted?{}:{isDeleted:{$ne:true}})});if(!r)throw new ApiError(404,'We could not find this restaurant. It may have been deleted.');return r;}
export async function list(req,res){const f=buildRestaurantQuery(req.query),p=pagination(req.query);let data,total;
 if(req.query.open==='now'){const all=await Restaurant.find(f).sort(sorting(req.query)).lean();const filtered=all.filter(r=>isOpenNow(r)===true);total=filtered.length;data=filtered.slice(p.skip,p.skip+p.limit);}else{[data,total]=await Promise.all([Restaurant.find(f).sort(sorting(req.query)).skip(p.skip).limit(p.limit).lean(),Restaurant.countDocuments(f)]);}
 ok(res,data.map(presentRestaurant),{page:p.page,limit:p.limit,total,pages:Math.ceil(total/p.limit)});}
export async function detail(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);if(r.approvalStatus!=='approved'&&!isStaff(req.user)&&String(r.ownerId)!==String(req.user?._id)&&String(r.createdBy)!==String(req.user?._id))throw new ApiError(404,'Restaurant not found.');ok(res,presentRestaurant(r));}
async function duplicate(b,exclude){const name=new RegExp('^'+b.name.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')+'$','i');if(await Restaurant.exists({name,'address.street':b.address.street,'address.building':b.address.building||'',isDeleted:{$ne:true},...(exclude?{_id:{$ne:exclude}}:{})}))throw new ApiError(409,'A restaurant with this name and address already exists.');}
export async function create(req,res){const b=validateRestaurant(req.body);if(!isStaff(req.user))for(const k of ['isFeatured','featuredStart','featuredEnd'])delete b[k];await duplicate(b);const r=await Restaurant.create({...b,restaurant_id:'BF-'+crypto.randomUUID(),countrySlug:b.country.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'-'),createdBy:req.user?._id,ownerId:req.user?.role==='owner'?req.user._id:undefined,isClaimed:req.user?.role==='owner',approvalStatus:req.user?.role==='owner'?'pending':'approved'});await audit(req,'create','restaurant',r._id,null,r.toObject());if(r.approvalStatus==='pending')await notifyStaff('Restaurant awaiting approval',r.name,'/admin/restaurants');ok(res,presentRestaurant(r),{},201);}
export async function update(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);canEdit(req,r);const b=validateRestaurant(req.body);if(!isStaff(req.user))for(const k of ['isFeatured','featuredStart','featuredEnd'])delete b[k];await duplicate(b,r._id);const before=r.toObject();Object.assign(r,b,{countrySlug:b.country.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g,'-'),updatedBy:req.user?._id});r.profileCompletion=Math.round(['tagline','story','openingHours','imageUrl','phone','website'].filter(k=>r.profile?.[k]).length/6*100);await r.save();await audit(req,'update','restaurant',r._id,before,r.toObject());ok(res,presentRestaurant(r));}
export async function remove(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);canEdit(req,r);const before=r.toObject();r.isDeleted=true;r.deletedAt=new Date();r.deletedBy=req.user?._id;await r.save();await audit(req,'delete','restaurant',r._id,before,r.toObject());ok(res,{id:r._id,message:'Restaurant deleted.'});}
export async function restore(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req,{deleted:true});r.isDeleted=false;r.deletedAt=undefined;r.deletedBy=undefined;await r.save();await audit(req,'restore','restaurant',r._id,null,r.toObject());ok(res,presentRestaurant(r));}
export async function status(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);canEdit(req,r);if(!['open','temporarily_closed','permanently_closed'].includes(req.body.status))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose a valid restaurant status.');r.status=req.body.status;await r.save();await audit(req,'status','restaurant',r._id,null,{status:r.status});ok(res,presentRestaurant(r));}
export async function approval(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);if(!['approved','rejected'].includes(req.body.approvalStatus))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose approved or rejected.');r.approvalStatus=req.body.approvalStatus;await r.save();await audit(req,'approval','restaurant',r._id,null,{approvalStatus:r.approvalStatus});if(r.ownerId)await notify(r.ownerId,'Restaurant decision',`${r.name}: ${r.approvalStatus}`,'/owner/restaurants');ok(res,presentRestaurant(r));}
export async function random(req,res){const f={...buildRestaurantQuery(req.query),status:'open'};let r;if(req.query.open==='now'){const a=(await Restaurant.find(f).lean()).filter(x=>isOpenNow(x)===true);r=a[Math.floor(Math.random()*a.length)];}else [r]=await Restaurant.aggregate([{$match:f},{$sample:{size:1}}]);if(!r)throw new ApiError(404,'No restaurants match these filters.');ok(res,presentRestaurant(r));}
export async function filters(req,res){
 const records=await Restaurant.find(publicFilter()).select('name borough cuisine country countrySlug priceLevel dietary avgRating grades.grade status hours specialHours timezone profile.signatureDishes menuPreview.name').lean();
 const availability=filterAvailability(records,req.query);
 ok(res,{boroughs:Object.keys(availability.borough),cuisines:Object.keys(availability.cuisine),countries:Object.keys(availability.country),availability});
}
export async function byIds(req,res){const ids=String(req.query.ids||'').split(',').filter(Boolean);if(ids.length>100)throw new ApiError(422,'Choose up to 100 restaurants.');ids.forEach(id);if(req.path==='/compare'&&(ids.length<2||ids.length>3))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose two or three restaurants to compare.');const rows=await Restaurant.find({...publicFilter(),_id:{$in:ids}}).lean();ok(res,ids.map(i=>rows.find(r=>String(r._id)===i)).filter(Boolean).map(presentRestaurant));}
export async function discovery(req,res){const type=req.params.kind||req.path.slice(1),f=publicFilter();if(type==='featured'){f.isFeatured=true;f.$and=[{$or:[{featuredStart:null},{featuredStart:{$lte:new Date()}}]},{$or:[{featuredEnd:null},{featuredEnd:{$gte:new Date()}}]}];}if(type==='top-rated'){f.reviewCount={$gte:2};f.status='open';}let rows;
 if(type==='trending'){rows=await ViewEvent.aggregate([{$match:{createdAt:{$gte:new Date(Date.now()-7*86400000)}}},{$group:{_id:'$restaurant',recentViews:{$sum:1}}},{$sort:{recentViews:-1}},{$limit:12}]);const all=await Restaurant.find({...f,_id:{$in:rows.map(x=>x._id)}}).lean();rows=rows.map(x=>all.find(r=>String(r._id)===String(x._id))).filter(Boolean);}else rows=await Restaurant.find(f).sort(type==='most-viewed'?{viewCount:-1}:type==='top-rated'?{avgRating:-1,reviewCount:-1}:{createdAt:-1}).limit(12).lean();ok(res,rows.map(presentRestaurant));}
export async function suggest(req,res){
 const f=buildRestaurantQuery({...req.query,search:req.query.q});
 const records=await Restaurant.find(f).select('name cuisine profile.signatureDishes menuPreview.name').limit(12).lean();
 const needle=String(req.query.q||'').toLowerCase(),labels=new Set();
 for(const r of records){const values=req.query.field==='cuisine'?[r.cuisine]:req.query.field==='dish'?[...(r.profile?.signatureDishes||[]),...(r.menuPreview||[]).map(item=>item.name)]:[r.name];for(const value of values)if(value&&(req.query.field==='dish'||req.query.field==='cuisine'?value.toLowerCase().includes(needle):true))labels.add(value);}
 ok(res,[...labels].slice(0,8).map((name,index)=>({_id:String(index),name})));
}
export async function similar(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);ok(res,(await Restaurant.find({...publicFilter(),_id:{$ne:r._id},cuisine:r.cuisine,$or:[{borough:r.borough},{country:r.country}]}).limit(4).lean()).map(presentRestaurant));}
export async function view(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);if(r.approvalStatus!=='approved')throw new ApiError(404,'Restaurant not found.');const sessionId=String(req.body.sessionId||'').slice(0,80);if(!sessionId)throw new ApiError(422,'A session id is required.');try{await ViewEvent.create({restaurant:r._id,sessionId});await Restaurant.updateOne({_id:r._id},{$inc:{viewCount:1}});}catch(e){if(e.code!==11000)throw e;}ok(res,{recorded:true});}
export async function claim(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);if(r.isClaimed)throw new ApiError(409,'This restaurant has already been claimed.');if(await Claim.exists({restaurant:r._id,user:req.user._id,status:'pending'}))throw new ApiError(409,'Your claim is already pending.');if(!req.user.emailVerified)throw new ApiError(403,'Please verify your email first.');const c=await Claim.create({restaurant:r._id,user:req.user._id,evidenceType:clean(req.body.evidenceType),evidenceUrl:clean(req.body.evidenceUrl),note:clean(req.body.note)});await notifyStaff('New ownership claim',r.name,'/admin/claims');ok(res,c,{},201);}
export async function report(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);if(!req.body.reason?.trim())throw new ApiError(422,'Please give a reason.');const item=await Report.create({type:req.body.type==='closed'?'closed':'restaurant_info',targetId:r._id,reporter:req.user._id,reason:clean(req.body.reason).slice(0,500),details:clean(req.body.details)});await notifyStaff('New restaurant report',r.name,'/admin/moderation');ok(res,item,{},201);}
export async function suggestions(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);if(req.method==='GET'){canEdit(req,r);return ok(res,await Suggestion.find({restaurant:r._id}).sort({createdAt:-1}));}if(req.method==='POST'){if(!req.user.emailVerified)throw new ApiError(403,'Please verify your email first.');if(!['name','cuisine','borough','city','profile.openingHours','profile.phone'].includes(req.body.field))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose an editable field.');if(!req.body.proposedValue?.trim())throw new ApiError(422,'Please enter a proposed value.');const s=await Suggestion.create({restaurant:r._id,user:req.user._id,field:req.body.field,currentValue:String(req.body.currentValue||''),proposedValue:clean(req.body.proposedValue).slice(0,120)});await notifyStaff('Suggested edit',r.name,'/admin/moderation');return ok(res,s,{},201);}canEdit(req,r);const s=await Suggestion.findOne({_id:id(req.body.id),restaurant:r._id,status:'pending'});if(!s)throw new ApiError(404,'Suggestion not found.');s.status=req.body.status==='approved'?'approved':'rejected';s.reviewedBy=req.user._id;if(s.status==='approved'){const before=r.toObject();r.set(s.field,s.proposedValue);await r.save();await audit(req,'suggestion','restaurant',r._id,before,r.toObject());}await s.save();await notify(s.user,'Suggested edit reviewed',s.status);ok(res,s);}
export async function contactOwner(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);if(!r.ownerId)throw new ApiError(422,'This listing has no verified owner yet.');if(!req.body.message||req.body.message.length<10||req.body.message.length>1000)throw new ApiError(422,'Please write a message of 10 to 1,000 characters.');const m=await OwnerMessage.create({restaurant:r._id,name:req.user.name,email:req.user.email,message:clean(req.body.message)});await notify(r.ownerId,'New message',r.name,'/owner/messages');ok(res,m,{},201);}
export async function images(req,res){const r=await getRecord(req);canEdit(req,r);if(!req.file)throw new ApiError(422,'Please upload a JPEG, PNG or WebP image.');const bytes=await fs.readFile(req.file.path);const valid=req.file.mimetype==='image/jpeg'?bytes[0]===255&&bytes[1]===216&&bytes[2]===255:req.file.mimetype==='image/png'?bytes.subarray(0,8).equals(Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10])):bytes.toString('ascii',0,4)==='RIFF'&&bytes.toString('ascii',8,12)==='WEBP';if(!valid){await fs.unlink(req.file.path);throw new ApiError(422,'The file content must match its image type.');}if(r.gallery.length>=10){await fs.unlink(req.file.path);throw new ApiError(422,'A gallery can contain at most ten images.');}const url='/uploads/'+req.file.filename;r.gallery.push({url,alt:clean(req.body.alt)||`Photo of ${r.name}`});await r.save();await audit(req,'image-upload','restaurant',r._id,null,{url});ok(res,{url}, {},201);}

server/src/controllers/reviewController.js

import {Review,Restaurant,Report} from '../models/index.js';
import {ok,id,clean} from '../utils/http.js';
import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
import {isStaff,canEdit} from '../middleware/auth.js';
import {notify,notifyStaff,audit} from '../utils/audit.js';
import {getRecord} from './restaurantController.js';
export async function updateRating(restaurant){const [x]=await Review.aggregate([{$match:{restaurant,status:'published'}},{$group:{_id:'$restaurant',avgRating:{$avg:'$rating'},reviewCount:{$sum:1}}}]);await Restaurant.updateOne({_id:restaurant},{avgRating:x?.avgRating||0,reviewCount:x?.reviewCount||0});}
export async function list(req,res){await getRecord(req);const sort={highest:{rating:-1},lowest:{rating:1},helpful:{helpfulUsers:-1}}[req.query.sort]||{createdAt:-1};ok(res,await Review.find({restaurant:req.params.id,status:'published'}).sort(sort).limit(100));}
function validated(b){if(!Number.isInteger(Number(b.rating))||b.rating<1||b.rating>5)throw new ApiError(422,'Rating must be a number from 1 to 5.');if(!b.comment?.trim()||b.comment.length>300)throw new ApiError(422,'Please write a comment of 1 to 300 characters.');return{rating:Number(b.rating),comment:clean(b.comment),title:clean(b.title||'').slice(0,100),status:/(https?:\/\/|buy now|free money)/i.test(b.comment)?'pending':'published'};}
export async function create(req,res){if(!req.user.emailVerified)throw new ApiError(403,'Please verify your email before posting reviews.');const r=await getRecord(req);if(r.approvalStatus!=='approved')throw new ApiError(404,'Restaurant not found.');if(await Review.exists({user:req.user._id,restaurant:r._id}))throw new ApiError(409,'You already reviewed this restaurant. Edit your existing review.');const x=await Review.create({...validated(req.body),restaurant:r._id,user:req.user._id,author:req.user.name});await updateRating(r._id);if(r.ownerId)await notify(r.ownerId,'New review',`${req.user.name} reviewed ${r.name}`,'/owner/reviews');if(x.status==='pending')await notifyStaff('Review awaiting moderation',r.name,'/admin/moderation');ok(res,x,{},201);}
async function find(req){const x=await Review.findById(id(req.params.id));if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Review not found.');return x;}
export async function update(req,res){if(!isStaff(req.user)&&!req.user.emailVerified)throw new ApiError(403,'Please verify your email before editing reviews.');const x=await find(req);if(!isStaff(req.user)&&String(x.user)!==String(req.user._id))throw new ApiError(403,'You do not have permission to do that.');if(isStaff(req.user)&&req.body.status){if(!['published','hidden','pending'].includes(req.body.status))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose a valid review status.');x.status=req.body.status;}else Object.assign(x,validated(req.body));await x.save();await updateRating(x.restaurant);await audit(req,'review-update','review',x._id,null,x.toObject());ok(res,x);}
export async function remove(req,res){const x=await find(req);if(!isStaff(req.user)&&String(x.user)!==String(req.user._id))throw new ApiError(403,'You do not have permission to do that.');await x.deleteOne();await updateRating(x.restaurant);await audit(req,'review-delete','review',x._id,x.toObject(),null);ok(res,{deleted:true});}
export async function helpful(req,res){const x=await find(req);if(x.status!=='published')throw new ApiError(404,'Review not found.');await Review.updateOne({_id:x._id},{$addToSet:{helpfulUsers:req.user._id}});ok(res,{message:'Thank you for your vote.'});}
export async function reply(req,res){const x=await find(req),r=await Restaurant.findById(x.restaurant);if(!r)throw new ApiError(404,'Restaurant not found.');canEdit(req,r);if(!req.body.text?.trim())throw new ApiError(422,'Please write a reply.');x.ownerReply={text:clean(req.body.text).slice(0,500),repliedAt:new Date()};await x.save();ok(res,x);}
export async function report(req,res){const x=await find(req);if(!req.body.reason?.trim())throw new ApiError(422,'Please give a reason.');const r=await Report.create({type:'review',targetId:x._id,reporter:req.user._id,reason:clean(req.body.reason).slice(0,500)});await notifyStaff('Review reported',r.reason,'/admin/moderation');ok(res,r,{},201);}

server/src/controllers/statsController.js

import {Restaurant} from '../models/index.js';
import {ok,pagination} from '../utils/http.js';
import {buildRestaurantQuery,topQualityPipeline,groupPipeline} from '../utils/restaurant.js';
import {env} from '../config/env.js';
import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
export async function topQuality(req,res){const min=req.query.min==null?env.min:Number(req.query.min);if(!Number.isFinite(min)||min<0)throw new ApiError(422,'Please enter a valid minimum score.');const {limit}=pagination(req.query);const data=await Restaurant.aggregate(topQualityPipeline(buildRestaurantQuery(req.query),min,limit));ok(res,data,{min,limit});}
export const group=field=>async(req,res)=>ok(res,await Restaurant.aggregate(groupPipeline(field,buildRestaurantQuery(req.query))));

server/src/controllers/submissionController.js

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import {RestaurantSuggestion,Restaurant,User} from '../models/index.js';
import {ok,id,clean,safeUser} from '../utils/http.js';
import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
import {notify,notifyStaff,audit} from '../utils/audit.js';
const text=(b,k,max=2000)=>clean(String(b[k]||'')).slice(0,max);
function details(b){const d={};for(const k of ['name','cuisine','address','contact','website','openingHours','description','photo'])d[k]=text(b,k,k==='description'?600:k==='name'?120:500);for(const k of ['name','cuisine','address','contact','openingHours','description'])if(!d[k])throw new ApiError(422,`Please enter ${k==='openingHours'?'opening hours':k}.`);for(const k of ['website','photo'])if(d[k]&&!(k==='photo'&&(/^\/images\/photos\/(bakery|bistro|burger|coffee|curry|dining|grill|pasta|pizza|ramen|salad|sushi|table|taco)\.jpg$/.test(d[k])||/^\/uploads\/[a-f0-9-]+\.(jpg|png|webp)$/.test(d[k])))){try{const u=new URL(d[k]);if(!['https:','http:'].includes(u.protocol))throw Error();}catch{throw new ApiError(422,`${k} must be an http or https URL.`);}}return d;}
export async function submit(req,res){const d=details(req.body);const escape=s=>s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');const name=new RegExp('^'+escape(d.name)+'$','i');const existing=await Restaurant.exists({name,isDeleted:{$ne:true}});const other=await RestaurantSuggestion.exists({'details.name':name,status:'pending'});if(await RestaurantSuggestion.exists({user:req.user._id,'details.name':name,'details.address':d.address,status:'pending'}))throw new ApiError(409,'You already have a pending suggestion for this restaurant.');const warning=existing||other?'Possible duplicate: a listing or suggestion with this name already exists. An administrator will check it.':'';const x=await RestaurantSuggestion.create({user:req.user._id,details:d,duplicateWarning:warning});await notifyStaff('New restaurant suggestion',d.name,'/admin/suggestions');ok(res,{suggestion:x,duplicateWarning:warning,message:'Your restaurant suggestion has been submitted. It will appear publicly once approved by an administrator.'},{},201);}
export async function list(req,res){ok(res,await RestaurantSuggestion.find(req.user.role==='admin'?{}:{user:req.user._id}).populate('user','name email').sort({createdAt:-1}).limit(100));}
export async function decide(req,res){let x=await RestaurantSuggestion.findById(id(req.params.id));if(!x)throw new ApiError(404,'Suggestion not found.');if(x.status!=='pending')throw new ApiError(409,'This suggestion has already been reviewed.');const status=req.body.status;if(!['approved','rejected'].includes(status))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose approved or rejected.');const reason=text(req.body,'reason',1000);if(status==='rejected'&&!reason)throw new ApiError(422,'Please provide a rejection reason.');const d=status==='approved'?details(req.body.details||x.details):x.details;
 // Keep the submission pending until publication completes; leases recover interrupted decisions.
 const lock=crypto.randomUUID();
 x=await RestaurantSuggestion.findOneAndUpdate({_id:x._id,status:'pending',$or:[{reviewLockUntil:null},{reviewLockUntil:{$lt:new Date()}}]},{$set:{reviewLock:lock,reviewLockUntil:new Date(Date.now()+60000)}},{new:true});
 if(!x)throw new ApiError(409,'Another administrator is reviewing this suggestion. Please try again shortly.');
 try{
  const existing=await Restaurant.findOne({restaurant_id:'SUG-'+x._id});
  if(existing&&status==='rejected')throw new ApiError(409,'Publication already started. Approve to finish this decision, then manage the public listing separately.');
  let listing=existing;
  if(status==='approved'&&!listing)listing=await Restaurant.findOneAndUpdate({restaurant_id:'SUG-'+x._id},{$setOnInsert:{name:d.name,cuisine:d.cuisine,borough:'Not specified',country:'Philippines',countrySlug:'philippines',address:{street:d.address},profile:{phone:d.contact,website:d.website,openingHours:d.openingHours,story:d.description,imageUrl:d.photo||'/images/photos/dining.jpg'},approvalStatus:'approved',createdBy:req.user._id}},{upsert:true,new:true,runValidators:true});
  x=await RestaurantSuggestion.findOneAndUpdate({_id:x._id,reviewLock:lock},{$set:{status,details:listing?{...d,name:listing.name,cuisine:listing.cuisine,address:listing.address.street,description:listing.profile.story}:d,rejectReason:reason,listing:listing?._id,reviewedBy:req.user._id,reviewedAt:new Date()},$unset:{reviewLock:1,reviewLockUntil:1}},{new:true});
  if(!x)throw new ApiError(409,'The review lease expired. Refresh and try again.');
 }catch(e){await RestaurantSuggestion.updateOne({_id:req.params.id,reviewLock:lock},{$unset:{reviewLock:1,reviewLockUntil:1}});throw e;}
 await notify(x.user,'Restaurant suggestion '+status,status==='approved'?`Your suggestion for ${d.name} was approved and is now public.`:`Your suggestion for ${d.name} was rejected: ${reason}`,'/account/suggestions');await audit(req,'restaurant-suggestion-decision','suggestion',x._id,null,x.toObject());ok(res,x);}
export async function ownerDecision(req,res){const u=await User.findOne({_id:id(req.params.id),role:'owner'});if(!u)throw new ApiError(404,'Owner application not found.');const status=req.body.status,reason=text(req.body,'reason',1000);if(!['approved','rejected','pending'].includes(status))throw new ApiError(422,'Choose a valid application status.');if(status==='rejected'&&!reason)throw new ApiError(422,'Please provide a rejection reason.');u.set('ownerProfile.verificationStatus',status);u.set('ownerProfile.rejectionReason',status==='rejected'?reason:'');await u.save();await notify(u._id,'Owner application '+status,status==='approved'?'Your owner account has been approved. You can now add and manage your restaurant listings.':status==='rejected'?'Your owner application was rejected: '+reason:'Your owner application is pending administrator review.','/owner');await audit(req,'owner-decision','user',u._id,null,safeUser(u));ok(res,safeUser(u));}

export async function photo(req,res){
 if(!req.file)throw new ApiError(422,'Choose a JPEG, PNG or WebP photo.');
 const bytes=await fs.readFile(req.file.path);
 const valid=req.file.mimetype==='image/jpeg'?bytes[0]===255&&bytes[1]===216&&bytes[2]===255:req.file.mimetype==='image/png'?bytes.subarray(0,8).equals(Buffer.from([137,80,78,71,13,10,26,10])):bytes.toString('ascii',0,4)==='RIFF'&&bytes.toString('ascii',8,12)==='WEBP';
 if(!valid){await fs.unlink(req.file.path);throw new ApiError(422,'The photo content must match its file type.');}
 ok(res,{url:'/uploads/'+req.file.filename},{},201);
}

server/src/middleware/asyncHandler.js

// Wraps an async route handler so rejected promises reach the error handler.
export const asyncHandler=fn=>(req,res,next)=>Promise.resolve(fn(req,res,next)).catch(next);
export default asyncHandler;

server/src/middleware/auth.js

import jwt from 'jsonwebtoken';
import {User} from '../models/index.js';
import {env} from '../config/env.js';
import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
export async function optionalAuth(req,res,next){try{const raw=req.headers.authorization;if(raw?.startsWith('Bearer ')){let token;try{token=jwt.verify(raw.slice(7),env.secret);}catch{throw new ApiError(401,'Your session has ended. Please log in again.');}const u=await User.findById(token.sub);if(!u||u.status!=='active'||u.tokenVersion!==token.ver)throw new ApiError(401,'Your session has ended. Please log in again.');req.user=u;}next();}catch(e){next(e);}}
export function requireUser(req,res,next){if(!req.user)return next(new ApiError(401,'Please log in to continue.'));next();}
export const roles=(...r)=>(req,res,next)=>{if(!req.user)return next(new ApiError(401,'Please log in to continue.'));if(!r.includes(req.user.role))return next(new ApiError(403,'You do not have permission to do that.'));next();};
export const isStaff=u=>['admin','moderator'].includes(u?.role);
export const canManage=u=>u?.role==='admin'||(u?.role==='owner'&&u.emailVerified&&u.ownerProfile?.verificationStatus==='approved');
export function requireManager(req,res,next){if(!req.user)return next(new ApiError(401,'Please log in to continue.'));if(!canManage(req.user))return next(new ApiError(403,'Owners need verified email and administrator approval to manage listings.'));next();}
export function requireVerified(req,res,next){if(!req.user)return next(new ApiError(401,'Please log in to continue.'));if(!req.user.emailVerified)return next(new ApiError(403,'Please verify your email first.'));next();}
export function canEdit(req,r){if(req.user?.role==='admin')return;if(canManage(req.user)&&String(r.ownerId)===String(req.user._id))return;throw new ApiError(403,'You do not have permission to manage this restaurant.');}

server/src/middleware/errorHandler.js

export function errorHandler(err,req,res,next){let status=err.status||500,message=err.message,errors=err.errors||[];if(err.code==='LIMIT_FILE_SIZE'){status=422;message='Upload an image of 5 MB or less.';}if(err.code===11000){status=409;message='A record with this identifier already exists.';}if(err.name==='CastError'){status=400;message='Invalid id or value.';}if(err.name==='ValidationError'){status=422;errors=Object.values(err.errors).map(e=>({field:e.path,message:e.message}));message='Please check the highlighted fields.';}if(status===500){console.error(err);message='Something went wrong on our side. Please try again in a moment.';}res.status(status).json({success:false,message,errors:Array.isArray(errors)?errors:[]});}

server/src/middleware/notFound.js

import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
// Friendly JSON 404 for unknown /api routes.
export function notFound(req,res,next){next(new ApiError(404,'API route not found.'));}

server/src/middleware/rateLimiter.js

import rateLimit from 'express-rate-limit';
const limiter=(max,minutes)=>rateLimit({windowMs:minutes*60000,limit:max,standardHeaders:'draft-7',legacyHeaders:false,message:{success:false,message:'Too many requests. Please wait a moment and try again.',errors:[]}});
export const generalLimit=limiter(400,15),loginLimit=limiter(25,15),publicLimit=limiter(15,15),reviewLimit=limiter(10,15);

server/src/middleware/validate.js

import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
// validate(fn) runs a validator that returns the cleaned body or throws, and stores it on req.body.
export const validate=fn=>(req,res,next)=>{try{req.body=fn(req.body);next();}catch(e){next(e instanceof ApiError?e:new ApiError(422,e.message));}};

server/src/models/index.js

import mongoose from 'mongoose';
const {Schema}=mongoose,ref=(model)=>({type:Schema.Types.ObjectId,ref:model});
const make=(name,shape,indexes=[],options={})=>{const s=new Schema(shape,{timestamps:true,...options});for(const [fields,opts] of indexes)s.index(fields,opts);return mongoose.models[name]||mongoose.model(name,s);};
export const Restaurant=make('Restaurant',{
 restaurant_id:{type:String,unique:true,required:true},name:{type:String,required:true,trim:true,maxlength:120},borough:{type:String,required:true,trim:true},cuisine:{type:String,required:true,trim:true},country:{type:String,default:'United States'},countrySlug:{type:String,default:'united-states'},city:String,
 address:{building:String,street:{type:String,required:true},zipcode:String,coord:{type:[Number],default:undefined}},grades:[{date:Date,grade:{type:String,enum:['A','B','C','Z']},score:{type:Number,min:0,max:200}}],
 profile:{tagline:String,story:String,signatureDishes:[String],priceRange:String,openingHours:String,dressCode:String,reservations:String,dietaryOptions:[String],tags:[String],funFact:String,imageUrl:String,website:String,phone:String,email:String,bookingLink:String},
 isFeatured:{type:Boolean,default:false},featuredStart:Date,featuredEnd:Date,status:{type:String,enum:['open','temporarily_closed','permanently_closed'],default:'open'},approvalStatus:{type:String,enum:['approved','pending','rejected'],default:'approved'},isDeleted:{type:Boolean,default:false},deletedAt:Date,deletedBy:ref('User'),ownerId:ref('User'),isClaimed:{type:Boolean,default:false},createdBy:ref('User'),updatedBy:ref('User'),lastVerifiedAt:Date,
 hours:[{day:Number,open:String,close:String,closed:Boolean}],specialHours:[{date:String,open:String,close:String,closed:Boolean,note:String}],timezone:{type:String,default:'America/New_York'},priceLevel:{type:Number,min:1,max:4,default:2},dietary:[String],allergens:[String],amenities:[String],occasions:[String],paymentMethods:[String],accessibility:{wheelchair:Boolean,stepFree:Boolean,accessibleRestroom:Boolean},social:{facebook:String,instagram:String,tiktok:String,x:String},gallery:[{url:String,alt:String}],menuPreview:[{name:String,price:Number,description:String}],avgRating:{type:Number,default:0},reviewCount:{type:Number,default:0},viewCount:{type:Number,default:0},saveCount:{type:Number,default:0},profileCompletion:{type:Number,default:0},announcement:{text:String,expiresAt:Date}
},[[{name:'text',cuisine:'text'}],[{borough:1}],[{cuisine:1}],[{countrySlug:1}],[{status:1,approvalStatus:1,isDeleted:1}],[{avgRating:-1}],[{viewCount:-1}],[{'address.coord':'2dsphere'},{sparse:true}]]);
export const Country=make('Country',{name:String,slug:{type:String,unique:true},isoCode:String,continent:String,capital:String,tagline:String,overview:String,signatureDishes:[{name:String,description:String}],mealRhythm:String,tipping:String,bestSeason:String,etiquette:[String],festivals:[{name:String,when:String,description:String}],phrase:{local:String,meaning:String,language:String},funFact:String,heroImage:String,isFeatured:Boolean});
export const Guide=make('Guide',{title:String,slug:{type:String,unique:true},excerpt:String,body:String,countrySlug:String,tags:[String],readTimeMinutes:Number,publishedAt:{type:Date,default:Date.now},coverImage:String});
export const Faq=make('Faq',{question:String,answer:String,category:String,order:Number});
export const Message=make('Message',{name:String,email:String,subject:String,message:String,status:{type:String,default:'new'}},[[{createdAt:1},{expireAfterSeconds:31536000}]]);
export const Subscriber=make('Subscriber',{email:{type:String,unique:true,lowercase:true},isActive:{type:Boolean,default:true},subscribedAt:{type:Date,default:Date.now}});
export const Review=make('Review',{restaurant:ref('Restaurant'),user:ref('User'),author:String,title:String,rating:{type:Number,min:1,max:5},comment:String,subRatings:{food:Number,service:Number,ambiance:Number,value:Number},helpfulUsers:[ref('User')],status:{type:String,enum:['published','pending','hidden'],default:'published'},photos:[String],ownerReply:{text:String,repliedAt:Date}},[[{restaurant:1,user:1},{unique:true}]]);
export const User=make('User',{name:String,email:{type:String,unique:true,lowercase:true},passwordHash:{type:String,select:false},role:{type:String,enum:['customer','owner','moderator','admin'],default:'customer'},status:{type:String,enum:['active','suspended'],default:'active'},emailVerified:{type:Boolean,default:false},tokenVersion:{type:Number,default:0},lastLoginAt:Date,failedLoginCount:{type:Number,default:0},lockUntil:Date,avatar:String,ownerProfile:{businessName:String,phone:String,position:String,verificationStatus:{type:String,default:'pending'},rejectionReason:String},preferences:{emailAlerts:{type:Boolean,default:true},language:{type:String,default:'en'},theme:{type:String,default:'system'}},verifyHash:{type:String,select:false},verifyUntil:{type:Date,select:false},verifySentAt:Date,verifyAttempts:{type:Number,default:0,select:false},resetHash:{type:String,select:false},resetUntil:{type:Date,select:false},resetSentAt:{type:Date,select:false},resetAttempts:{type:Number,default:0,select:false}});
export const Claim=make('Claim',{restaurant:ref('Restaurant'),user:ref('User'),status:{type:String,default:'pending'},evidenceType:String,evidenceUrl:String,note:String,reviewedBy:ref('User'),reviewedAt:Date,rejectReason:String},[[{restaurant:1,status:1}]]);
export const Report=make('Report',{type:{type:String,enum:['review','restaurant_info','closed']},targetId:Schema.Types.ObjectId,reporter:ref('User'),reason:String,details:String,status:{type:String,default:'open'},handledBy:ref('User')});
export const Suggestion=make('Suggestion',{restaurant:ref('Restaurant'),user:ref('User'),field:String,currentValue:String,proposedValue:String,status:{type:String,default:'pending'},reviewedBy:ref('User')});
export const Notification=make('Notification',{user:ref('User'),type:String,title:String,message:String,link:String,isRead:{type:Boolean,default:false}},[[{user:1,isRead:1,createdAt:-1}]]);
export const ActivityLog=make('ActivityLog',{actor:ref('User'),role:String,action:String,entityType:String,entityId:String,before:Schema.Types.Mixed,after:Schema.Types.Mixed,ip:String},[[{createdAt:-1}],[{actor:1}]]);
export const LoginHistory=make('LoginHistory',{user:ref('User'),email:String,success:Boolean,ip:String,userAgent:String},[[{createdAt:-1}]]);
export const Favorite=make('Favorite',{user:ref('User'),restaurant:ref('Restaurant')},[[{user:1,restaurant:1},{unique:true}]]);
export const Collection=make('Collection',{user:ref('User'),name:String,isPublic:{type:Boolean,default:false},restaurants:[ref('Restaurant')]});
export const SearchHistory=make('SearchHistory',{user:ref('User'),query:String,filters:Schema.Types.Mixed});
export const ViewEvent=make('ViewEvent',{restaurant:ref('Restaurant'),sessionId:String},[[{restaurant:1,sessionId:1},{unique:true}],[{createdAt:1},{expireAfterSeconds:2592000}]]);
export const OwnerMessage=make('OwnerMessage',{restaurant:ref('Restaurant'),name:String,email:String,message:String,status:{type:String,default:'new'},reply:String});
export const Setting=make('Setting',{key:{type:String,unique:true},banner:String,bannerExpiresAt:Date,maintenance:{type:Boolean,default:false}});

export const RestaurantSuggestion=make('RestaurantSuggestion',{user:ref('User'),details:Schema.Types.Mixed,duplicateWarning:String,status:{type:String,enum:['pending','approved','rejected'],default:'pending'},rejectReason:String,reviewedBy:ref('User'),reviewedAt:Date,listing:ref('Restaurant'),reviewLock:{type:String,select:false},reviewLockUntil:{type:Date,select:false}});

server/src/routes/index.js

import {Router} from 'express';

import multer from 'multer';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import * as N from '../controllers/submissionController.js';
import * as R from '../controllers/restaurantController.js';
import * as S from '../controllers/statsController.js';

import ApiError from '../utils/ApiError.js';
export const uploads=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../uploads',import.meta.url));fs.mkdirSync(uploads,{recursive:true});
const storage=multer.diskStorage({destination:uploads,filename:(req,file,cb)=>cb(null,crypto.randomUUID()+({'image/jpeg':'.jpg','image/png':'.png','image/webp':'.webp'}[file.mimetype]||'.bin'))});
const upload=multer({storage,limits:{fileSize:5*1024*1024},fileFilter:(req,file,cb)=>{if(!['image/jpeg','image/png','image/webp'].includes(file.mimetype))return cb(new ApiError(422,'Upload a JPEG, PNG or WebP image (up to 5 MB).'));cb(null,true);}});
const api=Router(),staff=roles('admin','moderator'),admin=roles('admin');
const route=(method,url,fn,...middleware)=>api[method](url,...middleware,wrap(fn));
route('get','/health',async(req,res)=>ok(res,{server:'ok',demo:process.env.DEMO_MODE==='true',simulatedEmail:!process.env.SMTP_HOST&&env.nodeEnv!=='production',database:mongoose.connection.readyState===1?'connected':'disconnected'}));

route('get','/restaurants/:id/reviews',V.list);route('post','/restaurants/:id/reviews',V.create,requireUser,reviewLimit);route('patch','/reviews/:id',V.update,requireUser,reviewLimit);route('delete','/reviews/:id',V.remove,requireUser);route('post','/reviews/:id/helpful',V.helpful,requireUser,reviewLimit);route('post','/reviews/:id/reply',V.reply,requireManager,reviewLimit);route('post','/reviews/:id/report',V.report,requireUser,publicLimit);

route('get','/admin/logs',D.records(M.ActivityLog,req=>req.user.role==='admin'?{}:{actor:req.user._id}),staff);route('get','/admin/login-history',D.records(M.LoginHistory),admin);route('get','/admin/users',D.records(M.User,req=>req.query.role==='owner'?{role:'owner'}:{}),admin);route('post','/admin/users',D.userAction,admin);route('patch','/admin/users/:id',D.userAction,admin);route('patch','/admin/users/:id/status',D.userAction,admin);route('get','/admin/reviews',D.records(M.Review),staff);route('get','/admin/subscribers',D.records(M.Subscriber),admin);route('get','/admin/quality-report',D.qualityReport,admin);route('get','/admin/restaurants/export',D.exportRestaurants,admin);route('post','/admin/restaurants/import',D.importRestaurants,admin);
