server/src/scripts/createAdmin.js

import bcrypt from 'bcryptjs';
import readline from 'node:readline/promises';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';
import {User} from '../models/index.js';
import {connectDB} from '../config/db.js';
const prompt=readline.createInterface({input:process.stdin,output:process.stdout});try{await connectDB();const email=(await prompt.question('Admin email: ')).trim().toLowerCase();const name=(await prompt.question('Admin name: ')).trim();const password=process.env.ADMIN_INITIAL_PASSWORD||await prompt.question('Initial password (visible in this terminal; use a private session): ');if(!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)||!name||password.length<8||!/[A-Za-z]/.test(password)||!/[0-9]/.test(password))throw new Error('Use a valid email, name, and password with 8+ characters, a letter and number.');if(await User.exists({email}))throw new Error('An account with this email already exists. Use Admin > Users to change its role.');await User.create({email,name,passwordHash:await bcrypt.hash(password,12),role:'admin',emailVerified:true});console.log('Admin created. Log in through the website.');}catch(e){console.error(e.message);process.exitCode=1;}finally{prompt.close();await mongoose.disconnect();}

server/src/scripts/importDbRestaurants.js

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';
import {Restaurant} from '../models/index.js';
import {connectDB} from '../config/db.js';
try{await connectDB();const file=process.argv[2];if(file){const raw=await fs.readFile(file,'utf8');let rows;try{rows=JSON.parse(raw);}catch{rows=raw.split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean).map(x=>JSON.parse(x));}if(!Array.isArray(rows))rows=[rows];for(const row of rows){delete row._id;row.grades=(row.grades||[]).map(g=>({...g,date:g.date?.$date||g.date}));if(!row.restaurant_id||!row.name)throw new Error('Each course record needs restaurant_id and name.');await Restaurant.updateOne({restaurant_id:String(row.restaurant_id)},{$setOnInsert:{...row,restaurant_id:String(row.restaurant_id),country:'United States',countrySlug:'united-states',city:'New York',approvalStatus:'approved',isDeleted:false,status:'open'}},{upsert:true});}console.log(`${rows.length} course records imported.`);}await Restaurant.updateMany({country:{$exists:false}},{$set:{country:'United States',countrySlug:'united-states',city:'New York'}});await Restaurant.updateMany({approvalStatus:{$exists:false}},{$set:{approvalStatus:'approved',isDeleted:false,status:'open'}});console.log('Course records enriched.');}catch(e){console.error(e.message);process.exitCode=1;}finally{await mongoose.disconnect();}

server/src/scripts/previewDemo.js

// Temporary local demo database for BonAppé Find.
// Starts an isolated MongoDB instance, seeds the project's real demo data,
// starts the API, and deletes the temporary database when the process exits.

import { MongoMemoryServer } from 'mongodb-memory-server';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';

const __filename = fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
const __dirname = path.dirname(__filename);
const projectRoot = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..', '..');
const seedDataDir = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', 'seed', 'data');

process.env.NODE_ENV = 'development';
process.env.DEMO_MODE = 'true';
process.env.JWT_SECRET =
  process.env.JWT_SECRET ||
  'bonappe-find-demo-secret-key-2026-secure-preview-only';
process.env.CLIENT_ORIGIN =
  process.env.CLIENT_ORIGIN || 'http://127.0.0.1:5173';
process.env.APP_URL =
  process.env.APP_URL || 'http://127.0.0.1:5173';
process.env.PORT = process.env.PORT || '5000';

const dbPath = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(projectRoot, '.preview-mongo-'));
console.log(`Demo database directory: ${dbPath}`);

let mongod;
let server;
let shuttingDown = false;

async function readSeed(name) {
  const file = path.join(seedDataDir, `${name}.json`);
  return JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(file, 'utf8'));
}

async function cleanup(exitCode = 0) {
  if (shuttingDown) return;
  shuttingDown = true;

  try {
    if (server) {
      await new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve));
    }
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Server shutdown warning:', error.message);
  }

  try {
    await mongoose.disconnect();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('MongoDB disconnect warning:', error.message);
  }

  try {
    if (mongod) await mongod.stop();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Demo MongoDB shutdown warning:', error.message);
  }

  try {
    await fs.rm(dbPath, { recursive: true, force: true });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Temporary-file cleanup warning:', error.message);
  }

  process.exit(exitCode);
}

try {
  mongod = await MongoMemoryServer.create({
    binary: {
      version: process.env.MONGOMS_VERSION || '7.0.24',
    },
    instance: {
      dbPath,
      // Windows keeps its original options; isolated Linux environments use TCP only.
      ...(process.platform==='win32'?{}:{args:['--nounixsocket']}),
    },
  });

  const mongoUri = mongod.getUri();
  process.env.MONGODB_URI = mongoUri;
  process.env.MONGO_URI = mongoUri;

  console.log('Demo MongoDB started successfully.');
  console.log(`MongoDB URI: ${mongoUri}`);

  await mongoose.connect(mongoUri, { dbName: 'bonappefind_preview' });
  console.log('MongoDB connected: bonappefind_preview');

  // Use the project's actual Mongoose models so the seeded documents
  // match the fields expected by the API and frontend.
  const M = await import('../models/index.js');

  const seedSets = [
    ['restaurants', M.Restaurant],
    ['countries', M.Country],
    ['guides', M.Guide],
    ['faqs', M.Faq],
  ];

  for (const [name, Model] of seedSets) {
    const rows = await readSeed(name);
    await Model.deleteMany({});
    if (rows.length) {
      await Model.insertMany(rows);
    }
    console.log(`Seeded ${rows.length} ${name}.`);
  }

  const {seedSampleReviews}=await import('../seed/sampleReviews.js');
  await seedSampleReviews(await readSeed('restaurants'));

  // Restore the project's original sample accounts.
  const bcrypt = (await import('bcryptjs')).default;
  const passwordHash = await bcrypt.hash('ReviewOnly123', 8);

  await M.User.deleteMany({
    email: {
      $in: [
        'reviewer@example.test',
        'owner@example.test',
        'customer@example.test',
      ],
    },
  });

  await M.User.create({
    name: 'Local reviewer',
    email: 'reviewer@example.test',
    passwordHash,
    role: 'admin',
    emailVerified: true,
  });

  const demoOwner = await M.User.create({
    name: 'Demo owner',
    email: 'owner@example.test',
    passwordHash,
    role: 'owner',
    emailVerified: true,
    ownerProfile: {
      businessName: 'Fictional demo restaurant',
      verificationStatus: 'approved',
    },
  });

  await M.User.create({
    name: 'Demo diner',
    email: 'customer@example.test',
    passwordHash,
    role: 'customer',
    emailVerified: true,
  });

  // Give the owner one of the seeded restaurants.
  await M.Restaurant.updateOne(
    { restaurant_id: 'BF-001' },
    {
      ownerId: demoOwner._id,
      createdBy: demoOwner._id,
      isClaimed: true,
    }
  );

  // Import app only after demo environment and database are ready.
  const { app } = await import('../app.js');
  const port = Number(process.env.PORT || 5000);

  server = app.listen(port, '127.0.0.1', () => {
    console.log('');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('       BonAppé Find — Demo Mode');
    console.log('==============================================');
    console.log('Demo database: SEEDED');
    console.log('Backend server: READY');
    console.log(`Backend API: http://127.0.0.1:${port}`);
    console.log('Frontend:    http://127.0.0.1:5173/');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Sample Accounts — For Checking');
    console.log('Customer: customer@example.test / ReviewOnly123');
    console.log('Owner:    owner@example.test / ReviewOnly123');
    console.log('Admin:    reviewer@example.test / ReviewOnly123');
    console.log('');
    console.log('Keep this terminal open while using BonAppé Find.');
  });
} catch (error) {
  console.error('');
  console.error('BonAppé Find demo startup failed:');
  console.error(error);
  await cleanup(1);
}

for (const signal of ['SIGINT', 'SIGTERM']) {
  process.on(signal, () => cleanup(0));
}

server/src/seed/seed.js

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';
import {seedSampleReviews} from './sampleReviews.js';
import {Country,Restaurant,Guide,Faq} from '../models/index.js';
import {connectDB} from '../config/db.js';
try{await connectDB();if(process.argv.includes('--reset-demo')){console.log('Resetting BF-001 to BF-016 only; course records and accounts are preserved.');await Restaurant.deleteMany({restaurant_id:/^BF-0(0[1-9]|1[0-6])$/});}
 for(const [name,Model,key]of[['countries',Country,'slug'],['restaurants',Restaurant,'restaurant_id'],['guides',Guide,'slug'],['faqs',Faq,'question']]){const data=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(new URL(`./data/${name}.json`,import.meta.url),'utf8'));const inserted=[];for(const row of data){const result=await Model.updateOne({[key]:row[key]},{$setOnInsert:row},{upsert:true,runValidators:true});if(result.upsertedCount)inserted.push(row);}if(name==='restaurants')await seedSampleReviews(inserted);console.log(`${data.length} ${name} ready (existing edits preserved).`);}console.log('Seed complete. No accounts or passwords were created.');}catch(e){console.error(e.message);process.exitCode=1;}finally{await mongoose.disconnect();}

server/src/server.js

import {app} from './app.js';
import {env,checkEnv} from './config/env.js';
import {connectDB} from './config/db.js';
import mongoose from 'mongoose';
try{checkEnv();await connectDB();const server=app.listen(env.port,()=>console.log(`BonAppé Find API running at http://localhost:${env.port}/api`));for(const signal of ['SIGINT','SIGTERM'])process.on(signal,()=>server.close(async()=>{await mongoose.disconnect();process.exit(0);}));}catch(e){console.error(e.message);process.exit(1);}

server/src/utils/ApiError.js

export default class ApiError extends Error{constructor(status,message,errors=[]){super(message);this.status=status;this.errors=errors;}}

server/src/utils/audit.js

import {ActivityLog,Notification,User} from '../models/index.js';
export const audit=(req,action,type,entityId,before,after)=>ActivityLog.create({actor:req.user?._id,role:req.user?.role||'visitor',action,entityType:type,entityId:String(entityId),before,after,ip:req.ip});
export const notify=(user,title,message,link='/account')=>Notification.create({user,type:'update',title,message,link});
export async function notifyStaff(title,message,link='/admin'){const users=await User.find({role:{$in:['admin','moderator']},status:'active'});await Promise.all(users.map(u=>notify(u._id,title,message,link)));}

server/src/utils/http.js

import mongoose from 'mongoose';
import ApiError from './ApiError.js';
export const ok=(res,data,meta={},status=200)=>res.status(status).json({success:true,data,meta});
export const wrap=fn=>(req,res,next)=>Promise.resolve(fn(req,res,next)).catch(next);
export const id=value=>{if(!mongoose.isValidObjectId(value))throw new ApiError(400,'Invalid id.');return value;};
export const pagination=q=>{const page=Math.max(1,Number.parseInt(q.page)||1),limit=Math.min(100,Math.max(1,Number.parseInt(q.limit)||20));return{page,limit,skip:(page-1)*limit};};
export const clean=s=>typeof s==='string'?s.replace(/[<>\u0000]/g,'').trim():s;
export function safeUser(user){const{passwordHash,resetHash,verifyHash,verifyUntil,verifyAttempts,resetUntil,__v,...data}=user.toObject?user.toObject():user;const rawId=String(data._id||'');return{...data,account_id:data.account_id||('ACC-'+rawId.toUpperCase())};}
export function check(errors){if(errors.length)throw new ApiError(422,'Please check the highlighted fields.',errors);}

server/src/utils/mail.js

import nodemailer from 'nodemailer';
import {env} from '../config/env.js';
export async function sendMail(to,subject,text){if(process.env.SMTP_HOST){const t=nodemailer.createTransport({host:process.env.SMTP_HOST,port:Number(process.env.SMTP_PORT||587),secure:Number(process.env.SMTP_PORT)===465,auth:process.env.SMTP_USER?{user:process.env.SMTP_USER,pass:process.env.SMTP_PASS}:undefined});await t.sendMail({from:process.env.MAIL_FROM,to,subject,text});return {delivered:true};}else if(env.nodeEnv!=='production'){console.log(`[DEV MAIL — SIMULATED, NO EMAIL SENT] To: ${to}\n${subject}\n${text}`);return {delivered:false};}else{throw new Error('SMTP_HOST is required for email delivery in production.');}}

server/src/utils/pagination.js

// Turns ?page= and ?limit= into safe skip/limit numbers (limit capped at 100).
export function paginate(query={},defaultLimit=20){
  const limit=Math.min(100,Math.max(1,parseInt(query.limit,10)||defaultLimit));
  const page=Math.max(1,parseInt(query.page,10)||1);
  return{page,limit,skip:(page-1)*limit};
}

server/src/utils/restaurant.js

import ApiError from './ApiError.js';
export const escapeRegex=s=>s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&');
export const publicFilter=()=>({isDeleted:{$ne:true},approvalStatus:'approved'});
export function buildRestaurantQuery(q={}){
 const f=publicFilter();
 for(const key of ['borough','cuisine','country','countrySlug'])if(typeof q[key]==='string'&&q[key])f[key]=q[key];
 if(q.search){const re=new RegExp(escapeRegex(String(q.search).slice(0,120)),'i');const fields=q.field==='name'?['name']:q.field==='cuisine'?['cuisine']:q.field==='dish'?['profile.signatureDishes','menuPreview.name']:['name','cuisine','profile.signatureDishes','menuPreview.name'];f.$or=fields.map(k=>({[k]:re}));}
 if(q.grade)f['grades.grade']=String(q.grade);if(q.price)f.priceLevel=Number(q.price);if(q.minRating)f.avgRating={$gte:Number(q.minRating)};if(q.dietary)f.dietary={$all:String(q.dietary).split(',')};if(q.status)f.status=String(q.status);if(q.open==='now')f.status='open';return f;
}
export const sorting=q=>({name:{name:1},'name-desc':{name:-1},rating:{avgRating:-1,reviewCount:-1},'rating-asc':{avgRating:1},newest:{createdAt:-1},views:{viewCount:-1},saves:{saveCount:-1}}[q.sort]||{name:1});
export function isOpenNow(r,now=new Date()){
 if(r.status!=='open')return false;if(!r.hours?.length)return null;
 let parts;try{parts=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US',{timeZone:r.timezone||'America/New_York',weekday:'short',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit',hourCycle:'h23',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).formatToParts(now);}catch{return null;}
 const obj=Object.fromEntries(parts.map(x=>[x.type,x.value]));const day=['Sun','Mon','Tue','Wed','Thu','Fri','Sat'].indexOf(obj.weekday),date=`${obj.year}-${obj.month}-${obj.day}`,time=`${obj.hour}:${obj.minute}`;
 const special=r.specialHours?.find(x=>x.date===date);if(special)return !special.closed&&time>=special.open&&time<special.close;
 const slots=r.hours.filter(x=>x.day===day&&!x.closed);if(slots.some(x=>x.close>x.open?time>=x.open&&time<x.close:time>=x.open))return true;
 return r.hours.some(x=>x.day===(day+6)%7&&!x.closed&&x.close<x.open&&time<x.close);
}
export function presentRestaurant(r){if(r?.toObject)r=r.toObject();const p=r.profile||{};return{...r,totalScore:(r.grades||[]).reduce((s,g)=>s+(Number(g.score)||0),0),openNow:isOpenNow(r),profile:{tagline:`A ${r.cuisine} favorite in ${r.borough}.`,story:`${r.name} serves ${r.cuisine} food in ${r.borough}, ${r.country}. A full story for this restaurant has not been written yet. Check its recorded grades and learn about the country before you go.`,signatureDishes:['Ask the staff for the house specialty.'],openingHours:'Please confirm hours with the restaurant.',dressCode:'Come as you are.',reservations:'Call ahead for large groups.',...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(p).filter(([,v])=>v!==''&&v!=null&&(!Array.isArray(v)||v.length)))}};}
export function topQualityPipeline(filters,min,limit){return[{$match:{...filters,status:'open'}},{$unwind:'$grades'},{$group:{_id:'$_id',name:{$first:'$name'},borough:{$first:'$borough'},cuisine:{$first:'$cuisine'},country:{$first:'$country'},address:{$first:'$address'},totalScore:{$sum:'$grades.score'},gradeCount:{$sum:1}}},{$match:{totalScore:{$gt:min}}},{$sort:{totalScore:-1,name:1}},{$limit:limit}];}
export function groupPipeline(field,filters){return[{$match:filters},{$addFields:{quality:{$sum:'$grades.score'}}},{$group:{_id:`$${field}`,count:{$sum:1},avgQuality:{$avg:'$quality'},views:{$sum:'$viewCount'},saves:{$sum:'$saveCount'}}},{$sort:{count:-1,_id:1}}];}

// Counts for available choices, excluding the dimension being changed.
export function filterAvailability(records,q={},now=new Date()){
 const values={borough:[...new Set(records.map(r=>r.borough))].sort(),country:[...new Set(records.map(r=>r.country))].sort(),cuisine:[...new Set(records.map(r=>r.cuisine))].sort(),price:['1','2','3','4'],dietary:['Halal','Vegan','Vegetarian','Gluten-Free'],minRating:['3','4','5'],grade:['A','B','C','Z'],open:['now']};
 function matches(r,filters){
  for(const key of ['borough','country','cuisine'])if(filters[key]&&r[key]!==filters[key])return false;
  if(filters.countrySlug&&r.countrySlug!==filters.countrySlug)return false;
  if(filters.status&&r.status!==filters.status)return false;
  if(filters.price&&r.priceLevel!==Number(filters.price))return false;
  if(filters.dietary&&!String(filters.dietary).split(',').every(value=>r.dietary?.includes(value)))return false;
  if(filters.minRating&&(r.avgRating||0)<Number(filters.minRating))return false;
  if(filters.grade&&!r.grades?.some(g=>g.grade===filters.grade))return false;
  if(filters.open==='now'&&isOpenNow(r,now)!==true)return false;
  if(filters.search){const needle=String(filters.search).slice(0,120).toLowerCase();const names=[r.name],cuisines=[r.cuisine],dishes=[...(r.profile?.signatureDishes||[]),...(r.menuPreview||[]).map(item=>item.name)];const fields=filters.field==='name'?names:filters.field==='cuisine'?cuisines:filters.field==='dish'?dishes:[...names,...cuisines,...dishes];if(!fields.some(text=>String(text||'').toLowerCase().includes(needle)))return false;}
  return true;
 }
 return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(values).map(([key,options])=>[key,Object.fromEntries(options.map(value=>[value,records.filter(r=>matches(r,{...q,[key]:value})).length]))]));
}

server/src/utils/slugify.js

export function slugify(text=''){return String(text).normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/^-+|-+$/g,'');}

server/src/validators/restaurantValidators.js

import {clean,check} from '../utils/http.js';
export function validateRestaurant(body){const e=[],add=(field,message)=>e.push({field,message});const b=structuredClone(body||{});for(const key of ['name','cuisine','country','borough','city'])b[key]=clean(b[key]);
 for(const [f,msg] of [['name','Please enter the restaurant name.'],['cuisine','Please enter a cuisine.'],['country','Please choose a country.'],['borough','Please enter a borough or region.']])if(typeof b[f]!=='string'||!b[f])add(f,msg);
 if(b.name?.length>120)add('name','The name must be 120 characters or fewer.');if(!clean(b.address?.street))add('address.street','Please enter the street.');
 if(b.address?.coord!=null&&(!Array.isArray(b.address.coord)||b.address.coord.length!==2||b.address.coord.some(v=>typeof v!=='number'||!Number.isFinite(v))||Math.abs(b.address.coord[0])>180||Math.abs(b.address.coord[1])>90))add('address.coord','Please enter valid numbers for longitude and latitude.');
 if(b.grades!=null&&!Array.isArray(b.grades))add('grades','Grades must be a list.');else for(const [i,g] of (b.grades||[]).entries()){if(!['A','B','C','Z'].includes(g.grade))add(`grades.${i}.grade`,'Grade must be A, B, C or Z.');if(typeof g.score!=='number'||!Number.isFinite(g.score)||g.score<0||g.score>200)add(`grades.${i}.score`,'Score must be a number from 0 to 200.');if(!g.date||!Number.isFinite(Date.parse(g.date)))add(`grades.${i}.date`,'Please enter a valid grade date.');}
 const p=b.profile||{};for(const [f,n,msg]of[['tagline',80,'The tagline must be 80 characters or fewer.'],['story',600,'The story must be 600 characters or fewer.'],['funFact',200,'The fun fact must be 200 characters or fewer.']])if(p[f]?.length>n)add('profile.'+f,msg);
 const urlFields=['profile.imageUrl','profile.website','profile.bookingLink',...Object.keys(b.social||{}).map(k=>'social.'+k)];for(const f of urlFields){const [a,z]=f.split('.'),v=b[a]?.[z];if(v&&!/^https?:\/\//i.test(v)&&!(f==='profile.imageUrl'&&/^\/images\/(?:photos\/)?[a-z0-9-]+\.(?:svg|jpg|jpeg|png|webp)$/.test(v)))add(f,'Please enter a link that starts with http:// or https://.');}
 if(b.priceLevel!=null&&![1,2,3,4].includes(Number(b.priceLevel)))add('priceLevel','Choose a price level from 1 to 4.');
 if(b.status&&!['open','temporarily_closed','permanently_closed'].includes(b.status))add('status','Choose a valid restaurant status.');
 if(b.timezone){try{new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en',{timeZone:b.timezone});}catch{add('timezone','Please enter a valid IANA time zone.');}}
 if(b.hours!=null&&!Array.isArray(b.hours))add('hours','Hours must be a list.');else for(const [i,h]of(b.hours||[]).entries()){if(!Number.isInteger(h.day)||h.day<0||h.day>6)add(`hours.${i}.day`,'Choose a valid weekday.');if(!h.closed&&(!/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(h.open)||!/^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$/.test(h.close)))add(`hours.${i}`,'Use HH:MM for opening and closing hours.');}
 if(b.menuPreview!=null&&!Array.isArray(b.menuPreview))add('menuPreview','Menu items must be a list.');else for(const [i,item]of(b.menuPreview||[]).entries()){if(!item.name?.trim())add(`menuPreview.${i}.name`,'Enter a dish name.');if(typeof item.price!=='number'||!Number.isFinite(item.price)||item.price<0)add(`menuPreview.${i}.price`,'Enter a price of zero or more.');}
 if(b.isFeatured&&b.featuredStart&&b.featuredEnd&&new Date(b.featuredEnd)<new Date(b.featuredStart))add('featuredEnd','End date must be on or after the start date.');
 check(e);const allowed=['name','cuisine','country','borough','city','address','grades','profile','status','hours','specialHours','timezone','priceLevel','dietary','allergens','amenities','occasions','paymentMethods','accessibility','social','gallery','menuPreview','announcement','isFeatured','featuredStart','featuredEnd'];return Object.fromEntries(allowed.filter(k=>b[k]!==undefined).map(k=>[k,b[k]]));}
